śrīmate rāmānujāya namaḥ | śriyai namaḥ | śrīdharāya namaḥ ||

pūrvācārya surakṣita bahumativyāghātadūrasthitāṃ
ānītāṃ tu nijākṣaraiḥ sumanaso bhaumāḥ pibantvanvaham || 2 ||

yadyapi sambandhasāmānyaparigrahe'pi jijñāsāyāḥ karmāpekṣatvena karmārthatvasiddhiḥ; tathāpi ākṣepataḥ prāptāt ābhidhānikasyaiva grāhyatvāt karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhī gṛhyate |

jñātumicchā 'jijñāsā' | icchāyāḥ iṣyamāṇapradhānatvāt iṣyamāṇaṃ jñānam iha vidhīyate | mīmāṃsāpūrvabhāgajñātasya karmaṇaḥ alpāsthiraphalatvāt, uparitanabhāgāvase- yasya brahmajñānasya anantākṣaya phalatvācca pūrvavṛttāt karmajñānādanantaram tata eva hetoḥ brahma jñātavyam ityuktaṃ bhavati | tadāha vṛttikāraḥ

'vṛttāt karmādhigamādanantaraṃ brahmavividiṣā'
iti | vakṣyati ca karmabrahmamīmāṃsayoḥ aikaśāstryam

'saṃhitametat śārīrakaṃ jaiminīyena ṣoḍaśalakṣaṇena iti śāstraikatvasiddhiḥ' iti | ataḥ pratipipādayiṣitārthabhedena ṣaṭkabhedavat adhyāya bhedavacca pūrvottara - mīmāṃsayoḥ bhedaḥ | mīmāṃsāśāstram -

'athāto dharmajijñāsā'

ityārabhya,

'anāvṛttiḥ śabdādanāvṛttiḥ śabdāt ' ityevamantam saṅgativiśeṣeṇa viśiṣṭakramam | tathā hi 'prathamaṃ tāvat

'svādhyāyosdhyetavyaḥ'

iti adhyayanenaiva svādhyāya śabdavācyavedākhyākṣara rāśeḥ grahaṇaṃ vidhīyate |

'nahayadhuvaiḥ prāpyate '

'lavā te adā yajñarūpāḥ '

evaṃ tarhi śārīrakamīmāṃsāyāmapi na pravartatām; sāṅga' vedādhyayanādeva kṛtsnasya jñātatvāt |

tathaiva dharmavicārospi kartavyaḥ iti paśyatu bhavān !

karmāṇi udgīthādyupāsanāni 'atraiva' cintyante |
na śakyaṃ kartum iti cet-

tat anadhigatakarmaṇa

karma sakalame dadaśananivṛttirūpājñānanivṛtteḥ kathamitra sādhanaṃ bhavet ! śrutayaśca karmaṇāṃ anityaphalatvena mokṣavirodhitvam, jñānasyaiva mokṣasādhanatvañca darśayati--

tadevaṃ janmāntara śatānuṣṭhitānabhisaṃhitaphala viśeṣakarma mṛditakaṣāyasya vividi-

ṣotpattau satyām,

'sadeva somyedamagra āsīdekamevādvitīyam '
' satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma'
' niṣkalaṃ niṣkriyaṃ 'śāntam ' '
ayamātmā brahma' 'tat tvamasi'
ityādivākya janyajñānāt' avidyā nivartate ||

pratiṣṭhāpanam - mananam | etadvirodhyanādibhedavāsanā nirasanāya asyaivārthasya anavarata- bhāvanā - nididhyāsanam ||

'ityuktamiti' | ataḥ karmajñānasya anupayogat uktameva sādhanacatuṣṭayaṃ pūrvavṛttam

iti vaktavyam |

na ca vācyam - bheda vāsanāyāṃ anirastāyāṃ vākyam 'avidyā' nivartakaṃ jñānaṃ na janayati | 'jāte'pi sarvasya sahasaiva bhedajñānānivṛttiḥ na doṣāya | candraikatve jñāte'pi dvicandrajñānānivṛttivat | anivṛttamapi chinnamūlatvena bandhāya na bhavati- iti |
satyāṃ sāmagryāṃ jñānānutpacyanupapatteḥ ; satyāmapi viparītavāsanāyām āpto padeśa - liṅgādibhiḥ bādhakajñānotpattidaśanāt |
satyapi vākyārthajñāne anādivāsanayā mātrayā bhedajñānamanuvartate - iti bhavatā na śakyate vaktuma; bhedajñānasāmagrayā api vāsanāyāḥ mithyārūpatvena jñānotpatyaiva nivṛttatvāt | jñānotpattāvapi mithyārūpāyāḥ tasyāḥ anivṛttau nivartakāntarābhāvāt kadācidapi na asyāḥ vāsanāyāḥ nivṛttiḥ |
vāsanākāryaṃ bhedajñānaṃ chinnamūlam, atha ca anuvartate – iti bāliśabhāṣitam | dvicandrajñānādau tu bādhaka sannidhāvapi mithyājñānahetoḥ paramārthatimirādidoṣasya jñāna - bādhyatvābhāvena " avinaṣṭatvat', mithyā jñānānuvṛtti: aviruddhā | vādhitatvena bhayādikāryaṃ tu nivartate |

prabalapramāṇa-

api ca bhedavāsanānirasanadvāreṇa jñānotpattimabhyugacchatāṃ kadācidapi jñano- bhedavāsanāyāḥ anādikālopacitatvena aparimitatvāt,

tyattiḥ na setsyati |

tadvirodhibhāvanāyāśca alpatvāt anayā ' tannirasanānupapatteḥ ||

prajñāṃ kurvīta' ityevamādibhiḥ, vākyārthajñānasya dhyānopakārakatvāt ' anuvidya' 'vijñāya ' ityanūdya, 'prajñāṃ kurvīta' 'vijānāti' iti 'dhyānaṃ vidhīyate |
'śrotavyaḥ' iti anuvādaḥ; svādhyāyasya arthaparatvena adhītavedaḥ puruṣaḥ prayo- janavadarthāvabodhitvadarśanāt tanirṇayāya svayameva śravaṇe pravartate iti śravaṇasya prāptatvāt | ṇaśrapratiṣṭhārthatvātva mananasya ' mantavyaḥ' iti ca anuvāda: | tasmāt dhyānameva vidhīyate | vakṣyati ca --

'āvṛtti rasakṛdupadeśāt'

iti ||

'yastadveda, yat sa veda, sa mayaitaduktaḥ '

ityatra --

'anu ma etāṃ bhagavo devatāṃ śādhi, yāṃ devatāmupāsse'

iti ||

dhyānaṃ ca tailadhārāvada vicchinna smṛtisantānarūpam |
'dhruvā smṛtiḥ | smṛtilambhe sarvagranthīnāṃ vipramokṣa' iti dhruvāyāḥ smṛteḥ apavargopāyatvaśravaṇāt | sā ca smṛtiḥ darśanasamānākārā | 'bhidyate hṛdayagranthiḥ chidyante sarvasaṃśayāḥ |
kṣīyante cāsya karmāṇi tasmin dṛṣṭe parāvare ||'
ityādinā aikārthyāt | evaṃ ca sati,
'ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ'

ityanena nididhyāsanasya darśanasamānākāratā vidhīyate | bhavati ca smṛteḥ bhāvanā- prakarṣāt 'darśanarūpatā' | vākyakāreṇa etatsarvaṃ prapañcitam -

'vedamupāsanaṃ syāt tadviṣaye śravaṇāt '
dhyānaṃ ceti — avicchinnatvamatra vijātīyapratyayāntarāvyavahitatvam | vicchinna | vicchinna- smṛtisantatisādhāraṇaścet dhyānaśabdaḥ, viśeṣāntare paryavasānaṃ dhyānaṃ ceti | avicchinnasmṛti- santatyasādhāraṇaścet, dhyānaśabda vyākhyānaṃ dhyānaṃ cetyādi |
vipramokṣaḥ - vibhajya ātyantikamocanam |
darśanasamānākārā - na kevalaṃ viṣayaikyataḥ sāmyam, api tu āparokṣyeṇāpi iti

iti sarvāsu upaniṣatsu mokṣasādhanatayā vihitaṃ vedanam upāsanam ityuktam | ' sakṛt pratyayaṃ kuryāt śabdārthasya kṛtatvāt prayājādivat '

iti pūrvapakṣaṃ kṛtvā,
'siddhaṃ tūpāsana śabdāt '
iti, vedanam asakṛdāvṛttaṃ mokṣasādhanam iti nirṇītam |
'upāsanaṃ syāt dhruvānusmṛtiḥ darśanānnirvacanācca '
iti tasyaiva vedanasya upāsanarūpasya asakṛdāvṛttasya dhruvānusmṛtitvam upavarṇitam | seyaṃ smṛtiḥ darśanarūpā pratipāditā | darśanarūpatā ca pratyakṣatāpattiḥ ||

'teṣāṃ satatayuktānāṃ bhajatāṃ prītipūrvakam |
dadāmi buddhiyogaṃ taṃ yena māmupayānti te || '

iti,

'priyo hi jñānino'tyarthamahaṃ sa ca mama priyaḥ | '
ataḥ sākṣātkārarūpā smṛtiḥ smaryamāṇātyarthapriyatvena 'svayamapi atyartha'- priyā yasya sa eva pareṇātmanā varaṇīyo bhavati iti, tenaiva labhyate para ātmā ityuktaṃ bhavati ||
'evaṃrūpā dhruvā'nusmṛtireva bhaktiśabdena abhidhīyate ; upāsanaparyāyatvāt bhaktiśabdasya | ata eva śrutismṛtibhiḥ evamabhidhīyate -

iti ca |

''tameva viditvā'ti' mṛtyumeti'
'tamevaṃ vidvānamṛta iha bhavati | nānyaḥ panthā ayanāya vidyate '
'nāhaṃ vedairna tapasā na dānena na cejyayā |
śakya evaṃ vidho draṣṭuṃ dṛṣṭavānasi māṃ yathā | '
'bhaktyā tvananyayā śakyaḥ ahamevaṃvidho'rjuna |
jñātuṃ draṣṭuṃ ca tattvena praveṣṭuṃ ca parantapa ||'
puruṣaḥ sa paraḥ pārtha bhaktyā labhyastvananyayā |'

iti ||

|| iti laghusiddhāntaḥ ||

- atha mahāpūrvapakṣa:

'atha parā, yathā tadakṣaramadhigamyate, yattadaddeśyamagrāhyamagotramavarṇa macakṣuḥ śrotraṃ tadapāṇipādaṃ nityaṃ vibhuṃ sarvagataṃ susūkṣmaṃ tadavyayaṃ yad bhūtayoniṃ pari- paśyanti dhīrā:'
'satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma'
'niṣkalaṃ niṣkriyaṃ śāntaṃ niravadyaṃ nirañjanam'
'yasyāmataṃ tasya mataṃ mataṃ yasya na veda saḥ |
avijñātaṃ vijānatāṃ vijñātamavijānatām ||'

'na dṛṣṭeṣṭāraṃ paśye..... na matermantāraṃ manvīthāḥ'
'ānando brahma'
'iṃdaṃ sarvaṃ yadayamātmā'
neha nānāssti kiñcana |

'vācārambhaṇaṃ vikāro nāmadheyaṃ mṛttiketyeva satyam' 'yadā hyevaiṣa etasminnudaramantaraṃ kurute, atha tasya bhayaṃ bhavati'
'na sthānato'pi parasyobhayaliṅgaṃ sarvatra hi !' 'māyāmātraṃ tu kātrtsnyenānabhivyaktasvarūpatvāt'

doṣaśca svarūpatirodhāna vividhavicitra vikṣepakarī sadasadanirvacanīyā anādyavidyā |
'anṛtena hi pratyūḍhā: '
'teṣāṃ 'satyānāṃ satā' manṛtamapidhānam'

• mama māyā duratyayā | '

iti | tathā ca vākyakāra:

nanu ca sakalabhedanivṛttiḥ pratyakṣaviruddhā kathamiva 'śāstra' janya' vijñānena ' kriyate ? 'kathaṃ vā' 'rajjureṣā na sarpa:' iti jñānena pratyakṣaviruddhā sarpanivṛttiḥ kriyate ?
tatra dvayoḥ pratyakṣayoḥ virodha: ; iha tu pratyakṣamūlasya śāstrasya pratyakṣasya ca iti cet-
tulyayoḥ virodhe vā kathaṃ bādhyabādhakabhāvaḥ 1

etaduktaṃ

nanu ceti | 'agniḥ anuṣṇaḥ', 'ādityo yūpaḥ' ityādyanumānāgamavat śruteḥ pratyakṣaviruddhārthapratipādanāśaktatvam iti ākṣepāśayaḥ | kriyate – viṣayīkriyate | kathamiva na kathaṃcit | pratyakṣaviruddhaṃ śāstrametra bādhitamukhyārtha bhāktaṃ neyam |

kathaṃ vā iti | pratyakṣaśabdaḥ aparokṣamātraparaḥ | bādhāt pūrvam anābhāsatvābhimānaḥ bādhaśca prapañca rajjusarpabhramayoḥ samaḥ iti bhāvaḥ (ta ṭī) | sarpanivṛttiḥ kriyate sarpābhāvo viṣayīkriyate | pratyakṣatvam abādhyatve na prayojakam |

'na kāraṇam'; jvālābhedānumānena pratyakṣopamardā yogāt | tatra hi jyālaikyaṃ pratyakṣeṇa avagamyate |
' evaṃ ca sati dvayoḥ pramāṇayoḥ virodhe, yat sambhāvyamānānyathā siddhi, tat bādhyam | ananyathāsiddham anavakāśam itarat bādhakam ' iti sarvatra' bādhya - bādhakabhāvanirṇayaḥ - iti ||

nanu anādinidhana / vicchinnasampradāyatayā nirdoṣasyāpi śāstrasya, 'jyotiṣṭomena svargakāmo yajeta'
ityevamādeḥ bhedāvalambinaḥ bādhyatvaṃ prasajyeta !
satyam, pūrvāparāpacchede pūrvaśāstravat mokṣaśāstrasya

vedāntavākyeṣvapi saguṇa 'brahmopāsana' parāṇāṃ

niravakāśatvāt tena bādhyata eva |

'śāstrāṇām ayameva' nyāyaḥ ; nirguṇatvāt parasya brahmaṇaḥ ||
nanvityādi | bhedagrāhakapratyakṣādīnām advaitaśrutivirodhāt bādhyatvamuktam tatrā'ti-
prasaktiḥ iti codyāśayaḥ | api : virodhārthaḥ |
bādhyatvaprayojaka- bhedāvalambitvavyaktyai jyotiṣṭomādyuktiḥ | bādhyatvamiti | bhedavāsanākhyadoṣajanya- tvāt bādhyatvaṃ prasajyetetyarthaḥ | na ca iṣṭāpattiḥ ; apauruṣeyatvena nirdoṣatvasya pratipāditatvāt iti bhāva: |

satyamiti | duṣṭakāraṇajanyatve anaṅgīkāraḥ ityarthaḥ | anyathāsiddhatvānanyathāsiddhatvayoreva hi bādhya bādhakabhāve prayojakatvam | pūrvoktaṃ duṣṭakāraṇajanyatvamapi hi anyathāsiddhatvaprakāraviśeṣaḥ | pūrvāparāpacchede iti | 'paurvāvarye pūrvadaurbalyaṃ prakṛtivat' iti karmamīmāṃsāyāmuktam – pūrvasya utpattikāle virodhyabhāvāt tadabādhenaivotpattiḥ sambhavati, uttarasya tu svotpattikāle virodhisadbhāvāt tadabādhenotpattyanupapattyā pūrvaṃ bādhitvaivotpannamiti ananyathāsiddhatvāt uttarasya prābalyam iti |

bhedāvalambino bādhyatvamaṅgīkṛtam |

pūrvaṃ paramajātatvādabādhitvaiva jāyate |
pūrvābādhena notpattiḥ uttarasya hi sidhyati || ' iti |

| mokṣaśāstrasya – bandharūpabhedanivarta- kataśvāvedakavākyasya | niravakāśatvāt – pūrvābādhenotpattumanavakāśāt | nirguṇa- śāstrasya apacchedanyāyena balavattvāt, upāsanaviṣayatayā saguṇaśāstrasya sāvakāśatvācca saguṇa- śāstrasya bādhyatvam iti bhāvaḥ ||

nanu ca

'ya: sarvajñaḥ sarvavit'
'parāssya śaktirvividhaiva śrūyate svābhāvikī jñānabalakriyā ca | '

'satyakāmaḥ satyasaṅkalpaḥ
ityādibrahmasvarūpapratipādanaparāṇāṃ śāstrāṇām kathaṃ bādhyatvam 1
nirguṇavākyasāmarthyāt iti brūmaḥ |

etaduktaṃ bhavati -

'asthūlamanaṇu ' ahrasvamedīrgham " ' satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma'
" nirguṇaḥ "
'nirañjanam'

ityādivākyāni – nirastasamastaviśeṣa kūṭa sthanityacaitanyaṃ brahma - iti pratipādayanti | itarāṇi ca saguṇam | ubhaya vidhavākyānāṃ virodhe, tenaiva apacchedanyāyena nirguṇa- vākyānāṃ 'guṇāpekṣatvena' paratvāt balīyastvam, 'iti na kiñcit apahīnam ||

netyucyate ; sāmānādhikaraṇyena ekārthatvapratīteḥ |
anekaguṇaviśiṣṭābhidhāne'pi ekārthatvam aviruddham iti cet,

anabhidhānajño devānāṃpriyaḥ | ekārthatvaṃ nāma sarvapadānām arthaikyam | viśiṣṭapadārthābhidhāne viśeṣaṇabhedena padānām arthabhedaḥ avarjanīyaḥ | na sidhyati |

|

tataśva ekārthatvaṃ

ekasyavārthasya tattatpadārthavirodhipratyanīkaparatvena sarvapadānām arthavattvam, ekārthatvam, aparyāyatā ca ||

evam ekasyaiva vastunaḥ sakaletaravirodhyākāratām avagamayat arthavattaram, ekārtham, 'aparyāyaṃ ca' padatrayam - iti ||
tasmāt - ekameva brahma svayaṃjyotiḥ nirdhṛtanikhila viśeṣam - ityuktaṃ bhavati | evaṃ vākyārthapratipādane satyeva-
'sadeva somyedamagra āsīdekamevādvitīyam'

ityādibhiḥ aikārthyam ||

iti | tatra sarvaśākhā pratyayanyāyena kāraṇavākyeṣu sarveṣu sajātīya- vijātīyavyāvṛttam advitīyaṃ brahma avagatam | jagatkāraṇatayā upalakṣitasya brahmaṇaḥ advitīyasya pratipipādayiṣitaṃ svarūpaṃ tadavirodhena vaktavyam |

advitīyatvaśrutiḥ

guṇato'pi sadvitīyatāṃ na sahate, anyathā 'nirañjanam' 'nirguṇaḥ ' ' ityādibhiśca virodhaḥ' | ataśca etat lakṣaṇavākyam akhaṇḍaikarasa meva' pratipādayati ||

nanu ca sarvapadānāṃ lakṣaṇā na dṛṣṭacarī !

dhāya sarvapadānāṃ lakṣaṇā'pi na doṣaḥ | ataḥ idameva arthajātaṃ pratipādayantaḥ vedāntāḥ pramāṇam ||

' ghaṭaḥ asti' ityatra astitvam, tadbhedaśca vyavahriyate | na ca dvayorapi vyava- hārayoḥ pratyakṣabhūlatvaṃ sambhavati ; tayoḥ bhinnakālajñānaphalatvāt, pratyakṣa 'jñānasya ca ' ekakṣaṇavartitvāt |

tatra svarūpaṃ vā, bhedo vā pratyakṣasya viṣayaḥ ? iti vivecanīyam |
bhedagrahaṇasya svarūpagrahaṇa- tatpratiyogismaraṇa savyapekṣatvādeva, svarūpaviṣayatvam avaśyāśrayaṇīyam iti na bhedaḥ pratyakṣeṇa gṛhyate | | ataḥ bhrāntimūla eva bheda-

vyavahāraḥ ||

'svarūpa' mātra bhedavādino hi 'pratiyogyapekṣā' ca na utprekṣituṃ kṣamā ; svarūpa- bhedayoḥ svarūpatvāviśeṣāt | yathā svarūpavyavahāraḥ na pratiyogyapekṣaḥ, bhedavyava- hāro'pi tathaiva syāt | 'hastaḥ karaḥ' itivat 'ghaṭaḥ bhinnaḥ' iti ca paryāyasvaṃ

syāt |

nāpi dharma' ;
'svarūpameva ' syāt |

dharmatve sati tasya svarūpāt bhedaḥ avaśyāśrayaṇīyaḥ | anyathā bhede ca tasyāpi bhedaḥ taddharmaḥ, tasyāpi iti anavasthā |

sarve padārthāḥ sattāsnubhūtighaṭitā eva dṛśyante | tram anuvartamānaṃ dṛśyate iti tadeva paramārthaḥ ||

'atra' sarvāsu 'pratipattiṣu sanmā-

kim 'ghaṭaḥ asti' ityatra 'paṭādyabhāvaḥ ' 9

siddhaṃ tarhi 'ghaṭaḥ asti' ityanena paṭādīnāṃ bādhitatvam | ataḥ bādhyaphala- bhūtā viṣayanivṛttiḥ vyāvṛttiḥ | sā vyāvartamānānām apāramārthyaṃ sādhayati | rajjuvat sanmātram abādhitam anuvartate | tasmāt sanmātrātireki sarvam 'aparamārthaḥ ' | prayogaśca bhavati —
sat paramārthaḥ, anuvartamānatvāt, rajjusarpādau 'rajjyādivat | ghaṭādayaḥ aparamārthāḥ, vyāvartamānatvāt, rajjvādyadhiṣṭhāna sarpādivat iti ||
evaṃ sati anuvartamānā anubhūtireva 'paramārthaḥ ' | saiva satī | nanu ca sanmātram 'anubhūteḥ' viṣayatayā tato bhinnam |
kiṃ ... paṭādyabhāvaḥ ? paṭābhāvavyatirekeṇa paṭavyāvṛtteḥ vaktumaśakyatvāt abhāva eva vyāvṛttiḥ ityāśrīyate cet ityarthaḥ |
siddhaṃ tarhi ityādi - yathā 'na rajatam' iti niṣedhābhāve'pi 'śuktiḥ' ityanena rajata- bādhaḥ tathā [ 'na paṭaḥ' iti niṣedhābhāve'pi 'ghaṭaḥ asti' ityanena paṭādīnāṃ bādhitattvam | bādhaphalabhūtā - bādho nāma aduṣṭakāraṇajā niṣedhabuddhiḥ, 'paṭo nāsti' ityādi- rūpā, tādṛśadhīsthāpyā | viṣaya nivṛttiḥ - paṭā didhīvedyānām abhāvaḥ | vyāvṛttiḥ- kiṃrūpā iti vikalpitā vyāvṛttiḥ | sanmātramanuvartate – sanmātrasyāpi mādhya- mikayuktatyā bādhoktiḥ niradhiṣṭhānabhramāsambhavāt ayuktā II
evaṃ sati hetvo:

anubhūtireva - | - evakāraḥ anubhāvyamidhyātvaṃ sūcayati | saiva satī - anubhūtireva satśabdārthaḥ |

naivam ; bhedo hi pratyakṣāviṣayatvāt, durnirūpatvācca purastādeva nirastaḥ | ata eva sataḥ anubhūtiviṣayabhāvo'pi na pramāṇapadavīm anumarati | tasmāt sat anubhūtireva | sāca svatassiddhā; anubhūtitvāt | anyatassiddhau vaṭādivat 'ananubhūtitva' prasaṅgaḥ' ||

bhūyate' iti | na hi kaścit 'ghaṭaḥ ayam' payabhūtām anidabhāvām anubhūtimapi anubhavati | cakṣurādikaraṇasaṃnikarṣavat anubhūteḥ sadbhāva eva hetuḥ | citkaprakāśātiśayaliṅgena anubhūti: anumīyate ||

iti jānan tadānīmeva avi- tasmāt ghaṭādiprakāśa niṣpattau,

tadanaḥ taram arthagatakādā-

'evaṃ' tarhi anubhūteḥ ajaḍāyāḥ arthavat jaḍatvam 'āpadyate iti cet- kimidam ajaḍatvaṃ nāma ?

na tāvat svasattāyāḥ prakāśāvyabhicāraḥ; sukhādiṣvapi tatsaṃbhavāt | na hi kadācidapi sukhādayaḥ santaḥ nopalabhyante | 'ataḥ anubhūtiḥ svayameva na anu- bhūyate ; arthāntaraṃ spṛśataḥ aṅgulyagrasya svātmasparśavat aśakyatvāt iti ||

viṣayadharmasya prakāśasya rūpādivat anupalabdhe', ubhayābhyupetānubhūtyaiva aśeṣavyava- hāropapattau prakāśākhyadharmakalpanānupapatteśca | ataḥ na anubhūti: anumīyate, nāpi jñānāntarasiddhā | api tu sarva sādhayantī anubhūtiḥ svayameva sidhyati | prayogaśca-
anubhūti: ananyādhīnasvadharmavyavahārā svasambandhāt arthāntare taddharmavyavahāra- hetutvāt | yaḥ svamambandhāt arthāntare 'yaddharmavyavahārahetuḥ saḥ tayoḥ svasmin ananyādhīnaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ | yathā rūpādiḥ cākṣuṣatvādau |
rūpādihiṃ pṛthivyādau svasambandhāt cākṣuṣatvādi janayan svasmin na rūpādi- sambandhādhīnaḥ cākṣuṣatvādau | ataḥ anubhūtiḥ ātmanaḥ prakāśamānatve, 'prakāśate '

iti vyavahāre ca svayameva hetuḥ ||

svataddhitvādeva | na hi anubhūteḥ svatassiddhāyāḥ prāgabhāvaḥ svataḥ anyato vā avagantuṃ śakyate |

'anubhūtiḥ' svābhāvam avagamayantī, satī tāvat na avagamayati tasyāḥ sattve virodhādeva tadabhāvaḥ nāstīti kathaṃ sā svābhāvam avagamayati 1
evam asatyapi na avagamayati ; anubhūtiḥ svayam asatī svābhāve kathaṃ pramāṇaṃ bhavet ?
nāpi anyataḥ atragantuṃ śakyate anubhūte: ananyagovaratvāt | ; | asya: prāgabhāvaṃ sādhayat pramāṇam 'anubhūti: iyam' 'iti' viṣayīkṛtya tadabhāvaṃ sādhayet | svatassiddhatvena ' iyam' iti 'iyam' iti viṣayī kārānarhatyāt, na tatprāgabhāvaḥ anyataḥ

i

śakyāvagamaḥ ||

anutpannā iyam anubhūtiḥ ātmani nānātvamapi na sahate; vyāpaka viruddho- palabdheḥ | na hi anutpannam nānābhūtaṃ dṛṣṭam | bhedādīnām anu 'bhāvyatvena' ca rūpa/dekhi anubhūtidharmatvaṃ na saṃbhavati | 'ataḥ anubhūteḥ' anubhavasvarūpatvādeva anyo'pi kaścit anubhāvyaḥ na asyāḥ dharmaḥ ||

ajaḍatvācca | anātmatvavyāptaṃ jaḍatvam saṃvidi vyāvartamānam 'anātmatvamapi hi ' saṃvidaḥ vyāvartayati ||

gocaratvam | kartṛtve ahaṃpratyayagocaratve ca ātmanaḥ abhyupagamyamāne, dehasyeva jaḍatva-parāktvānātnatvādiprasaṅgaḥ duṣpariharaḥ |
ahampratyagocarāt kartṛ tathā prasiddhāt dehāt tatkriyāphalasvargāderbhoktuḥ ātmanaḥ anyatvaṃ prāmāṇikānāṃ prasiddhameva | tathā ahamarthāt jñāturapi vilakṣaṇaḥ sākṣī pratya- gātmā iti pratipattavyam ||

iti mā vocaḥ ; ravikara nikara bhivyaṅgyakaratalasya tadabhivyañjakatvadarśanāt | jālakarandhaniṣkrāntadhumaṇikiraṇānāṃ tadabhivyaṅgayenāpi karatalena sphuṭataraprakāśo hi

dṛṣṭacaraḥ ||

mahāsiddhāntaḥ

tathā hi-nirviśeṣavastuvādibhiḥ 'nirviśeṣe vastuni iṃdaṃ pramāṇam' iti na śakyate vaktum ; saviśeṣavastu viṣayatvāt sarvapramāṇānām | yastu svānubhava' siddham' iti svagoṣṭhīniṣṭhaḥ samayaḥ - so'pi ātmasākṣikasa viśeṣānubhavādeva nirastaḥ; 'idam aham adarśam' iti kenacit viśeṣeṇa viśiṣṭaviṣayatvāt sarveṣāmanubhavānām ||

dviśeṣairviśiṣṭa' syaiva vastunaḥ anye viśeṣāḥ nirasyante iti na kvacit nirviśeṣa- vastusiddhiḥ ||

nirvikalpakaṃ nāma kenacidviśeṣeṇa viyuktasya grahaṇam | na sarvaviśeṣarahitasya ; tathābhūtasya kadācidapi grahaṇādarśanāt, anupapatteśca |
kenacidviśeṣeṇa ' idam ittham' iti hi sarvā pratītiḥ upajāyate; trikoṇa - sāsnādisaṃsthāna' viśeṣeṇa vinā kasyacidapi padārthasya grahaṇāyogāt | ataḥ nirvikalpakam ekajātīyadravyeṣu prathamapiṇḍagrahaṇam ; dvitīyādipiṇḍagrahaṇaṃ savikalpakam ityucyate ||

saṃsthānarūpajātyāderapi aindriyakatvāviśeṣāt saṃsthānena vinā saṃsthāninaḥ pratītyanupa- patteśca prathamapiṇḍagrahaṇe'pi sasaṃsthānameva vastu ' ittham ' iti gṛhyate |
ataḥ dvitīyādipiṇḍagrahaṇeṣu gotvādeḥ anuvṛttidharmaviśiṣṭatā saṃsthānivat saṃsthānavacca sarvadeva gṛhyate iti teṣu savikalpatvameva |

ataḥ pratyakṣasya kadācidapi na nirviśeṣaviṣayatvam |

vyāvṛttiśca gotvādisaṃsthāna viśeṣa viśiṣṭatayā 'ittham' iti pratīteḥ | sarvatra viśeṣaṇa- viśeṣyabhāvapratipattau tayoḥ atyantabhedaḥ pratītyava suvyaktaḥ |
tatra daṇḍa- kuṇḍalādayaḥ pṛthaksaṃsthānasaṃsthitāḥ svaniṣṭhā kadācit kvacit dravyāntaraviśeṣaṇatayā avatiṣṭhante | gotvādayastu dravyasaṃsthāna tayaiva padārthabhūtāḥ santaḥ dravyaviśeṣaṇatayā avasthitāḥ |

ubhayatra viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvaḥ samānaḥ | tata
iyāṃstu viśeṣaḥ - pṛthaksthiti 'pratipatti' yogyāḥ

eva tayoḥ bheda pratipattiśca' |

gotvādayastu niyamena tadanarhāḥ - iti |

daṇḍādayaḥ |

|

ataḥ - vastuvirodhaḥ pratītiparāhataḥ - iti pratītiprakāranihnavādeva ucyate | pratīti prakāro hi 'idam ittham' ityeva sarvasaṃmataḥ | tadetat sūtrakāreṇa - 'naikasminnasambhavāt '

iti suvyaktamupapāditam ||

anumānamapi saviśeṣaviṣayameva |

pramāṇasaṃkhyāvivāde'pi sarvābhyupagatapramāṇānām
ayameva viṣayaḥ iti, na kenā'pi pramāṇena nirviśeṣavastusiddhiḥ |
vastugata svabhāvaviśeṣaiḥ tadeva vastu 'nirviśeṣam' iti vadan jananīvandhyātva- pratijñāyāmiva svavāgvirodhamapi na jānāti ||

ata eva ca' na anavasthā, anyonyāśrayagaṃ ca | ekakṣaṇavartitve'pi pratyakṣa- jñānasya tasminneva kṣaṇe vastubhedarūpatatsaṃsthānarūpagotvādeḥ gṛhītatvāt, gṛhītatvāt, kṣaṇāntara- grāhyaṃ na kiñcidiha tiṣṭhati ||

|

kiṃ ca ave hastini ca saṃvedanayoḥ ekaviṣayatvena uparitanasya gṛhītagrāhitvāt, viśeṣābhāvācca smṛtivailakṣaṇyaṃ na syāt |
pratisaṃvedanaṃ viśeṣābhyupagame pratyakṣasya viśiṣṭārthaviṣayatvameva abhyupagataṃ bhavati | sarveṣāṃ saṃvedanānāmekaviṣayatāyām ekenaiva saṃvedanena aśeṣagrahaṇāt andhabadhirādyabhāvaśca prasajyeta ||

anuvādakatvameva syāt samāhmaṇaḥ prameyabhāvaśca | tataḥ jaḍatvanāśitvādayaḥ tvayaivoktāḥ | ataḥ vastusaṃsthānarūpajātyādilakṣaṇabhedaviśiṣṭaviṣayameva pratyakṣam ||

nanu ca jātyādiretra bhedaścet tasmin gṛhīte tadvyavahāravat bhedavyavahāro'pi

syāt' !

satyam, bheda vyavahiyata eva gotvādivyavahārāt | gotvādireva hi sakaletaravyāvṛttiḥ ; gotvādau gṛhīte sakaletarasajātīyabuddhivyavahārayoḥ nivṛtteḥ | bhedagrahaṇenaiva hi abhedanivṛttiḥ |
'ayam asmāt bhinnaḥ' iti tu vyavahāre pratiyoginirdeśasya tadapekṣatvāt', pratiyogyapekṣayā 'bhinnaḥ' iti vyavahāraḥ ityuktam ||

atra ghaṭapaṭādiṣu deśakālabhedena virodha eva nāsti | kāle yasya sadbhāvaḥ pratipannaḥ, tasmin deśe tasmin kāle tāya tatra virodhāt balavato bādhakatvam bādhitasya ca nivṛttiḥ |

yasmin deśe yasmin abhāvaḥ pratipannaścet-

deśāntarakālāntarasambandhitayā anubhūtasya anyadeśakālayoḥ abhāva' pratītau' na virodhaḥ iti, katham atra bādhya bādhakabhāvaḥ 1 anyatra nivṛttasya anyatra nivṛttirvā kathamucyate ?
rajjusarpādiṣu tu taddeśakālasambandhitathaiva śrabhāvapratīteḥ, virodhaḥ bādhakatvaṃ vyāvṛttiśca iti, 'deśāntarakālāntara vyāvartamānatvam mithyātvavyāptaṃ na dṛṣṭamiti na vyāvartamānatvamātram apāra' mārthya hetuḥ |

yattu -- anuvartamānatvāt sat paramārthaḥ

iti,

tat siddhameveti na sādhana marhati | ataḥ na sanmātrameva vastu ||
tatra virodhāditi | bhāvābhāvavirodhāt anyatarasya bādhyatve sati balavato bādhakatvam, itarasya bādhitatvāt nivṛttiḥ | caḥ evārthe | nivṛttiḥ tvadabhimatavyāvṛttiviśeṣaḥ | – |

niṣedhadhīḥ |

virodhaḥ - sāmānādhikaraṇyānatvam |

- aduṣṭakāraṇajanyā

bādhaḥ-

bādhakatvam – adu:kāraṇajanyatve sati niṣedharūpatvam |

nivṛttiḥ - purovartini abhāva:,
anyatra nivṛttasya — virodhabādhasthale

anavabhāso vā satyatvāvabhāsābhāvo vā |

anubhūti - 'sadviśeṣayośca viṣayaviṣayibhāvena bhedasya pratyakṣa siddhatvāt, abā- dhitatvācca anubhūtireva satī - ityetadapi nirastam ||

śabdavyavahārocchedaprasaṅgaḥ | ācāryasya jñānavaccam anumāya tadupasattiśca kriyate |

sāca nopapadyate ||

anubhūteḥ anubhāvyatve ajñānāvirodhitvamapi tasyāḥ ghaṭādekhi prasajyate iti cet —
ananubhāvyatve'pi gaganakusumādekhi ajñānāvirodhitvamapi prasajyata eva | ataḥ - anubhāvyatve ananubhūtitvam iti upahāsyam ||

atha manyase - anubhūtiprāgabhāvādeḥ siddhayataḥ tatsamakālabhāvaniyamaḥ asti-

iti,

kiṃ tvayā kacit evaṃ dṛṣṭam ! yena niyamaṃ bravīṣi | hanta ! tarhi tata eva darśanāt prāgabhāvādiḥ siddhaḥ iti na 'tadapahnavaḥ' | tatprāgabhāvaṃ ca tatsamakāla- bartinam anunmattaḥ ko bravīti ?
indriyajanmanaḥ pratyakṣasya hi eṣa svabhāvaniyamaḥ ; yat svasamakālavartinaḥ padārthasya grāhakatvam | na 'sarveṣāṃ jñānānāṃ pramāṇānāṃ ca smaraṇānumānāgamayogi- pratyakṣādiṣu kālāntaravartino'pi grahaṇadarśanāt ||

ataḥ idamapi nirastam – smṛtiḥ na bāhya viṣayāḥ naṣṭe'pyarthe smṛtidarśanāt-

iti |

viṣayendriyādidoṣajanyaṃ rajjvādau sarpādivijñānam satyameva bhayādihetuḥ |
satyaiva adaṣṭe'pi svātmani sarpasannidhānāt daṣṭabuddhiḥ |
satyaiva śaṅkāviṣabuddhiḥ maraṇahetubhūtā |

vastubhūte eva jalādau mukhādipratibhāsaḥ vastubhūtamukhagataviśeṣaniścayahetuḥ | eṣa saṃvedanānām utpattimatvāt, arthakriyākāritvācca satyatvam avasīyate ||

nanu – varṇātmanā pratipannā rekhā varṇabuddhihetuḥ varṇātmatā tu asatyā,

'naivam'; 'varṇātmatāyāḥ asatyāyāḥ " upāyatvāyogāt |

asata: nirupā-

khyasya hi upāyatvaṃ na dṛṣṭam, anupapannaṃ ca |

atha tasyāṃ varṇabuddheḥ upāyatvam-

evaṃ tarhi asatyāt satyabuddhiḥ na syāt ; buddheḥ satyatvādeva | peyayoḥ 'ekatva' prasaṅgaśva; ubhayoḥ varṇabuddhitvāviśeṣāt |

| upāyo-

rekhāyāḥ avidyamānavarṇātmanā upāyatve ca - ekasyāmeva rekhāyām avidya- mānasarvavarṇātmakatvasya sulabhatvāt, eka rekhādarśanāt sarvavarṇapratipattiḥ syāt |

atha – piṇḍaviśeṣe 'devadattādi śabda saṅketavat, cakṣurgrāhyarekhāviśeṣe śrotragrāhya- varṇaviśeṣasaṅketavaśāt rekhāviśeṣaḥ varṇaviśeṣabuddhihetuḥ – iti,

na ca - ' ekarūpasya' śabdasya nādaviśeṣeṇa 'arthabheda' buddhihetutve'pi - asa- tyāt satyapratipattiḥ ; nānānādābhivyaktasya ekasyaiva śabdasya tattannādābhivyaṅgaya- svarūpeṇa arthaviśeṣaiḥ saha sambandhagrahaṇavaśāt arthabhedabuddhayutpatti' hetutvāt |
śabdasya ekarūpatvamapi na sādhīya: ; gakārādeḥ bodhakasyaiva śrotragrāhyatvena śabdatvāt | ataḥ asatyāt śāstrāt satyabrahmaviṣayapratipattiḥ durupapādā ||

naivam ; asati śāstre 'asti śāstram' iti buddhe: mithyātvāt |

tataḥ kim ?

idaṃ tataḥ | mithyābhūtaśāstrajanyajñānasya mithyātvena tadviṣayasyāpi brahmaṇaḥ mithyātvam ; yathā dhūmabuddhayā gṛhītavāṣpajanyāgnijñānasya mithyātvena tadviṣayasya agnerapi mithyātvam | paścāttanabādhādarśanaśca asiddham ; 'śūnyameva tattvam' iti 'vākye ' tasyāpi bādhadarśanāt |

tattu bhrāntimūlam - iti cet,

etadapi bhrāntimūlam iti tvayaivoktam | "pāśrāsya bādhādarśanaṃ tu tasyaiva iti, alam apratiṣṭhitakutarka parihasanena ||

'sadeva somyedamagra āsīt '

ityevamādīni - iti,

tadayuktam ; ekavijñānena sarvavijñānapratijñopapādanamukhena sacchabdavācyasya parasya brahmaṇaḥ 'jagadupādānatvam, nagannimittatvam, sarvajñatā, sarvaśaktiyogaḥ, satyasaṅkalpatvam, 'sarvāntaratvam', 'sarvādhāratvam, sarvaniyamanam ityādyaneka- kalyāṇaguṇaviśiṣṭatām, kṛtsnasya jagataḥ tadātmakartā ca pratipādya', 'evambhūta- brahmātmakaḥ tvamasi' iti, śvetaketuṃ prati upadeśāya pravṛttatvāt prakaraṇasya | paścitaca ayamarthaḥ vedārthasaṅgrahe | atrāpi ārambhaṇādhikaraṇe nipuṇataram 'upapādayiṣyate ||

'satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma'

ityatrā'pi sāmānādhikaraṇyasya aneka viśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭaikārthābhidhānavyutpattyā na nirviśeṣa- vastusiddhiḥ | pravṛttinimittabhedena 'ekārthavṛttitvaṃ hi sāmānādhikaraṇyam | tatra satyajñānādipada mukhyārthaiḥ guṇaiḥ, tattadguṇavirodhyākārapratyanīkākārairvā ekasminnevārthe padānāṃ pravṛttau nimittabhedaḥ avaśyāśrayaṇīyaḥ |
iyāṃstu viśeṣaḥ ekasmin pakṣe padānāṃ mukhyārthatā, aparasmiṃśca teṣāṃ lakṣaṇā |
na ca ajñānādīnāṃ pratyanīkatā vastusvarūpameva ; ekenaiva padena svarūpaṃ prati- pannamiti, padāntaraprayogavaiyarthyāt | tathā sati sāmānādhikaraṇyāsiddhiśca ; eka- smin vastuni vartamānānāṃ padānāṃ nimittabhedānāśrayaṇāt |
na ca ekasyaivārthasya viśeṣaṇabhedena viśiṣṭatābhedāt anekārthatvam, padānāṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyavirodhi; ekasyaiva vastunaḥ aneka viśeṣaṇa viśiṣṭatāpratipādanaparatvāt sāmānādhikaraṇyasya |
'bhinnapravṛttinimittānāṃ śabdānāmekasminnarthe vṛttiḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyam' " iti hi śābdikāḥ ||

yaduktam — 'ekamevādvitīyam' ityatra advitīyapadam guṇato'pi sadvitīyatāṃ na sahate | ataḥ sarvaśākhāpratyayanyāyena kāraṇavākyānām advitīyavastupratipādanaparatvam 'abhyupagamanīyam' | kāraṇatayā upalakṣitasya advitīyasya brahmaṇaḥ lakṣaṇamidamucyate, 'satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma' iti | ataḥ lilakṣayiṣitaṃ brahma nirguṇameva | anyathā 'nirguṇa:' 'nirañjanam' ityādibhiḥ virodhaśca - iti,
tat anupapannam ; jagadupādānasya brahmaṇaḥ svavyatiriktādhiṣṭhātrantara nivāraṇena vicitraśaktiyogapratipādanaparatvāt advitīyapadasya | tathaiva vicitraśaktiyogameva

avagamayati,

' tadaikṣata bahu syāṃ prajñāyeyeti, tarājo'sṛjata'

ityādi |

aviśeṣeṇa 'advitīyam' ityukte nimittāntaramātra niṣedhaḥ kathaṃ jñāyate 1 iti cet,

sisṛkṣoḥ brahmaṇaḥ upādānakāraṇatvam 'sadeva somyedamagra āsīdekameva ' iti pratipāditam | kāryotpattisvābhāvyena buddhisthaṃ nimittāntaram iti, tadeva advitīya- padena niṣidhyate ityavagamyate | sarvaniṣedhe hi svābhyupagatāḥ siṣādhayiṣitā nityatvādayaśca niṣiddhāḥ syuḥ ||

'yaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvavit' 'tadaikṣata'
'seyaṃ devataikṣata'
' sa īkṣata lokānnu sṛjā iti'
'nityo nityānāṃ cetanacetanānāmeko bahūnāṃ yo vidadhāti kāmān'

'jñājñau dvāvajāvīśanīśau'
'tamīśvarāṇāṃ paramaṃ maheśvaraṃ taṃ devatānāṃ paramaṃ ca daivatam |

patiṃ patīnāṃ paramaṃ parastādvidāma devaṃ bhuvaneśa mīḍyam || 'na tasya kārya karaṇaṃ ca vidyate na tatsamaścābhyadhikaśca dṛśyate | parā'sya śaktirvividhaiva śrūyate svābhāvikī jñānabalakriyā ca ||'
'eṣa ātmā'pahatapāpmā vijaro vimṛtyurviśoko vijighatso'pipāsaḥ satya- kāmaḥ satyasaṅkalpaḥ'
ityādyāḥ śrutayaḥ jñātṛtvapramukhān kalyāṇaguṇān jñānasvarūpasyaiva brahmaṇaḥ svābhāvikān vadanti, samasta heyarahitatāṃ ca ||

nirguṇavākyānāṃ, saguṇavākyānāṃ ca viṣayam – 'apahatapāpmā' ityādi 'apipāsaḥ' ityantena guṇān pratipivya, 'satyakāmaḥ satyasaṅkalpaḥ' iti śrahmaṇaḥ kalyāṇaguṇān vidadhatī iyaṃ śrutireva vivinaki iti, saguṇa-nirguṇavākyayoḥ virodhābhāvāda, anyatarasya mithyāviṣayatāśrayaṇamapi na 'āṅkatīyam ' ||

'brahma veda brahmaiva bhavati' iti, jñānāt mokṣopadeśaḥ na syāt |
'asanneva sa bhavati | asad brahmeti veda cet | asti brahmeti cedveda | santamenaṃ tato viduḥ
iti, brahmaviṣayajñānāsadbhāvasadbhāvābhyām ātmanāśam ātmasattāṃ ca vadati | 'brahmaviṣayavedanameva' 'apavargopāyam sarvāḥ śrutayaḥ vidadhati ||

ataḥ

iti tatva - vijñātatvavacanaṃ tatraiva 'viruddhayate' ||

tat - 'jñānāśrayasya brahmaṇa: jñānaṃ svarūpam' iti vadatīti parihṛtam | jñānameva hi anukūlam ānanda ityucyate |

|

'vijñānamānandaṃ brahma'
iti - ānanda' rūpameva jñānaṃ brahma ityarthaḥ |

ata eva bhavatām ekarasatā | asya

'jñāna' svarūpasyaiva jñātṛtvamapi śrutiśatasamadhigatam ityuktam |

tadvadeva,

' sa eko brahmaṇa ānanda : '
'ānandaṃ brahmaṇo vidvān '
iti vyatirekanirdeśācca na ānandamātraṃ brahma, api tu ānandi | ānanditvam ||

jñātṛtvameva hi

yadidamuktam-

'yatra hi dvaitamiva bhavati'

neha nānāsti kiñcana |

mṛtyoḥ sa mṛtyumāpnoti ya iha nāneva paśyati ||'

'yatra 'tvasya' sarvamātmaivābhūt... tat kena kaṃ paśyet ?' iti bhedaniṣedhaḥ bahudhā dṛśyate iti,

tat — kṛtsnasya jagataḥ brahmakāryatayā tadantaryāmikatayā ca tadātmakatvena aikyāt tatpratyanīka nānātvaṃ pratiṣidhyate, na punaḥ

'bahu syāṃ prajāyeya'

iti bahubhava saṅkalpapūrvakaṃ brahmaṇo nānātvaṃ śrutisiddhaṃ pratiṣidhyate iti parihṛtam nānātvaniṣedhāt iyam aparamārthaviṣayā -- iti cet,
na; pratyakṣādisakalapramāṇānavagataṃ nānātvaṃ durārohaṃ brahmaṇa: pratipādya, tadeva bādhyate iti upahāsyamidam |

'yadā hyeṣa etasminnudaramantaraṃ kurute |

atha tasya bhayaṃ bhavati '

iti brahmaṇi nānātvaṃ paśyataḥ bhayaprāptiḥ --- iti yaduktam,

tadasat

'sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahma tajjalāniti śānta upāsīta'
iti, tannānātvānusandhānakha śāntihetutvopadeśāt | tathā hi sarva jagataḥ tadutpatti- sthiti- layakarmatayā tadātmakatvānu-sandhānena atra śāntiḥ vidhīyate | ataḥ 'yathāva- sthitadeva-tirthaṅ-manuṣya-sthāvarādibhedabhinnaṃ sthitadeva- tirthaṅ- manuṣya- sthāvarādibhedabhinnaṃ 'jagat' brahmātmakam' iti anusandhānasya śāntihetutayā abhayaprāptihetutvena na bhayahetutvaprasaṅgaḥ |

evaṃ tarhi,

'atha tasya bhayaṃ bhavati'

iti kimucyate ?

idamucyate ;

tasminnadṛśye'nātmye'nirukte'nilayane'bhayaṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ vindate | atha
so'bhayaṃ gato bhavati'

' yadā

iti abhayaprāptihetutvena brahmaṇi yā pratiṣṭhā abhihitā, tasyāḥ vicchede bhayaṃ bhavatīti |

yathoktaṃ maharṣibhiḥ-

'yanmuhūrta kṣaṇaṃ vā'pi vāsudevo na cintyate |
sāhānistanmahacchidraṃ sā bhrāntiḥ sā ca vikriyā ||'

ityādi | brahmaṇi pratiṣṭhāyāḥ antaram – avakāśaḥ - viccheda eva ||

tadasat ;

'yo māmajamanādiṃ ca vetti lokamaheśvaram |'
'matsthāni sarvabhūtāni na cāhaṃ teṣvavasthitaḥ | ' 'na ca matsthāni bhūtāni paśya meṃ yogamaiśvaram | bhūtabhṛnna ca bhūtastho mamātmā bhūtabhāvanaḥ ||'
'ahaṃ kṛtsnasya jagataḥ prabhavaḥ pralayastathā | mattaḥ parataraṃ nānyat kiñcidasti dhanañjaya |
mayi sarvamidaṃ protaṃ sūtre maṇigaṇā iva ||'
' viṣṭabhyāhamidaṃ kṛtsnamekāṃśena sthito jagat |'
'uttamaḥ puruṣastvanyaḥ paramātmetyudāhṛtaḥ |
yo lokatrayamāviśya bibhartyavyaya īśvaraḥ ||
yasmāt kṣaramatīto'hamakṣarādapi cottamaḥ |
ato'smi loke vede ca prathitaḥ puruṣottamaḥ ||'
'sa sarvabhūtaprakṛtiṃ vikārān guṇādidoṣāṃśca mune vyatītaḥ |
atīta sarvāvaraṇo'khilātmā tenāstṛtaṃ yad bhuvanāntarāle |

samasta kalyāṇaguṇātmako'sau svaśakti' leśādghṛtabhūtasargaḥ | icchāgṛhītābhimato rudehaḥ saṃsādhitāśeṣajagaddhito'sau ||
tejobalaiśvaryamahāvodhasuvīrya śaktayādiguṇaikarāśiḥ |
paraḥ parāṇāṃ sakalā na yatra kleśādayaḥ santi parāvareśe ||

vasanti 'tatra' bhūtāni bhūtātmanyakhilātmani | sa ca bhūteṣvaśeṣeṣu vakārārthastato'vyayaḥ || 'jñānaśaktibalaiśvarya vīrya tejāṃsyaśeṣataḥ |
bhagavacchabdavācyāni vinā heyairguṇādibhiḥ ||'
'evameṣa mahāśabdo maitreya bhagavāniti |
paramabrahmabhūtasya vāsudevasya nānyagaḥ || tatra pūjyapadārthoktiparibhāṣāsamanvitaḥ |
śabdo'yaṃ nopacāreṇa hyanyatra chupacārata: ||'
'samastāḥ śaktayacaitā nṛpa yatra pratiṣṭhitāḥ | tadviśvarūpavairūpyaṃ rūpamanyaddharermahat ||
samastaśaktirūpa ṇi tatkaroti janeśvara |
devatiryaṅ - manuṣyākhyaceṣṭāvanti svalīlayā ||
jagatāmupakārāya na sā karmanimittajā |
ceṣṭā tasyāprameyasya vyāpinyavyāhataḥ tmikā ||'

'evaṃprakāramakhilaṃ nityaṃ vyāpakamakṣayam |
samasta heyarahitaṃ viṣṇvākhyaṃ paramaṃ padam ||'

mahāśabdaḥ; mahārthagarbhatvāt | pūjyapadārthoktiḥ - pūjyānāmavayavārthānāmuktiḥ | pari- bhāṣā – rūḍhiḥ | anyatra upacārataḥ iti | yatra jñānādayaḥ niraṅkuśāḥ sa eva mukhyārthaḥ | guṇaleśayogādapi prayogasambhavāt anyatra vṛttiḥ aupacārikī | anyathāsiddhe prayoge na hi śaktikalpanaṃ yuktam | viśvarūpavairūpyam ityanena saṃniveśavailakṣaṇyamuktam | mahat - aparicchedyam | svalīlayeti | līlāyāḥ prayojanatvāt tadabhisandhirhetuḥ | na tu karmaṇā ityarthaḥ | upakārāya, na tu svakarmaphalopabhogāya |
vatārānuguṇā | avyāhatātmikaḥ deśakālavastukṛta pratibandharahitā |

'paraḥ parāṇāṃ paramaḥ paramātmā''tmasaṃsthitaḥ | 'rūpavarṇādi' nirdeśaviśeṣaṇavivarjitaḥ ||
apakṣayavināśābhyāṃ pariṇāmarddhijanmabhiḥ |

varjitaḥ śakyate vaktuṃ yaḥ sadā'stīti kevalam ||
sarvatrāsa samastaṃ ca vasatyatreti vai yataḥ |
tataḥ sa vāsudeveti vidvadbhiḥ paripaṭhyate ||
tadbrahma paramaṃ nityamaja' makṣara' mavyayam |
ekasvarūpaṃ ca sadā heyābhāvācca nirmalam ||
tadeva 'sarvamevaitat ' vyaktāvyaktasvarūpavat |
tathā puruṣarūpeṇa kālarūpeṇa ca sthitam ||'
'prakṛtiyāṃ mayākhyātā vyaktāvyaktasvarūpiṇī |
puruṣaścā'pyubhāvetau līyete paramātmani ||
paramātmā ca sarveṣāmādhāraḥ parameśvaraḥ |
viṣṇunāmā sa vedeṣu vedānteṣu ca gīyate ||'

'dve rūpe brahmaṇastasya mūrta cāmūrtameva ca | kṣarākṣarasvarūpe te sarvabhūteṣu ca sthite ||
akṣaraṃ tatparaṃ brahmakṣaraṃ sarvamidaṃ jagat |
ekadeśasthitasyāgnerjyotsnā vistāriṇī yathā ||
parasya brahmaṇaḥ śaktistathedamakhilaṃ jagat ||'
'viṣṇuśaktiḥ parā proktā kṣetrajñākhyā tathā'parā |
avidyā karmasaṃjñā'nyā tṛtīyā śaktiriṣyate ||
yathā kṣetrajñaśaktiḥ sā veṣṭitā nṛpa sarvagā |
saṃsāratāpāna khilānavāpnotyatisantatān ||

yathā saktaṃ jale vātoṃ bibharti kaṇikāśatam
śaktiḥ sā'pi tathā viṣṇoḥ pradhānapuruṣātmanaḥ ||'
' tadeta 'dakṣayaṃ nityaṃ jaganmunivarākhilam |
avirbhāva- tirobhāvajanma-nāśavikalpavat ||'
ityādinā, "paraṃ brahma' svabhāvata eva nirasta nikhiladoṣagandhaṃ samastakalyāṇaguṇātmakaṃ jagadutpatti-sthiti-saṃhārāntaḥ praveśa niyamanādilīlaṃ pratipādya, kṛtsasya cidacidvastunaḥ sarvāvasthāvasthitasya pāramārthikasyaiva parasya brahmaṇaḥ śarīratayā rūpatvaṃ śarīrarūpatanvaṃśa- śaktivibhūtyādiśabdaiḥ tattacchabdasāmānādhikaraṇyena ca abhidhāya tadvibhūtibhūtasya cidvastunaḥ svarūpeṇāvasthitim acinmiśratayā kṣetrajñarūpeṇa 'sthiti' ca utktvā, kṣetrajñāvasthāyāṃ puṇyapāpātmaka karmarūpā vidyāveṣṭitatvena svābhāvikajñānarūpatvānanusandhānam 'acidrapārthākāratayā' anusandhānaśca pratipāditamiti, paraṃ brahma 'saviśeṣam', tadvi- bhūtibhūtaṃ jagadapi pāramārthikameva iti jñāyate ||

jñānasattaikalakṣaṇaṃ svasaṃvedyaṃ yogayumanasaḥ na gocaraḥ ityucyate iti, anena na

prapañcāpalāpaḥ |

kathamidamavagamyate 1 - iti cet,

dvitīyasya karmākhyā vidyāvirahiṇaḥ acidviyuktasya jñānaikākārasya u. jūrtākhyavibhāgasya niṣpannayogidhyeyatayā yogayuṅmanasaḥ anālambanatayā svataśśuddhivirahāca śubhāśrayatvaṃ pratiṣidhya, paraśaktirūpamidam amūrtam, aparaśaktirūpaṃ kṣetrajñākhyabhūrta ca, paraśakti- rūpasya ātmanaḥ kṣetrajñatāpattihetubhūta tṛtīyaśaktyākhyakarmarūpāvidyā ca ityetacchaktitrayā- śrayaḥ, bhagavadasādhāraṇam 'ādityavarṇam' ityādivedāntasiddhaṃ 'mūrtasvarūpam' śubhā- śrayaḥ ityuktam | atra pariśuddha iti ucyate | tathā hi-
'na tadyogayujā śakyaṃ nṛpa cintayituṃ yataḥ |'
'dvitīyaṃ viṣṇusaṃjñasya 'yo gidhyeyaṃ paraṃ padam
samastāḥ śaktayaścaitā nṛpa yatra pratiṣṭhitāḥ |

||

tadviśvarūpabairūpyaṃ rūpamanyaddharermahat ||'

iti ca vadati ||

'ābrahmastambaparyantāḥ jagadantarvyavasthitāḥ |
prāṇinaḥ karmajanita' saṃsāravaśavartinaḥ || yatastato na te dhyāne dhyānināmupakārakāḥ |
avidyāntargatāḥ sarve te hi saṃsāragocarāḥ ||
paścādudbhūtabodhāśca dhyāne naivopakārakāḥ |
naisargiko na vai bodhaḥ teṣāmapyanyato yataḥ ||
tasmāt tadamalaṃ brahma nisargādeva bodhavat || '
ityādinā parasya brahmaṇaḥ viṣṇoḥ svarūpam svāsādhāraṇameva śubhāśrayaḥ - ityuktam | ataḥ atra na bhedāpalāpaḥ pratīyate ||

jagadbrahmaṇoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyena aikyapratīteḥ, brahmaṇa: jñavasvarūpasya arthā- kāratā bhrāntiḥ ityukte sati, arthajātasya kṛtsnasya mithyātvam uktaṃ syāt- iti cet,
tadasat ; asmin śāstre parasya brahmaṇaḥ viṣṇoḥ nirastājñānādinikhiladoṣa- gandhasya samasta kalyāṇaguṇātmakasya mahāvibhūteḥ pratipannatayā, tasya bhrāntidarśanā- sambhavāt |
sāmānādhikaraṇyena aikyapratipādanaṃ ca bādhāsaham, aviruddhaṃ ca iti anantara- meva upapādayiṣyate | ataḥ ayamapi ślokaḥ na arthasvarūpasya bādhakaḥ | tathā hi- 'yato vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante | yena jātāni jīvanti | yat prayantyabhi - saṃviśanti | tadvijijñāsasva | tadbrahma'
iti, 'jagajjanmādikāraṇaṃ brahma' ' ityavasite' sati,

'itihāsapurāṇābhyāṃ vedaṃ samupabṛṃhayet |
vibhetyalpaśrutādvedo māmayaṃ pratariṣyati ||'
iti śāstreṇa, arthasya itihāsa-purāṇābhyām upabṛṃhaṇaṃ kāryam iti vijñāyate | upabṛṃhaṇaṃ nāma - - viditasakalavedatadarthānāṃ svayogamāhimasākṣātkṛta vedatattvārthānāṃ vākyaiḥ svāvagatavedavākyārthavyaktīkaraṇam | sakala' śākhāvagatasya' vākyārthasya alpabhāga- śravaṇāt duravagamatvena tena vinā niścayāyogāt upabṛṃhaṇaṃ hi kāryameva |
tatra pulastya - vasiṣṭhabarapradānalabdhapara devatāpāramārthyajñānadattaḥ bhagavataḥ parāśarāt svāvagatavedārtho hi micchan maitreyaḥ paripapraccha -
'so'hamicchāmi dharmajña śrotuṃ tvatto yathā jagat |
babhūva bhūyaśca yathā mahābhāga ! bhaviṣyati ||

yanmayaṃ ca jagadbrahman ! yataścaitaccarācaram |

līnamāsīt yathā yatra layameṣyati yatra ca || '

ityādinā |

iti mayaṭ | kṛtsnaṃ ca jagat taccharīratayā tatpracurameva | tasmāt ' yanmayam' ityasya prativacanaṃ 'jagacasaḥ' iti sāmānādhikaraṇyam, jagadbrahmagoḥ śarīrātmabhāva- nibandhanam iti niścīyate |
anyathā nirviśeṣavastupratipādanapare śāstre abhyupagamyamāne, sarvāṇyetāni, praśna - prativacanāni na saṃgacchante | tadvivaraṇarūpaṃ kṛtsnaṃ ca śāstram na saṅga- cchate | tathā hi sati 'prapañcabhramasya kimadhiṣṭhānam 1" ityevaṃrūpasya ekasya praśnasya, 'nirviśeṣajñānamātram' ityevaṃrūpam ekamevottaraṃ syāt ||

iti ślokena prathamaṃ praṇamya, tameva hiraṇyagarbha - svāvatāra - śaṅkararūpattri- mūrtipradhānakālakṣetrajñasamaṣṭivyaṣṭirūpeṇa avasthitaṃ ca namaskaroti | tatra 'jñānasvarūpam ' ityayaṃ ślokaḥ kṣetrajñavyaṣṭyātmanā avasthitasya paramātmanaḥ svabhāva- māha' | tasmāt nātra nirviśeṣavastupratītiḥ ||

utpatyādikāryam sattvādiguṇayukta paripūrṇakarmavaśyeṣu dṛṣṭamiti, sattvādiguṇa- rahitasya paripūrṇasya akarmavaśyasya karmasambandhānarhasya kathaṃ 'sargādi' kartṛtvam abhyu- pagamyate 1 - iti codyam |
dṛṣṭasakalavisajātīyasya brahmaṇaḥ yathoditasvabhāvasyaiva, jalādivisajātīyasya agnyādeḥ auṣṇyādiśaktiyogavat sarvaśaktiyogaḥ na virudhyate - iti parihāraḥ ||

'jagataḥ pate tvam' ityādīnāṃ padānāṃ lakṣaṇā ca syāt, bhagavato mahāvarāhasya stutiprakaraṇavirodhaśca |

līlayā mahīm uddharataḥ

'yataḥ kṛtsnaṃ jagat jñānātmanā tvayā ātmatayā vyāptatvena tava mūrtam, tasmāt tvadātmakatvānubhava sādhanayogavirahiṇaḥ 'etat kevaladeva - manuṣyādirūpam ' iti bhrānti- jñānena paśyanti' ityāha 'yadetadddaśyate' iti |
' na kevalaṃ, vastutaḥ tvadātmakaṃ jagat devamanuṣyādyātmakam iti darśanameva bhramaḥ, jñānākārāṇāmātmanāṃ devamanuṣyādyarthākāratvadarśanamapi bhramaḥ' ityāha 'jñānasvarūpamakhi- lam' iti |
'ye punaḥ buddhimantaḥ jñānasvarūpātmavidaḥ sarvasya bhagavadātmakatvānubhavasādhana- yogayogyapariśuddhamanasaśca te devamanuṣyādiprakṛtipariṇāma' viśeṣaśarīrarūpam' 'idam akhilaṃ jagat śarīrātiriktajñānasvarūpātmakam tvaccharīrañca paśyanti' ityāha 'ye tu

jñānavida : ' iti | anyathā ślokānāṃ paunaruktyam, padānāṃ lakṣaṇā, arthavirodhaḥ, prakaraṇavirodhaḥ, śāstratātparyavirodhaśca ||

tatra paraśabdaḥ svavyatiriktātmavacanaḥ, anyaśabdaḥ tasyāpi jñānaikākāratvāt anyākāratva- pratiṣedhārthaḥ |
etaduktaṃ bhavati - yadi madhyatiriktaḥ ko'pyātmā madākārabhūta jñānā 'kārāt anyākāraḥ asti, tadā ' aham evamākāraḥ, ayaśca anyādṛśākāraḥ' iti śakyate vyapadeṣṭum | na caivamasti; sarveṣāṃ jñānaikākāratvena samānatvādeva iti |
'veṇurandhravibhedena' ityatrā'pi, ākāravaiṣamyam ātmanāṃ na svarūpakṛtam: api tu devādipiṇḍa praveśa kṛtam iti upadiśyate; na ātmaikyam | dṛṣṭānte ca anekarandhravartināṃ vātraṃśānāṃ na svarūpaikyam; api tu ākārasāmyameva | teṣām vāyutvena ekākārāṇām randhrabhedaniṣkramaṇakṛto hi ṣaḍjādisaṃjñā bhedaḥ | evam ātmanāṃ devādi saṃjñā bheda:' | ' yathā taijasā 'pyapārthivadravyāṃśabhūtānāṃ padārthānāṃ tattadravyatvenaikyameva na svarūpaikyam ; tathā vāyavīyānāmaṃśānāmapi svarūpabhedaḥ avarja- nīyaḥ ||

ākāra:' iti upasaṃharan, 'devādyākārabhedena ātmasu bhedamohaṃ parityaja' ityāha | anyathā dehātiriktātmopadeśyasvarūpa 'aham tvam, sarvametat ātmasvarūpam ' iti bhedanirdeśaḥ na ghaṭate | ahaṃ tvamādiśabdānām upalakṣyeṇa 'sarvametat ātmasvarūpam' ityanena sāmānādhikaraṇyāt upalakṣaṇatvamapi na saṅgacchate | 'so'pi yathopadeśamakarot ' ityāha, 'tatyāja bhedaṃ paramārthadṛṣṭiḥ' iti ||

jīvaparayoḥ | 'ātmasvarūpaikyam' uktarītyā niṣiddham | jīvaparayorapi svarūpaikyaṃ dehātmanovi na sambhavati | tathā ca śrutiḥ-

'dvā suparṇā sayujā sakhāyā samānaṃ vṛkṣaṃ pariṣasvajāte |
tayoranyaḥ pippalaṃ svādvatyanaśnannanyo abhicākaśīti ||'
'ṛtaṃ pibantau sukṛtasya loke guhāṃ praviṣṭau parame 'parāye |
chāyātapau brahmavido vadanti pañcāgnayo ye ca triṇāciketāḥ ||'
'antaḥ praviṣṭaḥ śāstā janānāṃ sarvātmā |'

ityādyā | asminnapi śāstre

'sa sarvabhūtakṛtiṃ vikārān guṇādidoṣāṃśca mune vyatītaḥ |
atīta sarvāvaraṇo'khilātmā tetṛtaṃ yad bhuvanāntale ||' 'samasta kalyāṇaguṇātmako'sau '
'paraḥ parāṇāṃ sakalā na yatra kleśādayaḥ santi parāvareśe | ' 'avidyā karmasaṃjñāsnyā tṛtīyā śaktiriṣyate ||'

"yayā' kṣetrajña śaktiḥ sā veṣṭitā nṛpa ! sarvagā ||'

iti bhedavyadeśāt |

" ubhaye'pi hi bhedenainamadhīyate '

'bhedavyapadeśāccānyaḥ'

" adhika tu' bhedanirdeśāt'

'ya ātmani tiṣṭhannātmano'ntaro yamātmā na veda, yasyātmā śarīram, ya ānānamantaro yamayati'
'prājñenātmanā sampariṣvaktaḥ '
'prājñenātmanā'nvārūḍhaḥ'
ityādibhiḥ ubhayoḥ anyonyapratyanīkākāreṇa svarūpanirṇaya t |
nāpi sādhanānuṣṭhānena nirmuktā vidyasya pareṇa svarūpaikyasambhavaḥ; avidyāśrayatva- yogyasya tadanarhatvāsambhavāt | yathoktam -
ubhaye'pi ityādimūtradvayasya viṣayavākyam - ya ātmanīti |

iti ||

'bhogamātrasāmyaliṅgācca '

'ya ihātmānamanuvidya vrajanti etāṃśca satyān kāmān,

' sa tatra paryeti'

" tadā' vidvān puṇyapāpe vidhūya nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyamupaiti '

ityādyāḥ ||

'brahma veda brahmaiva bhavati'

ityatrā'pi -

'nāmarūpādvimuktaḥ parātparaṃ puruṣamupaiti divyam'
'nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyamupaiti'
'paraṃjyotirupasaṃpadya svena rūpeṇābhiniṣpadyate'
ityādibhiḥ aikārthyāt prākṛtanāmarūpābhyāṃ vinirmuktasya nirastatatkṛtabhedasya jñānaikā- kāratayā brahmaprakāratā ucyate | prakāraikye ca tattvavyavahāraḥ mukhya eva | yathā " seyaṃ' gauḥ" iti | atrā'pi

'kṣetrajñaḥ karaṇī jñānaṃ karaṇaṃ tasya vai dvija |

niṣpādya mukti kārya hi 'kṛtakṛtyaṃ' 'nivartayet' |'
iti karaṇasya " parabrahma' dhyānarūpasya prakṣīṇāśeṣabhāvanātmakharūpaprādhyā kṛtakṛtyatvena nivṛttivacanāt yāvatsiddhi anuṣṭheyam ityuktvā,

'tadbhāvabhāvamāpannaḥ tadā'sau paramātmanā |
bhavatyabhedī bhedaśca tasyājñānakṛto bhavet ||'

iti muktasya svarūpamāha | tadbhāvaḥ - brahmaṇo bhāvaḥ - svabhāvaḥ, tadbhāvaḥ - brahmaṇo bhāvaḥ -- svabhāvaḥ, na tu svarūpaikyam; ' tadbhāvabhāvamāpannaḥ' iti dvitīya bhāvaśabdānanvayāt pūrvoktārthavirodhācca | brahmaṇaḥ prakṣīṇāśeṣabhāvanatvam, tadāpattiḥ tadbhāvabhāvāpattiḥ | tadā 'asau paramātmanā abhedī bhavati - bhedarahito bhavati |

| yat

yadā evamāpannaḥ,

jñānaikākāratayā paramātmanā 'ekaprakārasya asya tasmādbheda: devādirūpaḥ | tadanvayaḥ asya karmarūpa jñānamūlaḥ na svarūpakṛtaḥ | sa tu devādibhedaḥ parabrahmadhyānena

mūlabhūtājñānarūpe karmaṇi vinaṣṭe, hetvabhāvāt nivartate iti abhedī bhavati |

"ekasvarūpa' bhedastu bāhyakarma vṛtiprajaḥ |
devādibhede'padhvaste' 'nāstyanāvaraṇo hi saḥ ||'

etadeva vivṛṇoti

'vibhedajanake jñāne nāśamātyantikaṃ gate |

ātmano brahmaṇo bhedamasantaṃ kaḥ kariṣyati 1 ||'

'caturvidho'pi bhedo'yaṃ mithyājñānanibandhanaḥ | '

'devādirūpa' bhedam kaḥ kariṣyati | ityarthaḥ | 'avidyā karmasaṃjñāsnyā'

iti hi atraivoktam ||

na tadasti vinā yat syāt ' iti | bhagavadvibhūtyupasaṃhāraśrāyamiti, tathaiva abhyupagantavyam |

tataḥ idamucyate-

I

avidyāyāṃ saptavidhāḥ anupapattayaḥ

'jñānarūpaṃ paraṃ brahma tanivartya mṛṣātmakam | ajñānaṃ cet tiraskuryāt kaḥ prabhu' stannivartane' ||
jñānaṃ brahmeti cedjñānamajñānasya nivartakam |

brahmavat tatprakāśatvāt tadapi nivartakam ||

jñānaṃ brahmeti vijñānamasti cet syāt prameyatā |

|

brahmaṇo'nanubhūtitvaṃ tvaduktyaiva prasajyate || '
'jñānasvarūpaṃ brahma' iti jñānam tasyāḥ avidyāyāḥ bādhakam na svarūpabhūtaṃ jñānam - iti cet,

na, ubhayorapi brahmasvarūpaprakāśatve sati anyatarasya avidyāvirodhitvam', 'anyatarasya na', iti viśeṣānavagamāt |
etaduktaṃ bhavati - " jñānasvarūpaṃ ' brahma' ' ityanena jñānena' brahmaṇi yaḥ svabhāvaḥ avagamyate, saḥ brahmaṇaḥ svayaṃprakāśatvena svayameva prakāśate ' iti, avidyāvirodhitve' na kaścidviśeṣaḥ svarūpa- tadviṣayajñānayoḥ - iti ||

brahmasvarūpājñānaṃ nāma tasya 'sadvitīyatvameva ' | mithyātvajñānena nivṛttam | svarūpaṃ tu svānubhavasiddham - iti cet,
na; brahmaṇaḥ advitīyatvaṃ svarūpaṃ svānubhavasiddhamiti, tadvirodhi sadvitīyatva-

tattu tadvayatiriktasya

" rūpājñānaṃ tadvādhaśca na syātām |

advitīyatvaṃ dharmaḥ iti cet,

na; anubhava svarūpasya' brahmaṇaḥ anubhāvyadharmavirahasya bhavataiva 'pratipādita- ataḥ jñāna' svarūpasya ' brahmaṇaḥ virodhādeva na ajñānāśrayatvam ||

svāt' |

navat paramārthaḥ ; anabhyupagamāt |

nā'pi aparamārthaḥ ; tathā sati hi draṣṭatvena vā, dṛśyatvena vā, dṛśitvena

vā abhyupagamanīyaḥ |

na tāvat dṛśi ; dṛśisvarūpa bhedānabhyupagamāt | sākṣādddaśeḥ mādhyamikapakṣa' prasaṅgena' apāramārthyānabhyupagamācca |
bhramādhiṣṭhānabhūtāyāstu | draṣṭṛ-dṛśyayoḥ, tadavacchinnāyāḥ dṛśeśca kālpanikatvena, mūladoṣāntara|pekṣayā anavasthā syāt |
atha — etatparijihīrṣayā paramārthasatī anubhūtireva brahmarūpā doṣaḥ - iti cet,
brahmaiva cet doṣaḥ prapañcadarśanasyaiva tat mūlaṃ syāt kiṃ prapañcatulyāvidyāntara- parikalpanena ? brahmaṇaḥ doṣatve sati, tasya nityatvena anirmokṣaśca syāt | ataḥ yāvat brahmavyatiriktapāramārthikadoṣānabhyupagamaḥ na tāvat bhrāntiḥ upapāditā bhavati ||

anirvacanīyatvaṃ ca kimabhipretam 1
sadasadvilakṣaṇatvam - iti cet,

etaduktaṃ bhavati sarvaṃ hi vastujātaṃ pratītivyavasthāpyam | sadasadākārā | sadasadākārāyāstu pratīteḥ sadasadvilakṣaṇaṃ viṣayaḥ, iti abhyupagamya- māne, sarvam sarvapratīteḥ viṣayaḥ syāt - iti ||

tadupādānatvaṃ ca mithyābhūtasya arthasya 'mithyābhūtameva kāraṇaṃ bhavitumarhati iti

hetubalāt avagamyate ||

asti cet, virodhādeva 'na jñānābhāvānubhava' sambhavaḥ |

no cet,

" sambhavati |

jñānābhāvasya anumeyatve, abhāvākhyapramāṇaviṣayatve ca iyam anupapattiḥ samānā | asya ajñānasya bhāvarūpatve, dharmi- pratiyogijñānasadbhāve'pi virodhābhāvāt, ayam anubhavaḥ bhāvarūpājñānaviṣaya eva abhyupagantavyaḥ - iti |

nanu ca idaṃ bhāvarūpamapi ajñānaṃ viṣayaviśeṣavyāvṛttameva sākṣicaitanyasya viṣayo bhavati | sa viṣaya : ' pramāṇādhīna siddhi: iti, kathamiva sākṣicaitanyena asmadarthavyāvṛttam ajñānaṃ viṣayīkriyate ?
naiṣa doṣaḥ ; sarvameva vastujātaṃ jñātatayā ajñātatayā vā sākṣicaitanyasya viṣayabhūtam | tatra jaḍatvena jñātatayā sidhyata eva pramāṇavyavadhānāpekṣā | ajaḍasya tu pratyagvastunaḥ svayaṃ sidhyataḥ na pramāṇavyavadhānāpekṣā iti, sadaiva

tasmāt nyāyopabṛṃhitena pratyakṣeṇa

'ajñānasya vyāvartakatvena' avabhāsaḥ yujyate |
bhāvarūpameva ajñānaṃ pratīyate ||

āloka bhāvamātraṃ vā, rūpadarśanābhāvamātraṃ vā tamaḥ, na 'dravyāntaram' | tat kathaṃ bhāvarūpājñānasādhane nidarśanatayā upanyasyate 1 - iti cet,
ucyate - bahulatva - viralatvādyavasthā yogena, rūpavattayā ca upalabdheḥ dravyāntarameva tamaḥ iti niravadyam - iti ||

apratipannaścet, vyāvartakāśrayaviṣayajñānaśūnyam ajñānaṃ kathamanu' bhūyeta' ?
atha – viśadasvarūpāvabhāsaḥ ajñānavirodhī | aviśadasvarūpaṃ tu pratīyate iti, āśraya viṣayajñāne satyapi na ajñānānubhavavirodhaḥ - iti,

hanta tarhi jñāna prāgabhāvo'pi viśadasvarūpaviṣayaḥ | aviśadasvarūpaviṣayam iti na kaścidviśeṣaḥ; anyatrābhiniveśāt ||

āśrayapratiyogijñānaṃ tu

sāpekṣatvam astyeva | tathāhi - 'ajñānam' iti jñānābhāvaḥ, tadanyaḥ tadvirodhī vā | trayāṇāmapi tatsvarūpajñānāpejhā avaśyāśrayaṇīyā |

yadyapi tamassvarūpapratipattau prakāśāpekṣā na vidyate ; tathāpi 'prakāśavirodhi' ityanena ākāreṇa pratipattau prakāśapratipatyapekṣā astyeva | kadācit svarūpeṇa sidhyati, api tu ajñānamityeva |

bhavadabhimatājñānaṃ na

tathā sati jñānābhāvavat

tadapekṣatvaṃ samānam ||

svānubhavasvarūpamapi tirohita' svarūpam ' ajñānam anubhavati — iti cet,

kimidaṃ tirohitasvarūpatvam 1
aprakāśitasvarūpatvam - iti cet,
svānubhavasvarūpasya katham aprakāśitasvarūpatvam 1

svānubhavasvarūpasyā'pi anyataḥ aprakāśitasvarūpatvam upapadyate - iti cet, evaṃ tahiṃ prakāśākhyadharmānabhyupagamena prakāśasyaiva svarūpatvāt, anyataḥ svarūpa- nāśa eva syāt, iti pūrvamevoktam ||

kiṃ ca - brahmaṇa: ajñānānubhavaḥ kiṃ 'svataḥ', anyato vā ?

svataścet, ajñānānubhavasya svarūpaprayuktatvena anirmokṣaḥ syāt ; anubhūti- svarūpasya brahmaṇaḥ ajñānānubhavasvarūpatvena, mithyārajatabādhakajñānena rajatānubhavasyā'pi nivṛttivat, nivartakajñānena ajñāna nubhūtirūpabrahmasvarūpanivṛttirvā |

anyataścet kiṃ tat anyat 1

ajñānāntaraṃ - iti cet, anavasthā syāt |
brahma tiraskṛtyaiva svayam anubhavaviṣayo bhavati iti cet,

tathāsati idam ajñānam kācādivat svasattayā brahma tiraskaroti iti, jñāna- bādhyatvam ajñānasya na syāt |
atha - idamajñānam svayamanādi brahmaṇaḥ svasākṣitvam brahmasvarūpatiraskṛtiṃ ca yugapadeva karoti | ataḥ na anavasthādayaḥ doṣāḥ - iti,
naitat ; svānubhava' svarūpasya brahmaṇaḥ svarūpatiraskṛtimantareṇa sākṣitvā- pādanāyogāt |

hetvantareṇa tiraskṛtam - iti cet,

tarhi asya anāditvam apāstam |

anavasthā ca pūrvoktā |

atiraskṛta svarūpasyaiva sākṣitvāpādane - brahmaṇaḥ svānubhavaikatānatā ca na syāt ||

niraṃśe nirviśeṣe prakāśamātre vastuni ākāradvayāsambhavena tiraskāraḥ prakāśaśca yugapat na saṅgacchete |
atha – saccidānandaikarasaṃ brahma avidyayā tirohitasvarūpam aviśadamiva lakṣyate iti,
prakāśamātrasvarūpasya viśadatā, aviśadatā vā kiṃ rūpā 1
etaduktaṃ bhavati---ya: sāṃśaḥ saviśeṣaḥ prakāśaviṣayaḥ, tasya sakalāvabhāsaḥ viśadāvabhāsaḥ | katipaya viśeṣarahitāvabhāsaśca aviśadāvabhāsaḥ | tatra ya ākāraḥ apratipannaḥ tasmin aṃśe prakāśābhāvādeva prakāśāvaiśadyaṃ na vidyate | yaśva aṃśaḥ pratipannaḥ, tasminnaṃśe tadviṣayaprakāśaḥ viśada eva | ataḥ sarvatra prakāśāṃśe avaiśadyaṃ na sambhavati | viṣaye'pi svarūpe pratīyamāne tadgatakatipayaviśeṣāpratītireva avaiśadyam | tasmāt aviṣaye nirviśeṣe prakāśamātre brahmaṇi- svarūpe prakāśamāne 'taṃgatakatipaya'- viśeṣā' pratipatti rūpāvaiśadyaṃ nāma ajñānakāryam na sambhavati iti ||

nivṛttau ca vastu kiṃ rūpam 1 - iti vivecanīyam |
viśadasvarūpam - iti cet,
tat viśadasvarūpaṃ prāk asti, na vā ?
asti cet, avidyākāryam avaiśadyam, tannivṛttiśca na syātām !
no cet, mokṣasya kāryatayā anityatā syāt !

yaduktam - anumānenā'pi bhāvarūpam ajñānaṃ siddhayati – iti,
tadayuktam ; anumānāsambhavāt |
nanu uktamanumānam !

_

satyamuktam, duruktaṃ tu tat; ajñāne'pi anabhimatājñānāntara' sādhanena' viruddhatvāt hetoḥ | tatra ajñānāntarāsādhane, hetoḥ anaikāntyam | sādhane ca,

tat ajñānam ajñānasākṣitvaṃ nivārayati |

tataśca ajñānakalpanā niṣphalā syāt ||

indriyāṇāmapi jñānotpattihetutvameva na prakāśakatvam | pradīpaprabhāyāstu cakṣuri- ndriyasya jñānamutpādayataḥ, virodhitamonirasanadvāreṇa upakārakatvamātrameva | kajñānotpattau vyāpriyamāṇacakṣurindriyopakārakahetutvamapekṣya, dīpasya prakāśakatva- vyavahāraḥ |

prakāśa-

pratiprayogāśva_

1. vivādādhyāsitam ajñānam - na jñānamātrabrahmāśrayam; ajñānatvāt, śukti- kādyajñānavat | jñātrāśrayaṃ hi tat |
2. vivādādhyāsitam ajñānam - na 'jñānamātrabrahmāvaraṇam ; ajñānatvāt, śuktikādyajñānavat | viṣayāvaraṇaṃ hi tat |
3. vivādādhyāsitam ajñānam – na jñānanivartyam ; jñāna viṣayānāvaraṇatvāt | yat jñānanivartyam ajñānam, tat jñānaviṣayāvaraṇam | yathā śuktikādyajñānam | 4. brahmana ajñānāspadam ; jñātṛtvavirahāt ghaṭādivat |

brahma-na ajñānāvaraṇam ; jñānāviṣayatyāt | yat ajñānāvaraṇam, tat jñānaviṣayabhūtam | yathā śuktikādi |

.5.

6. brahma - na jñānanivartyājñānam ; jñānāviṣayatvāt | yat jñānanivartyajñānam, tat jñānaviṣayabhūtam | yathā śuktikādi |
7. vivādādhyāsitam pramāṇajñānam - svaprāgabhāvātiriktājñānapūrvakaṃ na bhavati ; pramāṇajñānatvāt bhavadabhimatājñānasādhana pramāṇajñānavat |
8. jñānam - na vastuno vināśakam ; śaktiviśeṣopa' bṛṃhaṇavirahe' sati jñāna - tvāt | yat vastuno vināśakam, tat śaktiviśeṣopa bṛṃhitaṃ jñānam, ajñānaṃ ca dṛṣṭam yathā īśvara- yogiprabhṛtijñānam | yathā ca mudgarādi |

9. bhāvarūpam ajñānam - na jñānavināśyam ; bhāvarūpatvāt 'ghaṭādivat iti ||

śruti tadarthāpattibhyām ajñānāsiddhiḥ anantarameva vakṣyate | " midhyārthasya' mithyaiva upādānaṃ bhavitumarhati' ityetadapi, 'na vilakṣaṇatvāt ' ityadhikaraṇanyāyena parihiyate | ataḥ anirvacanīyājñānaviṣayā na kācidapi pratītiḥ asti ||

anyasya anyathābhānāyogācca 'sadasada nirvacanīyam apūrvameva idaṃ rajataṃ doṣavaśāt pratīyate' iti kalpanīyam - iti cet,

anyaviśeṣaṇam anyaviśeṣaṇatvena, jñānadvayaṃ 'ekatvena ca', viṣayāsadbhāvapakṣe'pi - vidyamānatvena ||

atha indriyādigato doṣaḥ,

tanna : tasya puruṣāśrayatvena arthagatakāryasya utpādakatvāyogāt |
nā'pi indriyāṇi teṣāṃ jñānakāraṇatvāt |
nāpi duṣṭāni indriyāṇi teṣāmapi 'svakāryabhūte jñāne' eva hi viśeṣa -

karatvam |

anādimithyājñānopādānatvaṃ tu pūrvameva nirastam ||

rajatādisādṛśyāt - iti cet,

tarhi 'tatsadṛśam' ityeva pratīti-śabdau syātām ! 'rajatādi' jātiyogāt – iti cet,
sā kiṃ paramārthabhūtā ! 'aparamārthabhūtā vā' ?
na tāvat paramārthabhūtā; tasyāḥ aparamārthānvayāyogāt |
nā'pi aparamārthabhūtā; paramārthānvayāyogāt, aparamārthe paramārthabuddhi-śabdayoḥ nirvāhakatvāyogācca – ityalam apariṇatakutarkanirasanena ||

yathārthaṃ sarvavijñānamiti vedavidāṃ matam | śrutismṛtibhyaḥ sarvasya sarvātmatvapratītitaḥ || 1 ||
'bahusyā' miti saṅkalpapūrva sṛṣṭyādyupakrame |
'tāsāṃ trivṛtamekaikā ' miti śrutyaiva 'coditam ' || 2 ||
trivṛtkaraṇamevaṃ hi pratyakṣeṇopalabhyate |
'yadagne rohitaṃ rūpaṃ tejasa' stadapāmapi || 3 ||
śuklaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ pṛthivyāśvedanādeva trirūpatā | śrutyaiva darśitā tasmāt sarve sarvatra saṅgatāḥ || 4 ||
purāṇe caivamevoktaṃ vaiṣṇave sṛṣṭyupakrame | 'nānāvīryāḥ pṛthagbhūtāstataste saṃhatiṃ vinā || 5 ||
nāśaknuvan prajāḥ sraṣṭumasamāgamya kṛtsnaśaḥ | sametyānyonyasaṃyogaṃ parasparasamāśrayāḥ || 6 ||

mahadādyā viśeṣāntā hyaṇḍa' mityādinā tataḥ | sūtrakāro'pi bhūtānāṃ trirūpatvaṃ ' tathā ' 'vadat || 7 || 'tryātmakatvāttu bhūyastvā ' ' diti tenābhidhābhidā | somābhāve ca pūtīkagrahaṇaṃ śruti coditam || 8 || somāvayavasadbhāvāditi nyāyavido viduḥ |
śrībhāve ca nīvāragrahaṇaṃ vrīhibhāvataḥ || 9 ||
tadeva sadṛśaṃ tasya yat tadravyaikadeśabhāk | śuktyādau rajatādeśva bhāvaḥ śrutyaiva bodhitaḥ || 10 || rūpyaśuktyādi 'nirdeśabhedo' bhūyastvahetukaḥ | rūpyādisadṛśaścāyaṃ śuktyādirupalabhyate || 11 ||
atastasyā'tra sadbhāvaḥ pratīterapi niścitaḥ | kadāciccakṣurādestu doṣāt śukthaṃśavarjitaḥ || 12 ||

rajatāṃśī gṛhīto'to rajatārthī pravartate | doṣahānau tu śuktayaṃśe gṛhīte tannivartate || 13 || ato yathārtha rūpyādivijñānaṃ śuktikādiṣu | bādhyabādhakabhāvo'pi bhūyastvenopapadyate || 14 ||

śuktibhūyastvavaikalya- sākalyagraharūpataḥ |
nāsto midhyārtha satyārthaviṣayatvanibandhanaḥ ||
evaṃ sarvasya sarvatve vyavahāra vyavasthitiḥ || 15 ||

tattatkāla vasānāḥ tathābhūtāśca' arthāḥ sṛjyante |

tathā hi śrutiḥ svapna-

viṣayā-

'na tatra rathāḥ, na rathayogāḥ, na panthāno bhavanti | atha rathān ratha- yogān pathaḥ sṛjate | na tatrā''nandāḥ mudaḥ pramudo bhavanti | athāss- nandān mudaḥ pramudaḥ sṛjate | na tatra "veśāntāḥ puṣkariṇyaḥ sravantyo bhavanti | atha veśāntān puṣkariṇyaḥ sravantyaḥ sṛjate | sa hi kartā |"

iti |

'tasmillokāḥ śritāḥ sarve tadu nātyeti kaścana ||'

" iti ca | sūtrakāro'pi -
'sandhye sṛṣṭirāha hi'
'nirmātāraṃ caike putrādayaśca'
iti sūtradvayena, 'svāpneṣu' artheṣu jīvasya sraṣṭṛtvam āśaṅkaya,
'māyāmātraṃ tu kātsnyenānabhivyaktasvarūpatvāt '
ityādinā, na jīvasya saṅkalpamātreṇa sraṣṭṛtvamupapadyate : jīvasya svābhāvika satya- saṅkalpatvādeḥ' kṛtsnasya saṃsāradaśāyām anabhivyaktasvarūpatvāt | īśvarasyaiva tatta- tpuruṣamātrānubhāvyatayā āścaryabhūtā sṛṣṭiriyam |
' tasmilokāḥ śritāḥ sarve tadu nātyeti kaścana ||' iti paramātmaiva tatra sraṣṭā' ' ityavagamyate -- iti pariharati ||

'śiraśchedādayaśca' puṇya-pāpaphalabhūtāḥ śayānadehasarūpasaṃsthānadehāntarasṛṣṭyā upapadyante ||

marīcikājalajñāne'pi - tejaḥ pṛthivyorapi ambuno vidyamānatvāt doṣeṇa tejaḥ pṛthivyoḥ agrahaṇāt, adṛṣṭa' vaśācca' ambunaḥ grahaṇāt yathārthatvam |
alātacakre'pi – alātasya drutataragamanena sarvadeśasaṃyogāt antarālāgrahaṇāt, tathā pratītiḥ upapadyate | cakrapratītāvapi antarālā grahaṇapūrvakata taddeśa saṃyuktatattadvastu- grahaṇameva | kvacit antarālābhāvāt antarālāgrahaṇam, kvacit śaighrayāt agrahaṇam- iti viśeṣaḥ | ataḥ tadapi yathārtham |
darpaṇādiṣu - nijamukhādipratītirapi yathārthā |

indriya-

darpaṇādipratihatagatayo hi

'nāyana' raśmayaḥ darpaṇādideśagrahaṇapūrvakaṃ nijamukhādi gṛhṇanti | tatrā'pi atiśaighrayāt antarālā 'grahaṇāt, tathā pratītiḥ |

diṅmospi - digantarasya asyāṃ diśi vidyamānatvāt adṛṣṭavaśena eta- hiṃgaṃśaviyuktaḥ digantarāṃśī gṛhyate | ataḥ digantarapratītiḥ yathārthaiva |
marīciketi | marīcikājaladhiyaḥ yāthārthyopapādanamidam | adṛṣṭeti | adṛṣṭasya niyāmakatvaṃ sarvairapi khyātyantaravādibhiḥ abhyupetyam | anyathā sarveṣāṃ sarvadā sarvatra sarva viṣaya - bhramaprasaṅgāt | kadācidapi bhramābhāvaprasaṅgācca |
antarālā- grahaṇāt — alātasya tattatpradeśena pūrvāpara saṃyogayo : madhyaka lāgrahaṇāt - kramāgrahaṇāt | cakrapratītiḥ – samyakcakrapratītiḥ |

darpaṇādiṣviti | darpaṇādiṣu savyadakṣiṇaviparyāsaḥ katham ? - iti cet, āpekṣiko hi savyadakṣiṇabhāva: | tatra svābhimukhatayā viparyastasavyadakṣiṇabhāvasya darpaṇasya, mukhasya ca antarālā grahaṇameva viparyāsaḥ darpaṇadeśagataḥ | tena agṛhītāntarālaṃ mukhamiti, abhimukhasya darpaṇasya, mukhasya ca antarālāgrahaṇāt ābhimukhyapratīteḥ yāthārthyam | svālpatvādi ca |

dvicandrajñānādāvapi – aṅgulyavaṣṭambhatimirādibhiḥ sāmagrībhedāt, sāmagrīdvayam anyonyanirapekṣaṃ candragrahaṇadvaya heturbhavati | sāmagrī svadeśaviśiṣṭaṃ candraṃ gṛhṇāti |

'nāyana' tejogatibhedena

tatra ekā

dvitīyā tu kiñcidvakragatiḥ candra- samīpadeśagrahaṇapūrvakaṃ candraṃ svadeśaviyuktaṃ gṛhṇāti | ataḥ sāmagrīdvayena yugapat deśadvaya viśiṣṭa candragrahaṇe, grahaṇabhedena grāhyākārabhedāt ekatva grahaṇābhāvācca ' ' dvau candrau' iti bhavati pratītiviśeṣaḥ | 'deśāntarasya tadviśeṣaṇatvam deśāntarasya agṛhītasvadeśacandrasya ca nirantaragrahaṇena bhavati | tatra sāmagrī dvitvaṃ pāramārthikam | tena deśadvaya viśiṣṭacandragrahaṇadvayaṃ ca pāramārthikam | kāradvitvaṃ ca pāramārthikam |

grahaṇadvitvena candrasyaiva grāhyā-

'tatra' 'viśeṣaṇadvaya viśiṣṭacandragrahaṇadvayasya eka eva candra: 'grāhyaḥ' iti' prahaṇe, 'pratyabhijñānavat' kevalacakṣuṣaḥ sāmarthyābhāvāt, cākṣuṣajñānaṃ tathaiva' avaṣṭhite | dvayoḥ

cakṣuṣoḥ ekasāmagracannarbhave'pi timirādidoṣaminnaṃ cākṣuṣaṃ tejaḥ sāmagrīdvayaṃ bhavati iti kāryakalpyam | apagate tu doṣe svadeśaviśiṣṭasya candrasya ekagrahaṇa vedyatvāt, "eka- ścandraḥ" iti bhavati pratyayaḥ | doṣakṛtaṃ tu sāmagrī dvitvam, tatkṛtaṃ ' grahaṇa dvitvam", tatkṛtaṃ grāhyākāradvitvaṃ ca iti niravadyam |
ataḥ sarva vijñānajātaṃ yathārtham iti siddham | taistairvādibhireva prapañcitāni iti, na tatra yatnaḥ kriyate |
athavā kimanena bahunā upapādanaprakāreṇa 1

khyātyantarāṇāṃ dūṣaṇāni

pratyakṣānumānāgamākhyaṃ pramāṇajātam, āgamagamyaṃ ca nirastanikhiladoṣagandham anavadhika tirāyāsaṃkhyeyakalyāṇaguṇagaṇaṃ sarvajñaṃ satyasaṅkalpaṃ paraṃ brahma abhyupagacchatāṃ kiṃ na setsyati 1 kiṃ nopapadyate ?
bhagavatā hi pareṇa brahmaṇā kṣetrajñapuṇyapāpānuguṇaṃ tadbhogyatvāya akhilaṃ jagat sṛjatā sukhaduḥkhopekṣāphalānubhavānubhāvyāḥ " padārthāḥ sarvasādhāraṇānubhava viṣayāḥ, kecana tattatpuruṣamātrānubhava viṣayāḥ tattatkālāvasānāḥ tathā tathā anubhāvyāḥ sṛjyante | bhūtayoḥ cakṣuṣoḥ sāmagrī dvitvamapīti bhāvaḥ | ākāra dvitvam iti |

svadeśaviśiṣṭatvam

ekaḥ ākāraḥ | agṛhītāntarālaṃ pradeśāntaram ekaḥ ākāraḥ | tasya ākāratvaṃ viśeṣaṇatvam | cākṣuṣatejo gati bhedarūpa sāmagrīdvayajanyam atiśaitryāt agṛhītakramam agṛhītabhedaṃ ca jñānadvayam ; agṛhītaikatvaṃ candram, agṛhītāntarālaṃ deśāntaraṃ ca nirantaraṃ gṛhṇāti | tataḥ dvicandra- vyavahāraḥ ityarthaḥ | iti niravadyam iti | yathārthakhyātisamarthanam ityarthaḥ |
' athavā kimanena' ityādipāṭho bahuṣu kośeṣu dṛśyate | vyākhyātṛbhiḥ vyākhyātaśca | tatra parāgarthaviṣayā parokṣameṣu ekenaivottareṇa parihāraḥ kṛto bhavati |

'tatra' bādhya bādhakabhāvaḥ sarvānubhava viṣayatayā, tadrahitatayā ca upapadyate iti sarva samañjasam ||

'etaṃ brahmalokaṃ na vindanti anṛtena hi pratyūḍhāḥ'

iti vacanāt |

'nā'sadāsīno sadāsīt'
ityatrā'pi sadasacchabdau cidacidvayaṣṭiviṣayoṃ; utpattivelāyāṃ sattyacchabdābhi- hitayoḥ cidacidvyaṣṭibhūtayoḥ vastunoḥ apyayakāle 'acitsamaṣṭi' bhūte tama- śabdābhidheye vastuni pralayapratipādanaparatvāt asya vākyasya | nātra kasyacit sadasada nirvacanīyatā ucyate ; sadasatoḥ kāla viśeṣe asadbhāva' mātravacanāt |

satyaṃ tamaśabdena acitsamaṣṭirūpāyāḥ prakṛteḥ sūkṣmāvasthā ucyate |

tasyāstu

'māyāṃ tu prakṛtiṃ vidyāt'
iti māyāśabdena abhidhānāt anirvacanīyatvam - iti cet,

naitadevam ; māyāśabdasya anirvacanīyavācitvaṃ na dṛṣṭam iti |
māyāśabdasya mithyā paryāyatvena 'anirvacanīya' vācitvam - iti cet,

tadapi nāsti | na hi sarvatra māyāśabdaḥ mithyāviṣayaḥ ; āsura rākṣasā- strādiṣu satyeṣveva māyāśabdaprayogāt | yathoktam -

'tena māyāsahasraṃ tacchambara syāśugāminā |
bālasya rakṣatā deha maikaikaśyena sūditam ||'

iti ||

ataḥ māyāśabdaḥ vicitrārtha sarga karā | bhidhāvī | prakṛteśca māyāśabdābhidhānam, vicitrārtha sarga karatvādeva |
'asmānmāyī sṛjate viśvametat tasmiṃścā'nyo māyayā saṃniruddha:' iti, māyāśabdavācyāyāḥ prakṛteḥ vicitrārthasargakaratvaṃ darśayati |

|

paramapuruṣasya ca

tadvattāmātreṇa māyitvamucyate ; na ajñatvena | jīvasyaiva hi māyayā nirodhaḥ śrūyate

'tasmiṃścāsnyo māyayā saṃniruddhaḥ'

iti,

'anādimāyayā supto 'yadā jīvaḥ' prabudhyate'

iti ca |

'indro māyāmiḥ pururūpa īyate'

'ityatrāspi vicitrāḥ śaktayaḥ' abhidhīyante

ata eva hi

ata iti | anuyāyina eva pravṛttinimittatvāt māyāśabdaprayogaviṣayeṣu sarveṣu anugatam āścaryatvameva māyāśabdapravṛttinimittam | āścaryatvaṃ ca vismayanīyatvam ; taca vicitrārthasargakaratvāt | vicitraśabdaśca nānāvidhavācī nānāvidhakāryakaratvāt prakṛterapi vismayanīyatvam | satyeṣu asatyeṣvapi māyāśabdaprayogaviṣayeṣu āścaryatvaṃ vidyate iti, prakṛteḥ āścaryatvameva māyāśabdaprayoge nibandhanamiti bhāvaḥ (śru pra ) |

.

māyitvamiti | 'māyī' iti padenoktaḥ māyāsambandhaḥ, na tattirobhāvyatvam ; api tu taccheṣitvam | jīvasyaiva tu tattirodheyatvam tasyaiva tathātvaśravaṇāt | śvanā- dīti | māyābhibhavaḥ jīvasyaivetyasminnarthe paroktamevedaṃ vacanam ityarthaḥ | śaktaya iti | 'māyābhiḥ' iti bahuvacanāt, anekavicitraśaktyabhidhāyitvam avagamyate | [tasyaiva anuvādo'yam | tadabhimatāyāḥ avidyāyāḥ na tāvadbahutvam ekatvābhyupagamāt iti |

'bhūri tvaṣṭeva rājati'

'na hi mithyābhibhūtaḥ kaścit virājate |
'mama māyā duratyayā'

ityucyate |

ityatrā'pi 'guṇamayī' iti vacanāt saiva triguṇātmikā prakṛtirucyata iti, na śrutibhiḥ sadasadanirvacanīyājñānapratipādanam ||

'anena jīvenātmanā'nupraviśya nāmarūpe vyākaravāṇi' iti sarvasya vastunaḥ paramātmaparyantasyaiva hi 'nāmarūpa' bhāktvamuktam | jñānaparikalpanam ||

ataḥ na brahmā-

iti ślokadvayena 'jagadupalabdhiprakāreṇā'pi vastubhedānām asatyatvam' upapādya, ' tasmānna vijñānamṛte' iti pratijñātaṃ 'brahmavyatiriktasya asatyatvam' upasaṃhṛtya, 'vijñānamekam '

iti 'jñāna' svarūpe' brahmaṇi bhedadarśananimittājñānamūlaṃ nijakarmaiva' iti sphuṭī- kṛtya, 'jñānaṃ viśuddham' iti 'jñānasvarūpasya brahmaṇaḥ svarūpaṃ viśodhya 'sadbhāva " evaṃ " bhavato mayoktaḥ' iti 'jñānasvarūpasya brahmaṇa eva satyatvam, na anyasya ; anyasya ca asatyatvameva | tasya bhuvanādeḥ satyatvaṃ vyāvahārikam' iti tattvaṃ tava upadiṣṭam iti hi upadeśo dṛśyate !

'saṃkṣepaḥ śrūyatām'

ityārabhya abhidhīyate ||

sāmānādhikaraṇyasya 'jyotīṃṣi viṣṇuḥ' ityārabhya vakṣyamāṇasya śarīrātmabhāva eva nibandhanam ' ityāha' |
asmin śāstre pūrvamapi etat asakṛduktam-
'tāni sarvāṇi tadvapuḥ'
'tatsarvaṃ vai harestanuḥ'
' sa eva sarvabhūtātmā viśvarūpo yato'vyayaḥ '

iti | tadidaṃ śarīrātmabhāvāyattaṃ tādātmyaṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyena vyapadiśyate'- 'jyotīṃṣi viṣṇuḥ' iti ||

aśeṣakṣetrajñātmanā avasthitasya bhagavataḥ jñānameva 'svābhāvikaṃ rūpam, na deva- manuṣyādivasturūpam | yata evam tata eva acidrūpadeva ' manuṣya 'śailābdhidharādayaśca tadvijñāna vijṛmbhitāḥ tasya jñānaikākārasya sataḥ devādyākāreṇa 'svātma vaividhyānu- sandhānamūlāḥ – devādyākārānusandhāna' mūlakarmamūlā ḥ ' ityarthaḥ |
yataśca acidvastu kṣetrajñakarmānuguṇapariṇāmāspadam tataḥ tat 'nāsti' śabdā- bhidheyam, itarat 'asti' śabdābhidheyam iti arthāduktaṃ bhavati | tadeva vivṛṇoti 'yadā tu śuddhaṃ nijarūpi' iti |
yadā etat jñānaikākāram ātmavastu devādyākāreṇa svātmani vaividhyānu - sandhānamūla sarva karmakṣayāt, nirdoṣaṃ pariśuddhaṃ nija rūpi bhavati tadā devādyā-

kāreṇaikī kṛtya ātpa'kalpanā' mūlakarmaphalabhūtāḥ 'tadbhogāthāḥ vastuṣu vastubhedāḥ na bhavanti |
ye devādiṣu vastuṣu ātmatayā abhimateṣu bhogyabhūtāḥ deva- manuṣya- śailābdhi- varādivastubhedāḥ, te tanmūlabhūtakarmasu vinaṣṭeṣu na bhavantīti, acidvastunaḥ kādā- citkā' vasthā viśeṣa yogitayā 'nāsti' śabdābhidheyatvam | itarasya sarvadā nijasiddha- 'jñānaikākāratvena' 'asti' śabdābhidheyatvam ityarthaḥ | pratikṣaṇam anyathābhūtatayā kādācitkāvasthāyoginaḥ 'acidvastunaḥ 'nāsti" śabdābhidheyatvameva ityāha ' vastvasti kim ?' iti |
'asti' śabdābhidheyo hi ādi-madhya - paryantahīnaḥ satataikarūpaḥ padārthaḥ, tasya kadācidapi 'nāsti 'buddhayana rha tvāt | acidvastu kiñcit kacidapi tathābhūtaṃ na dṛṣṭa- caram | tataḥ kim 1 ityatrāha -

|

' yaccānyathātvam'

iti | yadvastu pratikṣaṇam anyathātvaṃ yāti tat uttarottarāvasthāprāptyā pūrvapūrvāvasthāṃ jahāti iti, tasya pūrvāvasthasya uttarāvasthāyāṃ na pratisandhāna- masti | ataḥ sarvadā tasya nāstiśabdābhidheyatvameva | tathā hi upalabhyate ityāha 'mahīghaṭatvam' iti |
svakarmaṇā deva- manuṣyādibhāvena 'stimitā' tmaniścayaiḥ svabhogyabhūtam acidvastu pratikṣaṇam anyathābhūtam ālakṣyate - anubhūyate ityarthaḥ | evaṃ sati kimapi aci- dvastu asti śabdārham ādi-madhya- paryantahīnaṃ satataikarūpam ālakṣitam asti kim 1 nāsti ityabhiprāyaḥ |
yasmāt evam, tasmāt jñānasvarūpātmavyatiriktam acidvastu kadācit kvacit kevalāstiśabdavācyaṃ na bhavati ityāha ' tasmānna vijñānamṛte' iti |

ātmā tu sarvatra jñānaikākāratayā devādibheda pratyanīkasvarūpo'pi devādiśarīra- praveśahetubhūtasvakṛta vividhakarma mūladevādi bhedabhinnātmabuddhibhiḥ 'tena tena' rūpeṇa bahudhā anusaṃhitaḥ iti, tat bhedānusandhānaṃ na ātmasvarūpaprayuktam ityāha 'vijñānamekam' iti |
ātmasvarūpaṃ tu karmarahitam, tata eva malarūpaprakṛtisparśa rahitam | tataśca tatprayuktaśoka- 'moha-lobhā' dyaśeṣaheyaguṇāsaṅgi, upacayāpacayānarhatayā ekam tata eva sadā ekarūpam | tacca 'vāsudeva' śarīram iti tadātmakam

atadātmakasya kasya-

cidapi abhāvādityāha 'jñānaṃ viśuddham' iti |

cidacidātmakaṃ jagat vāsudevaśarīraṃ tadātmakam iti, jagadyāthātmyaṃ samyaguktam ityāha 'sadbhāva evam' iti |
atra 'satyam, asatyam' iti, 'yadasti, yannāsti' iti 'prakrāntasya ' upasaṃhāraḥ | etat ' jñānai kākāratayā samam aśabdagocarasvarūpabhedameva acinmiśraṃ bhuvanāśritaṃ devamanuṣyādirūpeṇa samyagvyavahārārha bhedaṃ yat vartate, tatra hetuḥ karmaiva ityu- ktam ityāha-

'etattu yat'
iti | tadeva vivṛṇoti -

'yajñaḥ paśuḥ'

iti |

'jagadyāthātmyajñāna' prayojanam - mokṣopāyayatanam ityāha-

' yacaitat'

iti ||

atra nirviśeṣe pare brahmaṇi, tadāśraye sadasadanirvacanīye ca ajñāne, jagataḥ tatkalpitatve 'ca' anuguṇaṃ kiñcidapi padaṃ na dṛśyate |
'asti - nāsti' śabdābhidheyaṃ cidacidātmakaṃ kṛtsnaṃ jagat, paramasya pareśasya
parasya brahmaṇaḥ viṣṇoḥ kāyatvena tadātmakam |

jñānaikākārasya ātmanaḥ devādi-

vividhākārānubhave, acitpariṇāme ca hetuḥ -- vastuyāthātmyajñānavirodhi kṣetrajñānāṃ karmaiva, iti pratipādanāt asti nāsti satyāsatyaśabdānāṃ ca sadasadanirvacanīya- vastvamidhānāsāmarthyācca nāstya satyaśabdau asti satyaśabda virodhinau | ataśca tābhyām 'asacaṃ hi 'pratīyate', na anirvacanīyatvam |

atra ca acidvastuni nāstyastyaśabdau na tucchatva- midhyātvaparau prayuktau | api tu vināśitvaparau | 'vastvasti kim', 'mahī ghaṭatvam' ityatrā'pi vinā- śitvameva hi upapāditam ; na niṣpramāṇakatvam jñānabādhyatvaṃ vā ekenākāreṇa ekasmin kāle anubhūtasya kālāntare pariṇāmaviśeṣeṇa anyathā upalabdhyā nāstitvopa- pādanāt I tucchatvaṃ hi pramāṇasambandhānarhatvam | bādho'pi yaddeśa' kālādi-

sambandhitayā yat 'asti' iti upalabdham, tasya tadeśa - kālādisambandhitayā 'nāsti' iti upalabdhiḥ ; na tu kālāntare anubhūtasya kālāntare pariṇāmādinā 'nā'sti' iti upalabdhiḥ ; kālabhedena virodhābhāvāt | ataḥ na mithyātvam |
etaduktaṃ bhavati - jñānasvarūpam ātmavastu ādi-madhya- paryanta' hīnaṃ' satataika- ' svarūpamiti, svata eva sadā 'asti' śabdavācyam | acetanaṃ tu kṣetrajñabhogyabhūtaṃ ' tatkarmā' nuguṇa' pariṇāmi vināśi' iti, sarvadā nāstyarthagarbhamiti nāstyasatyaśabdā- bhidheyam - iti |

yathoktam—

'yattu kālāntareṇā'pi nānyasaṃjñāmupaiti vai |
pariṇāmādisambhūtāṃ tadvastu nṛpa tacca kim ||'
pariṇāmādineti | 'pariṇāma' śabdaḥ pariṇāmasāmānyaparaḥ, tadviśeṣaparaśca | yathā 'vāda' - śabdaḥ sarvakathāsādhāraṇaḥ, vītarāgakathāmātraparaśca tadvat | atra ayaṃ 'pariṇāma' śabdaḥ vardha- mānāvasthāvācī, 'pariṇāmardhijanmabhiḥ' iti prayogāt | kālabhedeneti | kālāntarānubhūtasya kālāntare nāstītyupalabdhiḥ kathaṃ na bādhaḥ ? ityasyo- taramidam | virodhe hi bādhaḥ ityarthaḥ | ata iti | bādhyatvaṃ hi mithyātvamitya-

bhiprāyaḥ |

'anāśī paramārthaśca prājñairabhyupagamyate |
tattu 'nā'sti' na sandeho nāśidravyopapāditam ||'
iti | deśa-kāla- karmaviśeṣāpekṣayā astitva - 'nāstitvayogini vastuni kevalāsti buddhibodhyatvam aparamārthaḥ ityuktam | ānana eva kevalāstibuddhibodhyatvamiti saḥ 'paramārthaḥ' ityuktam ||

tavamasyādivākyeṣu sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ na nirviśeṣavastvaikyaparam ; 'tat tvam'- padayoḥ saviśeṣatrahmābhidhāyitvāt | tatpadaṃ hi sarvajñaṃ satyasaṅkalpaṃ jagatkāraṇaṃ brahma 'parāmṛśati',

'tadaikṣata bahu syām'
ityādiṣu tasyaiva prakṛtatvāt |

tatsamānādhikaraṇaṃ 'tvaṃ' padaṃ ca 'acidviśiṣṭa jīvaśarīrakaṃ brahma' prati- pādayati ; prakāradvayāvasthitaikavastuparatvāt sāmānādhikaraṇyasya | prakāradvayaparityāge pravṛttinimittabhedāsambhavena sāmānādhikaraṇyameva parityaktaṃ syāt, dvayoḥ padayoḥ lakṣaṇā ca |

'so'yaṃ devadattaḥ' ityatrāpi na lakṣaṇā ; bhūta vartamānakālasambandhitayā aikyapratītya virodhāt | deśabhedavirodhaca kālabhedena 'parihṛta': |

na; 'prāk adhiṣṭhānā' prakāśe tadāśrayabhrama-bādhayoḥ asaṃbhavāt |

"bhramāśrayam adhiṣṭhānam atirohitam - - iti cet,
tadeva adhiṣṭhānasvarūpaṃ bhramavirodhi, iti tatprakāśe sutarāṃ na tadāśrayaśrama - bādhau | ataḥ adhiṣṭhānātireki pāramārthikadharma- tattirodhānānabhyupagame bhrānti- bādhau durupapādau | adhiṣṭhāne hi puruṣamātrākāre pratīyamāne tadati rekiṇi pāramārthike rājatve tirohite satyeva vyādhatvabhramaḥ | rājatvopadeśena ca tannivṛttirbhavati, na adhiṣṭhānamātropadeśena; tasya prakāśamānatvena anupadeśyatvāt, bhramānupamaditvācca ||

viśiṣṭaikavastupratipādanena sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ ca siddham | nirastanikhiladoṣasya samasta- kalyāṇaguṇātmakasya brahmaṇaḥ jīvāntaryāmitvamapi aiśvaryam aparaṃ pratipāditaṃ bhavati, upakramānukūlatā ca | eka vijñānena sarvavijñānapratijñopapattiśca ; sūkṣmacidacidvastu- śarīrasyaiva brahmaṇaḥ sthūlacidacidvastuśarīratvena kāryatvāt |
'tamīśvarāṇāṃ paramaṃ maheśvaram '

'parāssya śaktirvividhaiva śrūyate'

satyakāmaiḥ satyasaṅkalpaḥ'

1 sāmānādhi-

kevalabhedavādināṃ ca - atyantabhinnayoḥ kenā'pi prakāreṇa aikyāsaṃbhavādeva brahmātmabhāvopadeśāḥ ' na sambhavanti' iti, sarvavedāntaparityāgaḥ syāt ||

karaṇyaṃ sarvatra anugatam iti, prakāratvameva sāmānādhikaraṇyanibandhanam na paraspara- vyāvṛttāḥ jātyādayaḥ |
svaniṣṭānāmeva hi dravyāṇāṃ kadācit kvacit dravyaviśeṣaṇatve matvarthīyapratyayo dṛṣṭa: 'daṇḍī, kuṇḍalī' 'iti', na pṛthakpratipatti-sthityanarhāṇāṃ dravyāṇām | teṣāṃ viśeṣaṇatvaṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyāvaseya meva ||

avagamyate | ātmaikaprayojanatvaṃ ca - ' tatkarma phalabhogārthatayaiva sadbhāvāt | tatprakāra- tvamapi 'devaḥ manuṣyaḥ' iti ātmaviśeṣaṇatayaiva pratīteḥ |

etadeva hi gavādiśabdānāṃ vyaktiparyantatve hetuḥ | 'daṇḍa-kuṇḍalādīnāṃ viśeṣaṇatve 'daṇḍī, kuṇḍalī' iti matvarthīyapratyayaḥ | deva- manuṣyādipiṇḍānām ātmai kāśrayatva- tadekaprayojanatva- tatprakāratvasvabhāvāt 'devaḥ manuṣyaḥ ātmā' iti loka - vedayoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyena vyavahāraḥ | jāti vyaktyoḥ niyamena saha pratītiḥ ; ubhayoścākṣuṣatvāt | ātmanastu acākṣuṣatvāt cakṣuṣā śarīra- grahaṇavelāyām ātmā na gṛhyate |
pṛthagrahaṇayogyasya prakāra taika' svarūpatvaṃ durghaṭam -

etatsvabhāvavirahādeva,

iti māvocaḥ jātyādivat tadekāśrayatvaṃ tadekaprayojanatva- tadviśeṣaṇatvaiḥ śarīra- syā'pi tatprakārataikasvabhāvatvāvagamāt | sahopalambhaniyamastu ekasāmagrīvedyatvanibandhanaḥ
