pratīteriti pañcamīnirdeśaḥ aupa-

cārikaḥ |

niyamena saha

yathā - cakṣuṣā pṛthivyādeḥ gandharasādisambandhitvaṃ svābhāvikamapi na gṛhyate, evam cakṣuṣā gṛhyamāṇaṃ śarīram ātmaprakārataikasvabhāvamapi na tathā gṛhyate; ātmagrahaṇe cakṣuṣaḥ sāmarthyābhāvāt |
na etāvatā śarīrasya 'tatprakāratva' svabhāvavirahaḥ ; tatprakārataikasvabhāvatvameva sāmānādhikaraṇyanibandhanam | ātma prakāratayā pratipādana samarthastu śabdaḥ sava prakāratayā pratipādayati ||

ityuktam |

ataḥ gavādiśabdavat devamanuṣyādiśabdāḥ ātmaparyantāḥ ||

atredaṃ tattvam – acidvastunaḥ cidvastunaḥ 'parasya ca' brahmaṇaḥ bhogyatvena bhoktṛ- tvena ca ' īśitvena ca svarūpavivekamāhuḥ kāścana śrutayaḥ -
'asmānmāyī sṛjate viśvametat tasmiṃścānyo māyayā saṃniruddha:' 'māyāṃ tu prakṛtiṃ vidyāt māyinaṃ tu maheśvaram'
'kṣaraṃ pradhānamamṛtākṣaraṃ haraḥ kṣarātmānāvīśate deva ekaḥ' 'pradhānam ātmanaḥ bhogyatvena
'amṛtākṣaraṃ haraḥ' iti bhoktā nirdiśyate | haratīti - haraḥ |
'sa kāraṇaṃ karaṇādhipādhipo na cāsya kaśvijanitā na cādhipaḥ' 'pradhānakṣetrajñapatirguṇeśaḥ'
'patiṃ viśvasyātmeśvaraṃ śāśvataṃ śivamacyutam '
'jñājñau dvāvajāvīśanīśa'

'nityo nityānāṃ cetanaścetanānāmeko bahūnāṃ yo vidadhāti kāmān'

'bhoktā bhogyaṃ preritāraṃ ca matvā'

' tayoranya: pippalaṃ svādvatyanaśnannanyo abhicākaśīti' 'pṛthagātmānaṃ preritāraṃ ca matvā juṣṭastatastenā'mṛtatvameti' 'ajāmekāṃ lohitaśuklakṛṣṇāṃ bahrīṃ prajāṃ janayantīṃ sarūpām |
ajo ko juṣamāṇo'nuśete jahātyenāṃ bhuktabhogāmajo'nyaḥ || '
'samāne vṛkṣe puruṣo nimagno'nīśayā śocati muhyamānaḥ |
juṣṭaṃ yadā paśyatyanyamīśamasya mahimānamiti vītaśokaḥ ||' ityādyāḥ | smṛtāvapi -
'ahaṅkāra itīyaṃ me bhinnā prakṛtiraṣṭadhā'
'apareyamitastvanyāṃ prakṛtiṃ viddhi me parām 1
jīvabhūtāṃ mahābāho yayedaṃ dhāryate jagat ||'
'sarvabhūtāni kaunteya ! prakṛtiṃ yānti māmikām |
kalpakṣaye punastāni kalpādau visṛjāmyaham ||

'prakṛtiṃ svāmavaṣṭabhya visṛjāmi punaḥ punaḥ | bhūtagrāmamimaṃ kṛtsnamavaśaṃ prakṛtervaśāt ||' 'mayāsdhyakṣeṇa prakṛtiḥ sūyate sacarācaram |
hetunā'nena kaunteya 'jagaddhi parivartate ||'
'prakṛtiṃ puruṣaṃ caiva viddhyanādī ubhāvapi ' 'mama yonirmahna tasmin garbha dadhāmyaham |
saṃbhavaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ tato bhavati bhārata ! |'

9-8)

iti | jagadyonibhūtaṃ mahadrā madīyaṃ prakṛtyākhyaṃ bhūtasūkṣmam acidvastu yat, tasmin cetanākhyaṃ garbha yat saṃyojayāmi, 'tataḥ ' 'matkṛtāt' cidacitsaṃsargāt devādisthā- varāntānām acinmiśrāṇāṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ sambhavaḥ bhavati ityarthaḥ |
evaṃ bhoktṛbhogya' rūpeṇa' ' avasthitayoḥ ' sarvāvasthāvasthitayoḥ cidacitoḥ parama- puruṣaśarīratayā tanniyāmyatvena tadapṛthaksthitim paramapuruṣasya ca ātmatvamāhuḥ

kāścana śrutayaḥ -

'yaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhan pṛthivyā antaro yaṃ pṛthivī na veda, yasya pṛthivī śarīraṃ, yaḥ pṛthivīmantaro yamayati'

ityārabhya,

'ya ātmani tiṣṭhan ātmano'ntaraḥ yamātmā na veda, yasyātmā śarīraṃ, ya ātmānantaro yamayati sa ta ātmā'ntaryāmyamṛtaḥ'

iti |

tathā -

'yaḥ pṛthivīmantare sañcaran yasya pṛthivī śarīraṃ yaṃ pṛthivī na beda' ityārabhya,
'yo'kṣaramantare sañcaran yasyākṣaraṃ śarīraṃ yamakṣaraṃ na veda, yo mṛtyumantare saṃcaran yasya mṛtyuḥ śarīraṃ yaṃ mṛtyurna veda eṣa sarvabhūtāntarātpahatapāpmā divyo deva eko nārāyaṇaḥ'
atra mṛtyuśabdena 'tama' śabdavācyaṃ sūkṣmāvastham acidvastu abhidhīyate asyāmeva upaniṣadi -
'avyaktamakṣare 'līyate', akṣaraṃ tamasi 'līyate"

iti vacanāt,

'antaḥ praviṣṭaḥ śāstā janānāṃ sarvātmā'

iti ca ||

evam sarvāvasthāvasthitacidacidvastuśarīratayā tatprakāraḥ paramapuruṣa eva kāryāvastha- kāraṇāvasthajagadrūpeṇa avasthitaḥ iti imamartha jñāpayituṃ kāścana śrutayaḥ ' kāryāvasthaṃ kāraṇāvasthaṃ 'ca jagat' sa eva' ityāhu:-
'sadeva somyedamagra āsīdekamevādvitīyam'
' tadaikṣata bahu syāṃ prajāyeyeti, tattejo'sṛjata' ityārabhya,
'sanmūlā' somyamāḥ sarvāḥ prajāḥ sadāyatanāḥ satpratiṣṭhāḥ' 'aitadātmyamidaṃ sarvaṃ tat satyaṃ sa ātmā, tatvamasi śvetaketo' iti | tathā-

'so'kāmayata | bahu syāṃ pranāyeyeti | idaṃ sarvamasṛjata | '

sa tapo'tapyata | sa tapastatvā |

ityārabhya,

'satyaṃ cānṛtaṃ ca satyamabhavat'

atrāpi śrutyantarasiddha: cidacitoḥ paramapuruṣasya ca svarūpavivekaḥ

ityādyāḥ |
smārita:-

'hantā'hamimāstisro devatā anena jīvenā''tmanā'nupraviśya nāmarūpe vyākara- vāṇi'

iti,

kāraṇāt kāryasya ananyatvena kāraṇavijñānena kāryasya 'jñātatayā', ekavijñānena 'sarvavijñānaṃ' samīhitam upapannataram 1
'ahamimāstisro devatā anena jīvenā''tmanā'nupraviśya nāmarūpe vyākaravāṇi'
iti, 'tisro devatā:' iti sarvam acidvastu nirdiśya tatra svātmakajīvā nupraveśena nāmarūpavyākaraṇavacanāt sarve vācakāḥ śabdāḥ 'acidviśiṣṭajīva 'viśiṣṭa paramātmana eva vācakāḥ iti, kāraṇāvasthaparamātmavācinā śabdena kāryavācinaḥ śabdasya sāmānā- dhikaraṇyaṃ mukhyavṛttam | ataḥ sthūlasūkṣmacida 'citprakārakaṃ brahmaiva kārya kāraṇaṃ ceti brahmopādānaṃ jagat ||

upādānatvena cidacitoḥ brahmaṇaśca svabhāvā saṅkaro'pi upapannataraḥ | yathā- śukla-kṛṣṇa-raktatantusaṃghātopādānatve'pi citrapaṭasya tattattantupradeśe evaṃ śaukkayādi- sambandhaḥ iti, kāryāvasthāyāmapi na sarvatra varṇasaṅkaraḥ; tathā - cidacidīśvara saṅghāto- pādānatve'pi jagataḥ kāryāvasthāyāmapi bhoktṛtva-bhogyatva- niyantṛtvādyasaṅkaraḥ | tantūnāṃ pṛthaksthitiyogyānāmetra puruṣecchayā kadācit saṃhatānāṃ kāraṇatvaṃ kāryatvañca | iha tu 'cidacitoḥ sarvāvasthayoḥ paramapuruṣaśarīratvena tatprakāratayaiva padārthatvāt tatprakāra: paramapuruṣaḥ sarvadā sarva śabdavācyaḥ iti viśeṣaḥ | svabhāvabhedaḥ tadasaṅkaraśca tatra ca

atra ca tulyaḥ ||

nirguṇavādāśca parasya brahmaṇaḥ 'heyaguṇāsambandhāt' upapadyante |
'apahatapāpmā vijaro vimṛtyurviśoko vijighatso'pipāsaḥ' iti heyaguṇān pratiṣidhya,
'satyakāmaḥ satyasaṃkalpa:'

iti kalyāṇaguṇān vidadhatī iyaṃ śrutireva anyatra sāmānyenāvagataṃ guṇaniṣedham, heya- guṇaviṣayaṃ vyavasthāpayati |
'jñānasvarūpaṃ brahma' iti vādaśva – sarvajñasya sarvaśakteḥ 'nikhila' heyapratyanīka- kalyaṇaguṇākarasya brahmaṇaḥ svarūpaṃ 'jñānaika' nirūpaṇīyam, 'svayaṃprakāśa' tathā jñānasvarūpaṃ ca ityabhyupagamāt upapannataraḥ |

'yaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvavit'
'parā'sya śaktirvividhaiva śrūyate svābhāvikī jñānabalakriyā ca' 'vijñātāramare keṉa vijānīyāt 1'

ityādikāḥ jñātṛtvam āvedayanti ;
'satyaṃ jñānam '

ityādikāśca jñānaikanirūpaṇīyatayā svaprakāśatayā ca jñānasvarūpatām ||

ityādiśrutisiddhaṃ svasaṅkalpakṛtaṃ brahmaṇaḥ nānānāmarūpabhātvena nānāprakāratvamapi niṣidhyate |
'yatra tvasya sarvamātmaivābhūt'
'ityādi' niṣedhavākyādau ca tat sthāpitam,
'sarva taṃ parādāt yo'nyatrā''tmanaḥ sarvaṃ veda'
'tasya ha vā etasya mahato bhūtasya niśvasitametat 'yahagvedo yajurvedaḥ "

ityādinā ||

bhireva jñāyate iti, brahmājñānavādasya, aupādhikabrahmabhedavādasya, anyasyā'pi apanyāya- mūlasya sakalaśrutiviruddhasya na kathañcidapi avakāśo dṛśyate |
cidacidīśvarāṇāṃ pṛthaksvabhāvatayā tattacchrutisiddhānāṃ śarīrātmabhāvena prakāra- prakāritayā śrutibhireva 'pratipannānāṃ' 'śrutyantareṇa' kāryakāraṇabhāvapratipādanam, kārya- kāraṇayoḥ aikyapratipādanaṃ ca hi aviruddham |

yathā - āgneyādīn ṣaṭ yāgān, utpattivākyaiḥ pṛthagutpannān samudāyānu- 'vādi' vākyadvayena samudāyadvayatvamāpannān,
' darśapūrṇamāsābhyām '
iti adhikāra' vākyaṃ kāminaḥ kartavyatayā vidadhāti tathā cidacidīśvarān vivikta- svarūpasvabhāvān,
'kṣaraṃ pradhānamamṛtākṣaraṃ haraḥ kṣarātmānāvīśate deva ekaḥ "
'patiṃ viśvasyāsstmeśvaram '

'ātmā nārāyaṇaḥ paraḥ'
ityādivākyaiḥ 'pṛthaka' pratipādya,

bhidhāne hi nā'sti virodha: : yathā manuṣyapiṇḍaśārīrakasya ātma viśeṣasya

'ayamātmā sukhī' iti ātmaśabdenāsbhidhāne', ityalamativistareṇa ||

'mithyaitadanyadravyaṃ hi naiti tadravyatāṃ yataḥ'

iti śāstrāt,

'uttamaḥ puruṣastvanyaḥ'
'pṛthagātmānaṃ preritāraṃ ca matvā'
iti, jīvātmavisajātīyasya tadantaryāmiṇaḥ brahmaṇaḥ jñānam, paramapuruṣārthalakṣaṇamokṣa- sādhanam ityupadeśācca |

api ca 'bhavadabhimatasyā'pi nivartakajñānasya' mithyā' rūpatvāt tasya nivarta- kāntaraṃ mṛgyam |
nivartakajñānamidam –khavirodhi sarvaṃ 'bhedajātaṃ ' 'nivartya', kṣaṇikatvāt svaya- meva ' naśyati' - iti cet,
na; tatsvarūpa tadutpatti vināśānāṃ kālpanikatvena 'vināśa - tatkalpanā kalpaka- rūpāvidyāyāḥ nivartakāntaram anveṣaṇīyam |

tadvināśaḥ brahmasvarūpameva iti cet,

tathāsati - nivartakajñānotpattireva na syāt tadvināśe tiṣṭhati, tadutpatya-

sambhavāt ||

brahmasvarūpasyaitra jñātṛtve – asmadīya eva pakṣaḥ parigṛhītaḥ syāt I
nivartakajñānasvarūpam, 'svasya' jñātā ca brahmavyatiriktatvena svanivartyāntargatam - iti vacanam, 'bhūtalavyatiriktaṃ kṛtsnaṃ devadattena chinnam ' ' ityasyāmeva' chedanakriyā- yām asya chettuḥ asyāḥ chedanakriyāyāśca chedyānupraveśavacanavat upahāsyam | adhyasta ḥ jñātā svanāśahetubhūtanivartakajñāne svayaṃ kartā ca na bhavati ; svanāśasya 'apuruṣārthatvāt' | tannāśasya brahmasvarūpatvāmyupagame bheda 'darśana'- tanmūlā vidyādīnāṃ 'kalpanameva' na syāt ! iti, alamanena diṣṭahatamudgarābhighātena ||

jñānādeva | tadutpattiśva aharaharanuṣṭhīyamānaparamapuruṣārādhanaveṣātma' yāthātmya' buddhiviśeṣa- saṃskṛtavarṇāśramocitakarmalabhyā |

tatra kevalakarmaṇām – alpāsthiraphalatvam, anamisaṃhitaphalaparamapuruṣārādhana- veṣāṇāṃ karmaṇām – upāsanātmakajñānotpattidvāreṇa brahmayāthātmyānubhavarūpānantasthira- phalatvaṃ ca karmasvarūpajñānāt ṛte na jñāyate, kevalākāraparityāga pūrvakayathoktasvarūpa- karmopādānaṃ ca na sambhavati iti karmavicārānantaram, tata eva hetoḥ brahmavicāraḥ kartavyaḥ iti 'athā'taḥ' ityuktam ||

iti mahāsiddhāntaḥ —

atha sūtrakārābhimatamutrārtha' yojanārambhaḥ

'tatra' pūrvapakṣavādī manyate - vṛddhavyavahārādanyatra śabdasya bodhakatvaśaktyavadhāraṇā- 'vyavahārasya ca ' ' kāryabuddhipūrvakatvena' kāryārthe eva śabdasya prāmāṇyamiti, 'kāryarūpa eva vedārthaḥ | ataḥ na vedāntāḥ pariniṣpanne 'pare' brahmaṇi pramāṇa- bhāvam anubhavitumarhanti ||

sambhavāt,

'na ca ' putrajanmādisiddhavastuviṣayavākyeṣu – harṣahetūnāṃ kālatrayavartinām arthā- nāmānantyāt,' sulana' - sukhaprasavā diharṣahetvarthāntaro panipātasaṃbhāvanayā ca priyārthaprati- pattinimittamukha'vikāsā' diliṅgena arthaviśeṣabuddhihetutvaniścayaḥ ||
tatra - prapañcasatyatve sthite sati | tatra ityasya adhikaraṇāntasthena 'sthitam ' iti padenā'nvayaḥ | prapañcasatyatve sthite sati, mṛṣāvādyuktādhikaraṇaśarīrāyogāt evamadhi - karaṇaśarīraṃ sthitamityarthaḥ | pūrvapakṣavādī - prābhākaraḥ, codanālakṣaṇa evārthaḥ vedapratipādyaḥ | codanā ca kriyāyāṃ pravartakaṃ vacanamiti jaiminimatīyo vā | tayoḥ vyavahāraḥ – vācikaḥ, kāyikaśca | kāryarūpa-

vṛddha:-

vyutpannaḥ, prayojyaḥ, prayojakaśca |

nā'pi 'vyutpannetarapada' vibhaktyarthasya padāntarārtha niścayena', prakṛtyarthaniścayena vā śabdasya siddhavastuni abhidhānaśaktiniścayaḥ ; jñātakāryābhidhāyipadasamudāyasya tadaṃśa- viśeṣaniścayarūpatvāt tasya ||

kāryabuddhi pravṛttivyāptibalena śabdasya pravartakārthāvabodhitvam 'upagatam' iti, sarvapadānāṃ kāryaparatvena, sarvaiḥ padaiḥ kāryasyaiva viśiṣṭasya pratipādanāt, 'na anyānvita - svārthamātre padaśaktiniścayaḥ ||

ataḥ kāryabuddhireva pravṛttihetuḥ iti, pravartakasyaiva śabda vācyatayā' kāryasyai vedavedyatvāt, pariniṣpannarūpabrahmaprāptilakṣaṇānantasthiraphalā pratipatteḥ ",
'akṣayya vai cāturmāsyayājinaḥ sukṛtaṃ bhavati'
ityādibhiḥ karmaṇāmeva sthiraphalatva' pratipādanācca karmaphalālpāsthiratva-brahmajñāna- phalānantasthiratvajñānahetukaḥ brahmavicārārambhaḥ na yuktaḥ iti ||

evaṃ kila !

bālāḥ śabdārthasambandham avadhārayanti - ' mātā- pitṛ' prabhṛtibhiḥ ambā tāta mātulādīn śaśi-paśu-nara- mṛgapakṣi- sarpādīṃśca 'enam avehi, imaṃ ca avadhāraya' ityabhiprāyeṇa 'aṅgulyā' nirdiśya nirdiśya taistaiḥ śabdaiḥ teṣu teṣvartheṣu bahuśaḥ śikṣitāḥ, śanaiśanaiḥ taistaireva śabdaiḥ' teṣu teṣvartheṣu 'svātmanāṃ' buddhayatpattiṃ dṛṣṭvā, śabdārthayoḥ sambandhāntarādarśanāt saṃketayitṛpuruṣājñānācca teṣvartheṣu teṣāṃ śabdānāṃ prayogaḥ bodhakatvanibandhanaḥ - iti niścinvanti |

punaśca vyutpannetaraśabdeṣu " asya śabdasya ayam arthaḥ' iti pūrvavaddhaiḥ

'śikṣitāḥ sarvaśabdānāmartha mavagamya, parapratyāyanāya tattadarthāvabodhi vākyajātaṃ prayuñjate ||

kāryārthatve'pi vedasya brahmavicāraḥ kartavya eva |

ityādibhiḥ brahmaprāptiḥ śrūyate iti brahmasvarūpa tadviśeṣaṇānāṃ duḥkhāsambhinna' deśaviśeṣa' - rūpasvargādivat, rātrisatra pratiṣṭhādivat apagoraṇa - śatayā tanāsādhya-sādhanabhāvavacca kāryopayogitayaiva siddheḥ ||

kṛtibhāvabhāvi kṛtyuddeśyaṃ hi bhavataḥ kāryam |

'kṛtyuddeśyatvaṃ ca ' kṛtikarmatvam | kṛtikarmatvaṃ ca kṛtyā prāptum iṣṭatamatvam | iṣṭatamaṃ ca sukham vartamāna duḥkhasya tanivṛttirvā | tatra iṣṭa sukhādinā puruṣeṇa svaprayatnādṛte yadi tadasiddhiḥ pratītā, tataḥ prayatnecchuḥ pravartate puruṣaḥ iti, na kvacidapi icchāviṣayasya kṛtyadhīna siddhitvamantareṇa, kṛtyuddeśyatvaṃ nāma kiñcidapi upalabhyate |
icchāviṣayasya prerakatvaṃ ca prayatnādhīna siddhitvameva ; tata eva pravṛtteḥ |
na ca puruṣānukūlatvaṃ kṛtyuddeśyatvam ; yataḥ sukhameva puruṣānukūlam 1

na ca duḥkhanivṛtteḥ puruṣānukūlatvam ; 'puruṣānukūlaṃ sukham, tatpratikūlaṃ duḥkham' 'iti hi' sukhaduḥkhayoḥ svarūpavivekaḥ | duḥkhasya pratikūlatayā tannivṛttiḥ iṣṭā bhavati, na anukūlatayā | | anukūla pratikūlānvaya' virahe svarūpeṇa avasthitirhi duḥkhanivṛttiḥ | ataḥ sukhavyatiriktasya kriyādeḥ anukūlatvaṃ na sambhavati | na sukhārthatayā tasyāpyanukūlatvam duḥkhātmakatvāt tasya | 'sukhārtha' tayā'pi tadu- pādānecchā mātrameva bhavati |

māṇatvāt pradhānasyā'pi bhṛtyoddeśapravṛtyarhatvadarśanācca |

pradhānastu bhṛtyapoṣaṇe'pi 'khoddeśena' pravartate iti cet, na; bhṛtyo'pi hi pradhāna poṣaṇe svoddeśenaiva pravartate |

kāryasvarūpasyaiva anirūpaṇāt, kāryapratisambandhī śeṣaḥ, tatpratisambandhī śeṣī- ityapi asaṅgatam |
mā'pi kutiprayojanatvaṃ kṛtyuddeśyatvam ; puruṣasya kṛtyārambhaprayojanameva hi kṛtiprayojanam | sa ca icchā viṣayaḥ |
tasmāt 'iṣṭatvātirekikṛtyuddeśya sva/ nirūpaṇāt, kṛtisādhyatākṛtiśvānatva' rūpaṃ ' kārya durnirūpameva ||

niyogasyā'pi sākṣā'diṣiviṣaya' bhūtasukhaduḥkha nivṛttibhyām anyatvāt, tatsā: dhanatayaiva iṣṭatvaṃ kṛtisādhyatvaṃ ca | ata eva hi tasya kriyātiriktatā | anyathā kriyaiva kārya syāt | svargakāmapadasamabhivyāhārā|nuguṇyena 'liṅādivācyaṃ' kārya svargasādhanameveti, kṣaṇabhaṅgikama tireki sthiraṃ 'svargasādhanam' apūrvameva kāryam iti, svargasādhama toralenaiva hi apūrvavyutpattiḥ |
ataḥ prathamam ananyārthatayā pratipannasya kāryasya ananyārthatvanirvahaṇāya apūrvameva paścāt svargasādhanaṃ bhavatīti upahāsyam ; svargakāmapada (nvitakāryābhidhāyipadena
iṣiḥ - icchā | niyogasyā'pi ... anyatvāditi | atra svata iṣṭatvam — icchā viṣaya bhūtārthāntarasādhanatvaviraheṇa iṣṭatvaṃ vivakṣitam |
samānam | svata iṣṭatvaṃ tatprayuktakṛtisādhyatvaṃ ca tayoreva |

tat sukhasya duḥkhanivṛtteśca
tasmāt dve api niyogasya na.
kriyaiva — dhātvartha eva |

sidhyataḥ ityarthaḥ | anyathā - sādhanatvena siddhyanaṅgīkāre |

ataḥ -- svargakāmapadānvayātpūrvaṃ dhātvartha pātrasyaiva kāryatā pratipatteḥ, tadanvaye tatsā -

'prathamamapi' ananyārthatānabhidhānāt 'ananyārthasya ' kṛtisādhyatāpratītyanupapatteśca ||

sukha-duḥkhanivṛtti - tatsādhanebhyaḥ anyasya

śāstreṇa niyogasya puruṣārthatayā pratipādanāt 'paścāttu' bhokṣyate iti cet,
kiṃ tat niyogasya puruṣārthatvavāci śāstram 1
na tāvat laukikaṃ vākyam

tasya duḥkhātmakakriyāviṣayatvāt | tena

'sukhādisādhanatayaiva kṛtisādhyatāmātrapratipādanāt |
nā'pi vaidikam tenā'pi 'svargādi' sādhanatayaiva kāryasya pratipādanāt |

nā'pi nityanaimittikaśāstram tasyā'pi tadabhidhāmitvaṃ svargakāmavākyasthā- pūrvavyutpattipūrvakamiti, uktarītyā tenā'pi mukhādi sādhana' kāryābhidhānam avarja nīyam | niyataihika phalasya karmaṇaḥ anuṣṭhitasya phalatvena tadānīmanubhūya- mānānnā dyāroga 'tādivyatirekeṇa niyogarūpa sukhānubhavānupalabdheśca, 'niyogaḥ sukham ' ityatra na kiṃcana pramāṇam upalabhāmahe |
arthavādādiṣvapi - svargādisukhaprakāra kīrtanavat, niyogarūpa sukhaprakāra kīrtanaṃ bhavatā'pi na dṛṣṭacaram ||

siddhameva liṅādeḥ vācyam - iti 'adhyavasīyate' | agnyādidevatāntaryāmiparamapuruṣasamārādhanarūpatā, samārādhitāt paramapuruṣāt phala-

'dhātvarthasya ca ' yāgādeḥ

siddhiśva - iti,

ataḥ 'kevalānāṃ karmaṇām' alpāsthira phalatvāt brahmajñānasya ca amantasthira- 'phalatvāt tannirṇayaphalaḥ brahmavicārārambhaḥ yuktaḥ iti sthitam || 1 ||

jñānānandādyanantakalyāṇaguṇāt sarvajñāt sarvaśakteḥ paramakāruṇikāt parasmāt puṃsaḥ, sṛṣṭi-sthiti- pralayāḥ pravartante tat brahma - iti sūtrārthaḥ ||

na tāvat janmādayaḥ viśeṣaṇatvena brahma lakṣayanti ; aneka viśeṣaṇavyāvṛttatvena brahmaṇaḥ anekatvaprasakteḥ | 'viśeṣaṇatvaṃ' hi "vyāvartakatvam |
nanu - 'devadattaḥ śyāmo yuvā lohitākṣaḥ samaparimāṇaḥ' ityatra viśeṣaṇabahutve'pi, eka eva devadattaḥ pratīyate; evam atrāpi ekameva brahma bhavati !
'naivam ; tatra pramāṇāntareṇa aikyapratīteḥ, ekasminneva viśeṣaṇānām upasaṃhāraḥ |

śrībhā:

· ]

iti ekasyaiva sacchandavācyasya nimittopādānarūpakāraṇatvena | tadapi "sadeva idam agre' ekamevā''sīt' iti upādānatāṃ pratipādya, 'advitīyam' iti adhiṣṭhātrantaraṃ pratiṣidhya,
' tadaikṣata bahu syāṃ prajāyeyeti | tattejo'sṛjata'
iti ekasyaiva pratipādanāt | tasmāt ' yanmūlā ḥ nagajanma-sthiti-layāḥ, tat brahma' iti, janma-sthiti-layāḥ, 'svanimittopādānabhūtaṃ vastu brahma' iti lakṣayanti ||

ityādi kāraṇa pratipannasya jagañjanmādikāraṇasya brahmaṇaḥ sakaletaravyāvṛttaṃ svarūpamabhidhīyate-

'satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma'

kiṃ ca ghaṭādikāryam, anīśvareṇa alpajñānaśaktinā saśarīreṇa parigrahavatā anāptakāmena 'nirmitaṃ dṛṣṭam' iti, tathāvidhameva cetanaṃ kartāraṃ sādhayan ayaṃ kārya- tvahetuḥ siṣādhayiṣitapuruṣasārvajñya sarvaiśvaryādi viparītasādhanāt viruddhaḥ syāt |

na ca etāvatā sarvānumānocchedaprasaṅgaḥ : liṅgini pramāṇāntaragocare liṅga- balopasthāpitāḥ 'viparīta' viśeṣāḥ tatpramāṇapratihatagatayo nivartante |

niravayava iti | aṅgulyāderiva chedo na sambhavatīti acchedyatvaṃ brahmaṇaḥ, tasya hetu: niravayavatvam | bhicveti | acchinnatve'pi pāṇipādādivat pratiniyatapradeśabhedenetyarthaḥ | pradeśabhedena chedanena vā brahmaṇi sambandham upādhayo nā'hanti; niravayavatvāt, acchedyatvācca- ityarthaḥ ||
yadi manvīteti, gaganavat upahitānupahitāṃśabhedena sadoṣatvanirdoṣatvopapattiśaṅkā | anādiriti upādhyavacchedāt jīvabhāvaḥ, jīvabhāvāt upādhyavacchedaḥ karmaṇā jīvabhāvaḥ, tasmāt karma iti ca anyonyāśrayaparihārāyoktam |

j

ayaṃ praṣṭavya iti | śrutau aṇuśabdasya sūkṣmaparatvābhiprāyasaṃbhavāt tṛtīyakalpodayaḥ | caturthapañcamau kalpau ābhyāṃ vinā gatirnāsti ityabhiprāyeṇa kṛtau; na parābhyupagatyā | brahma- śabdasya jīve'pyupacāravṛtteḥ caturthaḥ kalpaḥ | ādhitrahmaṇoḥ bhinnābhinnatvābhyupagamāt pañcamaḥ |

'dvitīye tu' kalpe - brahaṇa eva pradeśaviśeṣe upādhisambandhāt, aupādhikāḥ sarve doṣāḥ tasyaiva syuḥ | upādhau gacchati, upādhinā svasaṃyuktabrahmapradeśākarṣaṇā- yogāt, anukṣaṇam ' upādhi saṃyuktabrahmapradeśa bhedāt, kṣaṇe kṣaṇe bandha- 'mokṣau ca syātām | ākarṣaṇeca, 'acchinna' tvāt kṛtsnasya brahmaṇaḥ ākarṣaṇa syāt |

niraṃśasya vyāpinaḥ ākarṣaṇa na sambhavati - iti cet,
tarhi upādhireva gacchatīti, pūrvokta eva doṣaḥ syāt | acchinnabrahmapradeśeṣu 'sarvopādhisaṃsarge – 'sarveṣāṃ ca' jīvānāṃ 'brahmaṇa eva' pradeśatvena, 'ekatvena prati- sandhānaṃ syāt | pradeśabhedāt apratisandhāne ca, ekasyā'pi khopādhau " gacchati " pratisandhānaṃ na syāt |
tṛtīye tu kalpe - brahmasvarūpasyaiva upādhisambandhena jīvatvāpātāt tadatiri- ktānupahitabrahmāsiddhiḥ syāt | sarveṣu ca deheṣu eka eva jīvaḥ syāt |
turīye tu kalpe - brahmaṇaḥ anya eva jīva iti, jīvabhedasya aupādhikatvaṃ parityaktaṃ syāt |
carame― cārvākapakṣa eva parigṛhītaḥ syāt ||

ataḥ pravṛtti nivṛtti' prayojanaparatayaiva śāstrasya prāmāṇye'pi dhyānavidhi 'śeṣatayā # vedāntavākyānāṃ brahmasvarūpe prāmāṇyam ' upapannam ' - iti ||

tadapyayuktam ; dhyāna vidhiśeṣatve'pi vedāntavākyānām, arthasatyatve 'prāmāṇyā'-

yogāt |

etaduktaṃ bhavati - brahmakharūpagocarārāṇi vākyāni kiṃ dhyāna vidhinā ekavākyatā- māpannāni brahmasvarūpe prāmāṇyaṃ pratipadyante | uta khatantrāṇyeva 1
ekavākyatve, "dhyāna vidhi' paratvena brahmasvarūpe tātparyaṃ na sambhavati | bhinnavākyatve, pravṛttinivṛtti' prayojanavirahāt anavabodhakatvameva |
na ca vācyam - dhyānaṃ nāma smṛtisantatirūpam | tacca smartavyaikanirūpaṇīyamiti, dhyānavidheḥ smartavya viśeṣākāṃkṣāyām -
'idaṃ sa yadayamātmā' "brahma" sarvānubhūḥ" 'satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma'
tadapyayuktamiti | na kevalaṃ niṣprapañcīkaraṇavidhivāda eṣā'nupapannaḥ, idamapi matam anupapannam iti apiśabdasya bhāvaḥ |
etaduktamiti | dhyāna vidhiśeṣatve katham arthasatyatve tātparyāyogaḥ, vidhiśeṣatvaṃ hitvā svātantryeṇa vā brahmaṇi prāmāṇyaṃ syāt ? ityatrottaram |

ekavākyatvam – mahāvākyaikavākyatvam |
anavabodhakatvam ; duḥkhasādhyaśravaṇādau pravṛttyayogāt |
na ca vācyamiti, ekavākyatvapakṣe vidhiśeṣatayā brahmaṇaḥ siddhimāśaṅkaya | dhyānavidheriti | 'nāma brahmetyupāsīta' ityādiṣu dṛṣṭividhiṣu viśeṣyasatyatve'pi
viśeṣaṇāsatyatvena viśiṣṭāsatyatvamavarjanīyam |

ityādīni brahmasvarūpatadviśeṣādīni samarpayanti | tenaikavākyatāmāpannāni arthasadbhāve pramāṇam - iti;

dhyāna vidheḥ smartavyaviśeṣāpekṣatve'pi,
'nāma 'brahma'

ityādidṛṣṭividhivat asatyenā'pi arthaviśeṣeṇa dhyānanirvṛcyupapatteḥ, dhyeyasatyatvā- napekṣaṇāt ||

ataḥ - vedāntavākyānāṃ pravṛtti - nivṛttiprayojana vidhuratvāt, dhyānavidhiśeṣatve'pi, dhyeya viśeṣasvarūpa samarpaṇamātraparyavasānāt, svātantrye'pi balāturā pacchandanavākyavat jñānamātreṇaiva puruṣārtha paryantatāsiddheśva 'pari' niṣpannavastusatyatāgocaratvābhāvāt brahmaṇaḥ śāstrapramāṇakatvaṃ na saṃbhavati — iti prāptam ||

tatra pratipadyate tattu samanvayāt - iti | samanvayaḥ samyaganvayaḥ, puruṣārthatayā anvayaḥ ityarthaḥ | paramapuruṣārthabhūtasya anavādhikātiśayānandasvarūpasya brahmaṇaḥ abhidheyatayā anvayāt, tat - śāstrapramāṇakatvam, sidhyatyeva ityarthaḥ ||

|

vākyagaṇaḥ pravṛtti - nivṛttiparatāvirahāt na prayojanaparyavasāyī - iti bruvāṇaḥ rāja- kulavāsinaḥ 'puruṣasya' kauleyakakulānanupraveśena prayojanaśūnyatāṃ brūte |
etaduktaṃ bhavati – anādikarmarūpā vidyā veṣṭanatirohitaparāvara tatva yāthātmya svasvarūpā- vabodhānāṃ devāsuragandharvasiddha vidyādhara kiṃnara kiṃpuruṣayakṣa'rākṣasa' piśācamanujapaśuśakunisarī- sṛpavṛkṣagulmalatādūrvādīnāṃ strīpuṃnapuṃsakabhedabhinnānāṃ kṣetrajñānāṃ 'vyavasthitadhārakapoṣaka'- bhogyaviśeṣāṇāṃ muktānāṃ svasya ca aviśeṣeṇa anubhavasambhave svarūparūpa' guṇavibhava- ceṣṭitaiḥ anavādhikātiśayānanda' jananaṃ ' ' paraṃ brahma' asti iti 'bodhayadeva' vākyaṃ prayo- janaparyavasāyi | pravṛttinivṛttiniṣṭhaṃ tu yāvatpuruṣārthānvaya bodhaṃ na prayojanaparyavasāyi ||
evambhūtaṃ paraṃ brahma kathaṃ prāpyate 1 ityapekṣāyām -
rājakulavāsī - rājakulaprasūtaḥ, rājakulāntaraṅgo vā |

kauleyakāḥ- śvānaḥ,

śvopajīvinaḥ, mlecchā vā lakṣyante | prayojanaśūnyatāṃ vrata iti | svato niravadhika puruṣārtha- paravākyasya prayojanaśūnyatvakathanam anupapannamityarthaḥ |

anādikarmeti | karmabhiḥ anubhavapratibandhāt kṣetrajñānāṃ tasya puruṣārthatvajñānābhāvaḥ, na tu tadvaikalyāt ityabhiprāyaḥ | devāsura| diparigaṇanaṃ teṣāṃ vyavasthitabhogyatvapradarśanārtham | anubhavasambhava iti yogyānupalabdhiśaṅkāvyudāsā proktam | tadviṣayajñānānukūlatvaṃ hi tadanu kūlatvena vinā na saṃbhavati | ataḥ anavadhikā tiśayānandajananatvoktyā brahmaṇaḥ anavadhikā ti - śayānukūlatvaṃ sidhyati | svarūparūpetyādinā sarvaprakārabhogyatvam tena puruṣārthatvādhikyaṃ siddham | pravṛttītyādinā pravṛtti - nivṛttyoḥ puruṣārthatvavaikalyamuktam; iṣṭasukhalavasādhana- tayā upādeyatvāt, duḥkhātmakatayā svato bhogyatvābhāvācca ||

evaṃbhūtamiti | parokṣajñānasyā'pi sukharūpatvāt, svarūpaparāṇi vākyāni puruṣārthaparya- vasāyīnyeva | tadāparokṣyākāṅkṣiṇaḥ tatsādhanajñāpakatvāt dhyānavidhāyīni vākyānyapi na niṣphalānītyarthaḥ | yathetyādi | evaṃ parokṣajñānajanakasyaiva vākyasya puruṣārthaparyavasāyitve'pi

'brahmavidāpnoti param'
'ātmānameva lokamupāsīta'

iti vedanādiśabdaiḥ upāsanaṃ brahmaprāptyupāyatayā vidhīyate | yathā "sva' veśmani nidhirasti' iti vākyena nidhisadbhāvaṃ jñātvā tṛptaḥ san paścāt tadupādāne ca prayatate, yathā ca kaścidrājakumāraḥ bālakrīḍāsaktaḥ narendrabhavanāt niṣkrāntaḥ mārgāt bhraṣṭa', 'naṣṭaḥ' iti rājñā 'vijñātaḥ ', svayaṃ ca ajñātapitṛkaḥ kenacit dvijavaryeṇa vardhitaḥ, adhigatavedaśāstra; poḍaśavarṣaḥ sarvakalyāṇaguṇākarastiṣṭhan, pitā te sarvalokādhipatiḥ gāmbhīryaudārya vātsalya sauśīlya śauryavīryaparākramādiguṇasampannaḥ tvāmeva naṣṭaṃ putraṃ didṛkṣuḥ phalaviśeṣāpekṣayā dhyānapravṛttiryuktā | yathā 'darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ svargakāmo yajeta'. 'jyotiṣṭomena svargakāmo yajeta' 'agnihotraṃ juhuyāt svargakāma:' ityādiṣu darśapūrṇamāsādīnāmekaikasya svargasādhanatve'pi phalaviśeṣāpekṣayā karmāntarārambhaḥ ekasyaiva karmaṇaḥ āvṛttiśca upapadyate ; yathā putrajanmaśravaṇasaṃjātaharṣasyā'pi paścāt tanmukhā parokṣyasukhāśayā pravṛttiḥ upapadyate tathā'trā'pi ityabhiprāyaḥ | ala nidhi - rājakumāradṛṣṭāntadvayam cidacidvibhāgāt, ubhayagataṃ bhogyatvaṃ brahmaṇyasti iti jñāpanāyoktam | ' tadyathā hiraṇyanidhim ' iti śrutiḥ | rājakumāraḥ iti bhagavati rājatvaṃ cetaneṣu putratvaṃ ca | atra kuśa lava- samādhiḥ | krīḍāsakta iti, cetanānāṃ bāhyavipaya prāvaṇyam | niṣkrānta iti, bhagavatsa. kāśabahiṣṭhatvaṃ, paramavyomālābhaśca | mārgabhraṣṭa iti karmajñānādyapāyānanvayaḥ | dvijavaryeṇa vardhita iti, bhagavajjñānaniṣpattiyogyatā siddhihetubhūtādhyayanādiniṣpādakācārya sambandhaḥ | ṣoḍa śavarṣaḥ -- bhoktṛtvāvasthaḥ bhomyatvāvasthaśca | guṇākara iti bhagavadabhimatairguṇaiḥ pūrṇatā | pitā te ityādinā bhagavadguṇagaṇāḥ | gāmbhīryādaya iti upāsakaṃ prati saulabhyopayuktāḥ guṇāḥ | śauryādayaḥ paratvāntargatāḥ | gāmbhīryam - saṃpradānalāghavadīyamānagauravānapekṣatvam | audāryam - dātṛpratigṛhītṛvibhāga buddhiviraheṇa 'prāsaṃ kṛtam' iti pratipattimatdam ātmātmīyaṃ datvā'pi ṛṇaṃ pravṛddhamitra me' iti sthitirvā | vātsalyam - dopi guṇatvabuddhiḥ ; yathā mātuḥ vatse | suśīlaṃ hi nāma mahataḥ mandaiḥ saha nīrandhreṇa saṃśleṣasvabhāvatvam ; tadvattā sauśīlyam | śauryam - svagṛhe iva śatrumadhye praveśasāmarthyam | bhīrutvapratiyogi hi śauryam | vīryam - aśrāntiḥ | parākramaḥ --- vikramaṇam | atrānuktamapi dhairyam – śatrubhirdharṣaṇe tṛṇavanmanya-

puravaretiṣṭhati' iti kenacit abhiyuktatamenaṃ prayukta vākyaṃ śṛṇoti cet, tadānīmeva ahaṃ tāvat jīvataḥ putraḥ matpitā ca sarvasampatsagṛdraḥ' iti niratiśayaharṣasamantrito bhavati | rājā ca svaputraṃ jīvantam arogam atimanoharadarśanaṃ viditasakalavedyaṃ śrutvā, avāptasamasta puruṣārtho bhavati, paścāt tadupādāne ca prayatate, ' paścāt tāvubhau saṃgacchete ca - iti ||

yatpunaḥ pariniṣpannavastugocarasya vākyasya tajjñanamātreṇā'pi puruṣārthaparyava- sānāt bālāturādyupacchandanavākyavat arthasadbhāve prāmāṇyam – iti,

tadasat; arthasadbhāvābhāve niścite, jñāto'pyarthaḥ puruṣārthāya na bhavati | 'bālā- turādīnāmapi arthasadbhāva bhrānsyā' harṣādyutpattiḥ | teṣāmeva tasminneva jñāne vidyamāne, yadi arthābhāvaniścayo jāyeta, tataḥ tadānīmeva harṣādayaḥ nivarteran | aupaniṣadeṣvapi vākyeṣu brahmāstitvatātparyābhāvaniśvaye, brahmajñāne satyapi puruṣārthaparyavasānaṃ na syāt ||

ataḥ

'yato vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante'
ityādivākyam - nikhilajagadekakāraṇaṃ nirastanikhiladoṣagandhaṃ sārvajñyasatyasaṅkalpatvādya- mānatvam anusandheyam | didṛkṣuriti, sa mahātmā sudurlabhaḥ ' iti bhagavadamiprāyaḥ | puravare iti, brahmapurākhyaśarīrāntaḥsthitiḥ | abhiyuktatamaśabdena bhagavajjñānaprado guruḥ | avāpta... bhavati ityanena vākyasya prayojanaparyavasāyitvam | ataḥ pravartakārthaparatayaiva prayojanaparyavasānamiti niyamo bhana iti ||
arthasadbhāvābhāva iti | idaṃ vākyaṃ pakṣaviṣayam, pakṣadṛṣṭāntasādhāraṇaṃ vā | hetutvaṃ garbhitam | arthasadbhāvābhāvaniścaye sati puruṣārthaparyavasānābhāvāt ityarthaḥ |
nikhileti, kāraṇavākyānāmarthamāha | nikhilaśabdaḥ paraiḥ nityatayā abhimatānām ākāśādīnāmapi kāryatvajñāpanārthaḥ | ekakāraṇam iti ekaśabdaḥ nimittopādānaikyadyotakaḥ |

śriyaḥ prabhuṇā – mahālakṣmīvallabhena, śape – śapathaṃ kurve | mādṛkprabhāvanidarśanam- asmādṛśāṃ śakte: loke prakaṭīkaraṇam, maśakaśakanaṃ manye maśakānāṃ śakteḥ sadṛśamiti dṛr̤ha jāne | pratyarthivarga nirargalakṣapaṇanipuṇam – tāsvikārthaviruddhābhidhāyi janastomasya apratibandhena nirasane atyantaṃ samartham, yativaravacastejaḥ saṃyamīndrasya rāmānujasya vācāṃ tejaḥ, mayi- atyantamasamarthe'pi mayi, kīrtikaram - vijayaprathādhāyakaṃ sat, nityaṃ jāgarti — aviratam apramādaṃ vyāpṛtaṃ vartate || 2 ||

asmin śārīrakaśāstre, paunaruktatyoktabādhau - uktārthasya niṣphalaṃ punarabhidhānam, nyāyanirṇī- tārthaviruddhābhidhānaṃ ca mandatvāsaṅgatatve – alpaprayojanatva - yukti daurbalye, tathā pūrvāparasaṅgati-

rāhityaṃ ca viśayam —– prāptasaṃśayāparihāram aphalatām - aprayojanatvānapekṣitaprayojanatve, mānabādhaṃ ca -- itara pramāṇavyāhatiṃ ca mantṛn - āśaṅkamānān diṅamohakṣobhadīnān- nirdhāraṇīyapradhānārtha - tadupaṣṭambhakārthāvivekakṛtayā vaiyākulyā mlānacittān jijñāsūn digbhrāntān janān ujjihānā— uṣasi ūrdhvaṃ prasantī, dinakara kiraṇa śreṇikevaravimarīcīnām āvaliriva sthitā, hṛdayamadhigatā - hṛdayasparśinī, na tu karṇamātraṃ prāptā, iyam - kariṣyamāṇatvena buddhisthā, hṛdyā - ramaṇīyā, padyāvalī - padyānāṃ mālā, sāvadhānān- pramādarahitān kṛtvā, dhinotu tuṣṭān karotu || 3 ||

tantreṇātha prasaktyā tata upari caturlakṣaṇī devatārthā || 5 ||
nyāyacintye—– nyāyavyutpādanapūrvakaṃ nirṇeye, vedārthe - vedapratipādye, karma-devatā- brahmātmake'rthatraye, prathamam - ādau, karmavargaḥ - yāgādyātmakaḥ kriyākalāpaḥ, pramāṇai:- bhedaiḥ – karma svarūpabhedajñāpakaśabdāntarādibhiḥ,

dharmapramāpakaiḥ vidhyādibhiḥ, viniyojakaiḥ prayuktayā - karmānuṣṭhānaprayojikayā prayojya prayojakabhāva- rūpayuktatyā kramaviracanayā - karmapaurvāparyanirṇāyikayā śrutyādirūpayā, atha adhikṛtyā - adhikārapadavācyayā phalakāmanādirūpayā, atidiṣṭayā - ekatra vidhiprāptasya anyatrāpi prāpikayā atideśasaṃjñayā yuktyā, tatra - atideśe'pi, aśeṣairviśeṣaiḥ sarvairavāntaravibhāgaiḥ, tadanu- tataḥ paraṃ navame, tadanuvartyahataḥ – atideśalabdhasvarūpeṇa ūhanāmnā, prāptabādhaiḥ— atideśaprāptārthanivartakaiḥ tantreṇa - upakāradvayopayogyekānuṣṭhānarūpeṇa, atha prasaktyā - āvaśyakānuṣṭhānāt ānuṣaṅgikatayā itarasiddhirūpeṇa prasaṅgena, adhigataḥ— nirṇītasvarūpaḥ kṛtaḥ | tata upari - tadanantarabhāvinī, caturlakṣaṇī - caturadhyāyātmikā devatārthā – karmārādhya - devatā svarūpa - tadabheda tadupāsana-tatphalaviṣayaka nirṇayopayoginī

|| tattvasāraḥ ||

parehayā - puruṣāntarasya hastādiceṣṭyā, apavarake sthitaḥ antargṛhe vidyamānaḥ, daṇḍa : - daṇḍarūposrthaḥ, caitrāya bodhyatāmiti - caitranāmne puruṣāya jhāpyatāmiti, yaḥ- yādṛśo naraḥ, prābodhi prabodhitaḥ abhūt, saḥ sa nara eva tasya bodhanasamudyataḥ- caitraṃ prati tādṛśadaṇḍabodhane pravṛttaḥ san yat vacaḥ ūce - yat vākyamuvāca tat — tadvacaḥ, etacceṣṭitabhedavit-etādṛśaceṣṭā viśeṣajñaḥ, itaraḥ bālaḥ -- bodhya - bophaka prerayitṛbhinnaḥ kaścit vyutpitsuḥ, mūka iva – vāgindriyarahita iva ākarṇayan - śṛṇvan idam asya bodhakam iti - idaṃ prayuktaṃ vākyaṃ gṛhāntaḥ sthadaṇḍasya bodhajananasvabhāvamiti vyutpattim - pada-padārtha- sambandham, abhyasyati khalu - paricinotīti prasiddham | ( iyaṃ yādṛcchikī vyutpattiḥ siddhe'rthe) || 6 ||

|| adhikaraṇasārāvaliḥ ||

kimapi sambandhikam ambādi, asambandhikaṃ śaśipaśvādi ca aṅgulyānirdiśantaḥ - tarjanyādinā kararuheṇa bālānāṃ cakṣuḥ vastvabhimukhaṃ nayantaḥ, prayuktaiḥ kāśaiḥ–tattadvastubodhakaiḥ śabdaviśeṣaiḥ, bālān– vyutpitsūn, vyutpādayanti - gṛhītaśaktikān kurvanti ! te padānāṃ saṃghātāḥ - prayuktā: padarāśayaḥ, kkā'pi - ' putraste jāta:' isyādiprayogaviśeṣe, siddhe viśiṣṭe – niṣpanne jātiguṇā disahite arthe, dhiyaṃ vidadhati - buddhiṃ janayanti | kā'pi –' ghaṭamānaya' ityādiprayogaviśeṣe, kartavye iti- niṣpādye ānayanādyarthe, dhiyaṃ janayanti iti kvacidiha - siddhe sādhye vā anyatarasminne- vārthe, śabdaśakteḥ niyati. - śabdavyutpattinirṇaya niyamaḥ, na kalpyā – yuktyā sādhayituṃ nāhaḥ || 28 ||

uccāritaiḥ, tadvāca-

tasmāt sātha manuṣyaprabhṛtiṣu ca tathābhūta śikṣā viśeṣa :
kalpo bhāṣyodito'yaṃ na yadi kathamasau kalpate'nyo'pi mārgaḥ || 30 ||

prayatate iti cet — snāna - jātakarmādikriyānirvahaṇe yatnaṃ kurute iti kārye'rthe śābdadhīḥ prāptā -- ityāśaṅkane; astu kārye pravartatāṃ nāma, tat anādeśikam - ( athā'pi ) kāryanirvahaṇaṃ vākyābhidheyaṃ na bhavati, ( iti kārye eva vyutpattiriti nāsti ) || 31 ||

ādyā vyutpattiḥ --- prāthamika : padaśaktigrahaḥ, kārye bhavatu – sādhye'rthe bhavatu nāma, tadapi tathāpi, śaktitātparyasiddheḥ padagatabodhakatvaśaktaḥ kāryamātraviṣayatā siddhiṃ prati, kim - kiṃ vā kāraṇaṃ niṣkṛṣyopapāditaṃ bhavet ? prāgvyutpattiḥ -- prāthamikaśaktigrahaḥ, nṛvacasi mānuṣe vākye, kriyāyām – kriyārūpe ānayanādikāyeṃ, (bhavati) | nigame tu- vaidike tu prayoge, anyatheti kriyā kārya bhinne apūrvarūpakārye iti, abhyupaiṣi - (prābhā- karastvaṃ) aṅgīkṛtavānasi khachu | ata: --- niyāmakasāpekṣatvāt śaktinirṇayasya, ananyathā- sidhyanugamaniyataiḥ : - vṛttyantarasaṃbhāvanārāhityarūpayā ananyathā sidhyā, sarvaprayogasaṃgraharūpeṇa anugamena ca niyatasahakāravadbhiḥ, satprayogaiḥ - abhiyuktānāṃ śabdaprayogaḥ khalu, śaktiḥ sthāpyā śabdaśaktiḥ vyavasthāpyā bhavati | asauḥ kaḥ ayaṃ puruṣaḥ kaḥ ? iti praśne, pāñcālaḥ ityādivākyam - pāñcāladeśīya iti prativacanātmakaṃ vākyam, siddhamātre'pi kriyārahite siddhārthabodhaka pade'pi,

ucitaviratikam - viśrāntimattayā

nirdeśā hai

dṛṣṭam || 33 ||}

duḥkhā saṃbhinna deśapramṛti phalatayā codanāsveva siddham
śrī tatvādārthavādikyapi bhavati phalaṃ rātrisatre pratiṣṭhā |
aṅgīkurmo niṣedhānuguṇamiti tathānarthakṛttvaṃ niṣedhye
vidhyarthairapyataḥ syādavitathaviṣayā brahmavīrarthavādaiḥ || 34 ||

codanātaa' yajeta svargakāma : ' ityādividhivākyeṣveva duḥkhāsaṃbhinnadeśa- prabhṛti – duḥkhāsaṃsṛṣṭadeśātmakaṃ svargādi, phalatayā - yāgāde: phalatvena, siddham jñātaṃ, svīkṛtaṃ vā, (tayā) rātrisatre rātrināmake satrayāge, pratiṣṭhā - sampatsthairyam, ārthavādi- kyāpi—arthavādavākye eva jñātamapi śrautatvāt – 'pratitiṣṭhanti ha vā ya etā rātrīrūpayanti' iti śrutipratipannatvāt phalaṃ bhavati – phalatayā aṅgīkṛtamasti | tathā— uktayaiva rītyā, niṣedhye—' brāhmaṇāya nā'pagureta ' iti niṣedhagocare apagoraṇe, anarthakṛttvam - śatayā tanādi- rūpanāraka hiṃsā janakatvam, niṣedhānuguṇamiti nindyārthanindā nirvāhaka miti, aṅgīkurmaḥ- ubhaye'pyabhyupagacchāma eva | ataḥ uktena dṛṣṭāntena, vidhyarthairapi vidhivākyaśeṣatā- svabhāvairapi, arthavādai: ' satyaṃ jñāna 'mityādibhiḥ vākyaiḥ, brahmadhīḥ – siddha brahmaviṣayiṇī buddhi:, avitathaviṣayā - yogyārthaviṣayā jāyata eveti, nirargala eva sidhyati || 34 ||

ārdhakaraṇārthasaṅgrahaḥ
tatra jijñāsādhikaraṇe prathame -

|| atha janmādyadhikaraṇam || 1-1-2||
|| nyāyakalāpasaṅgrahaḥ ||

bhedaprasakteranubhūtyabhāvāt viśeṣaṇaṃ vā'pyupalakṣaṇaṃ vā |
janmādyaśakyapratipādanaṃ yat ' syāttanniraso' 'pyubhayatra śakteḥ || 8 ||
janmādi — jagajjanmasthitilayarūpaṃ kāryajātam, bheda prasakteḥ - viśeṣaṇa bhedakṛta- viśeṣya bhedaprasaṅgāt, viśeṣaṇaṃ vā'pi viśeṣaṇībhūtaṃ sat lakṣaṇam ityapi, aśakya- pratipādanam – pratipādayitu na bhavati, (tathā) anubhūtyabhāvāt - upalakṣaṇasthalīyā- kārantarapratītyabhāvāt upalakṣaṇam - upalakṣaṇībhūtaṃ sat lakṣaṇamityapi aśakyaprati- pādanam — pratipādayitumarham | yat — evaṃ yaducyate, tannirāso'pi – ubhayavidhānupa- pattipariharaṇamapi, ubhayatra śakteḥ janmādīnām upalakṣaṇatve, viśeṣaṇatve cetyubhayapakṣe'pi anvayayogyatvāt yujyate || 8 ||

' imāni ' iti idaṃpadaprayogavatyāṃ bhṛguvalyām, jagajjanmādibhirlakṣaṇaiḥ jagajjanmasthiti- layātmakaiḥ anitarasādhāraṇaiḥ dharmaiḥ akhileśvaraḥ- sarvaniyantā puruṣottamaḥ, agaṇi iti yat tena – svāsādhāraṇarūpeṇa jñāpitaḥ ityanenā'rthena, sṛjyasya jagataḥ – bhagavatā sraṣṭavyasya adarśi - spaṣṭīkṛtā | tenāspi uktena jagadvaicitryeṇa pi, tatsraṣṭuḥ brahmaṇaḥ - jagannirmātuḥ paramātmanaḥ, jijñāsyatā hetavaḥ - vicāraṇīyatvopapādakāḥ, ujjvalā :- aviruddhābādhitatayā śrutigamyāḥ, guṇagaṇāḥ – svābhāvikāḥ sṛṣṭyaupayikāḥ sarvajñatva- sarvaśaktitvādayo guṇā:, adarśiṣata-

vicitrataiva—acintyātyantavilakṣaṇādbhutākārataiva,

prapañcasya,

spaṣṭīkṛtā eva || 8 ||

|| adhikaraṇasārārāvilaḥ ||

teṣvekaṃ cedvṛthānyat samuditamaphalaṃ syādyavacchedyahāne:
khaṇḍatvādikramāccetyasada vihatitaḥ khaṇḍatāderviśeṣāt || 36 ||

trayāṇāmapi lakṣaṇe melanam, dhīmahimne – śiṣyabuddhivaiśadyāyaiva ; ( ekaikasyāpi anyagāmi- tvābhāvāt) | eke –kecana vedāntārthavyutpādane pravṛttāḥ sambhūyā'pi – milityaiva lakṣaṇatvam, āhuḥ varṇayanti | phalamapi samuditasya lakṣaṇatāyāḥ phalaṃ tu,

tadāśaṅkitārthavyudāsaḥ taiḥ āśaṅkitasya īśvaratrayāpattirūpasya doṣasya parihāra eva | (sṛṣṭyāditraya kartṛtvameva parabrahmatvaniyāmakamiti ) || 37 ||

tammadvedhā'pi bhāṣye'numatiranucitetyāśayājñasya codyaṃ
mokṣārthopāsyabhede hyubhayamapi samanveti vidyāvikalpāt || 39 ||

cintane akuśalāḥ (advaitinaḥ vadanti) | nyāyavṛddhā:- padavākyapramāṇajñāstu, iha- 'satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma' iti vākye, padānām satya jñāna - ananta brahmapadānām, pratipada- niyatāvarjyatattannimittadvārā -- ekaikasminnapi pade vyavasthitatayā avaśyaṃvaktavyāni yāni satyatva-jñānatva-anantatva- brahmatvākhyāni pravṛttinimittāni (śakyatāvacchedakāni ) tāni dvārīkṛtya, viśeṣye ekatrā'rthe – viśeṣyabhūte brahmarūpe ekasmin dharmiṇi, vṛrttim — bodhakatārūpaṃ sanbandham, aruṇādhuktivat - ( aruṇayā ekahāyanyā gavā somaṃ krīṇāti' ityādi vākyeṣviva (tatra hi āruṇya- ekahāyanītva- gotvānām ekasyāmeva gavi khalu anvayaḥ) viduḥ - niścinvanti || 40 ||

śāstrayonitvādhikaraṇam || 1-1-3 ||
|| nyāyakalāpasaṅgrahaḥ ||

kṣityādikaṃ sarvam, sāvayavatvataḥ - avayavārabdhatvāt hetoḥ, kārya ca - kṛtijanyatvarūpa- hetumadapi, saḥ - kartṛtvena sidhyaṃśca cetanaḥ, ādhāra siddhāntataḥ – adhikaraṇasiddhāntarūpāt balāt, ( kasyacitsādhane anyasya anuṣaṅgāt siddhi: - adhikaraṇasiddhāntaḥ) bhagavān īśvārakhyaścetanaḥ ; ityetat - evaṃrūpam anumānādīśvara siddhicintanam na ghaṭeta naiva yujyeta, yat - yasmāt, viśvajanane kāryabhūta jagatsṛṣṭau kartṛkālaikyayoḥ – ekenaiva yugapadeva nirmitatve sādhye, kāryatvavyabhicāribhedagaṇanā - kāryatve hetau anaikāntikaviśeṣasya sādhāraṇatvāsādhāraṇatvānyatarasya doṣasya cintanam hetoḥ viredho'thavā — vipulakāryeṣu kāladairghyakartṛbahutvadarśanāt kāryatvahetoḥ svasādhyāviruddhasādhakatvameva vā bhavati iti || 10 ||

seyaṃ nyāyapatha' prahīṇa-yugapatsargakṣayo nekṣitau
sarvasyeha na kalpanā parikarastannā'numeyaḥ paraḥ || 12 ||

nirmātum, īṣṭe - samartha bhavati | dehī davīyānapi jīvaḥ kāryāt dūravartyapi san, jagat sṛjatu — bhuvanākhyaṃ kāryaṃ janayatu, vā athavā varṣma jagannirmātuḥ śarīraṃ sambhavadapi, dṛśyetarat – atīndriyamevāstu iti || 13 ||

ityunnatau laghutvādanumitivaśataḥ karmajaiśvarya' yukte'
viśvāmitrādinītyā 'sphurati vibhumihā'darśayacchāstravedyam || 41 ||

vītāvīta prayogakramaniyatimatī - sādhyena sākaṃ anvayavyāptiśālinī, vyatireka- vyāptiśālinī ca, kāryatā-sakartṛkatvajñāpakatayā abhimatā utpattimattā, kṛtisādhyatā vā, etat viśvam - vivādaviṣayībhūtaṃ mahī- mahīdharādikaṃ jagat, sarvajñena praklaptam — upādānā- pakaraṇādyakhilābhijñena puruṣaviśeṣeṇa nirmitamiti, gamayati--anumāpayati | atra - anumāna- siddheśvaraviṣaye, śāstrairvicāraḥ - - upaniṣacchārīrakādibhiḥ cintanam viphalastu - piṣṭa- peṣaṇavat phalaśūnyameva | ityunnītau – evaṃ naiyāyikaḥ pratyavasthāne prāpte, anumitivaśataḥ - | anumityadhīnataḥ, viśvāmitrādinītyā -- viśiṣṭa prabhāvāt ' anyamindram' ityādidṛṣṭa- sraṣṭṛtvayukta-ṛṣyātmakacetanaviśeṣanyāyena, karmajaiśvaryayukte – tapoviśeṣādhīnavīśiṣṭasāmarthyavati jīvaviśeṣe eva sphurati — jagattraṣṭṛtvena siddhe sati ( tena arthāntare prāpte) laghutvāt- lāghavātmaka tarka saha kārabalena, iha — śārīrakaśāstre, vibhum - sṛṣṭisamarthamīśvaram, śāstravedyam- śāstraikasamadhigamyam asūtrayat- bādarāyaṇaḥ ' śāstrayonitvāt' iti sūtreṇa pratya - pādayat || 41 ||

kṣityādyaṃ kāryatādyaiḥ kaṭakamakuvat kartṛpūrva sa kartā sidhyedatrā'ṇvadṛṣṭaprabhṛti janakadṛk sarvaśaktiśca maivam |
śrotra : saurabhādigrahaṇaruciriyaṃ tādṛśacyātyabhāvāt
sarva hetuṃ na paśyedghaṭa kṛdiha na cā'kartṛtā tāvatā'sya || 42 ||

sarvavitraśaktimāṃśca bhavati | maivam evaṃ rītyā naiyāyikavarṇanamanucitam ; iyam - īdṛśī cintā, śrotrādyaiḥśravaṇādibhiḥ gandhādyagrāhakaiḥ indriyaiḥ, saurabhādigrahaṇaruciḥ – gandhā- dīnāṃ viṣayīkaraṇābhilāṣasadṛśī, ( kutaḥ ? ) tādṛśavyātyabhāvāt – (kāryatve hetutvenābhimate) sarvajñakartṛkatvāvinā bhāvarūpa vyāpteḥ asiddheḥ iha - ṭānte'pi ghaṭakṛt ghaṭanirmātā kulā- lādi:, sarvaṃ hetum — sarvakāryajanakaṃ kārakasāmānyam, na paśyet — na hi paśyati | tāvatā—sarvakārakādarśanamātreṇa, asya – kulālasya, akartṛtā na ca ghaṭakartṛtvābhāva:

naiva vaktuṃ śakyaḥ iti || 42 ||

yadyapyātmāntarāderanumitiranaghā liṅgabhedaistathā'pi
pratyakṣavyāptiśailī na khalu śithilitā kutracit pakṣabhede |
āmnāye tvadbhutoktirna bhavati vitathā tādṛśāptokti' nītyā '
bādhābhāvādisāmyāt vihatimati bhavellokavagauṇatādi || 44 ||
ātmāntarādeḥ dehāntararādhiṣṭhātaṇāmātmanām tadīyajñānecchādīnāṃ ca, anumitiḥ- anumityātmikā pramā, liṅgabhedaiḥ saprāṇatvā dibhiḥ hetubhiḥ,

yadyapyanaghā nirdoṣaiva

sidhyati | pakṣaviśeṣe, na khalu śithilitā na bhana khalu | ( īśvarānumāne tu - nityajñānādimataḥ pratyakṣato'siddhasya anumānāt, vaiṣamyam |) āmnāye tu vede tu, adbhutoktiḥ - atyantā dṛṣṭa vismayanīyaḥ rthoktiḥ, (arthavādabodhyā), tādṛśāptoktinītyā - adṛṣṭapūrva deśāntarāgatāptavākyasāmyena, vitathā na bhavati – apramāṇabhūtā na bhavati | ( kuta: ? ) bādhābhāvādisāmyāt – ubhayatrā'pi abādhitārthakatvasya samānatvāt | vihatimati—pramāṇāntaraviruddhe arthaviśeṣe ' ādityo yūpaḥ ' ityādau, lokavat – lokadṛṣṭe -iva, gauṇatādi - mukhyārthabādhena aupacārikārthakatvam bhavet — svīkārya bhavet || 44 ||
tathā'pi—–tathā satyapi, pakṣabhede kutracit parātmānumāna sthalīye kasminnapi pratyakṣavyāptiśailī — pratyakṣasiddhavyāptimaryādā,

nanvāmnāyapradhānāḥ kvacidanukathayantyasmadāderaśakyaiḥ
kāryaiḥ kartā'numeyaḥ para iti tadabhipraiti' janmādivākyam |

tasmā dīśā numānatyajanamanucitaṃ vaidikasyeti cenna
kvā'pyaucityopadeśādyata iti ca sadādyuktisiddhānuvādāt || 45 ||

prabhavanti māyāḥ || ' iti anukathayanti - tārkikamatamanusṛtyaiva jñāpayanti, janmādivākyam -' yato vā imānī 'tyādivākyam tat abhipraiti - anumānameva tātparyaviṣayīkaroti | tasmāt — śruti- tadupabṛṃhaṇādiṣu īśvarānumānasya sphuṭatvāt īśānu- mānatyajanam – īśvarānumānāparigrahaḥ, vaidikasya – vedaprāmāṇyavādinaḥ, anucitam - ayuktam ; iti cet na evaṃ cintanaṃ na samyak, kvā'pi paurāṇika bhāge, aucityāṃ- padeśāt – paramātmanaḥ anumeyatvamapi saṃbhavatītyaṃśasya pratipādanāt | yata iti ca ' yato - vā imānī'ti vākyagatam ' yataḥ ' padamapi sadādyaktisiddhānuvādāt - ' sadeva saumyedam ' iti sadvidyādigatavākya siddhārthasya anuvādarūpatvāt yuktaṃ bhavati, ( na tu anumānabodha- katvena ) || 45 ||

.

|| atha samanvayādhikaraṇam || 1-1-4 ||

|| tattvasāraḥ ||
dhik kaumāriladarśanavyasaninaḥ prābhākara prakiyā- vikrāntānapi hanta ! nāthavidhuraṃ viśvaṃ jagajjalpataḥ |
dūre kāraṇadoṣa- bādhakadhiyonathe pumarthe svataḥ
prāmāṇyaṃ na kathaṃ bhajanti vidhivanmantrārthavādoktayaḥ || 15 ||

viśva jagat - nikhilaṃ kāryajātam nāthavidhuram - nirīśvaramiti, hanta — avisaṃvādena, jalpataḥ – avivekena nirlajjaṃ kathayataḥ, kaumāriladarśānavyasaninaḥ-bhāṭṭamīmāṃsaka- prakriyāyāmāsaktān, (tathā) prābhākaraprakriyāvikrāntānapi prābhākaramīmāṃsaka prakriyāyāṃ nipuṇamanyānapi prati, dhik dhikkāraṃ prakāśayāmaḥ | (te śocyāḥ) kāraṇadoṣabādhakadhiyoḥ dūre — doṣādhīnajñānasya bādhakajñānasya ca aviṣaye, svataḥ pumarthe – cetanānāṃ svayaṃprayojanātmake kiñcit advākṛtya paramapuruṣārthasvarūpe, nāthe - apekṣitārthaprade sarvalokaśaraṇye svāmini viṣaye, mantrārthavādoktayaḥ— mantrarūpāḥ arthavādarūpāśca aupaniṣadagiraḥ, vidhivat – vidhivākyānīva, svataḥ prāmāṇyam - svātantryeṇa abādhitārthapratipattijanakatām, kathaṃ na bhajanti-kena vā hetunā na prāpnuvanti ? sarvathā prāpnuvantyeva || 15 ||

!

kartavye arthe - sādhyarūpe vastuni
syāt—vākyaśrotuḥ śravaṇaprayojanaṃ sidhyet |

ukte hi – pratipādite khalu niśamayitṛphalaṃ siddharūpe ukte tu - ' aṣṭau kākasya dantāḥ

ityādivat pravṛttinivṛttyanupayogini nippanne'rthe pratipādite param na syāt - prayojanaṃ na prītyā – ' tava gṛhe nivirasti ityādivākyeṣviva sukhajananamātreṇa, tadartham - prītimā traprayojanakam, vitathamapi vacaḥ–ayathārthamapi vākyam, kiṃ na dṛṣṭam – loke anāptaiḥ prayujyamānaṃ kiṃ nā'nubhūtam ? etatsaṃskaraṇopayuktapramāṇasaṅkhyāvivaraṇī – 10
asmin catuḥsūtrīparyantaṃ sampādite śīrṣikayā, ṭippaṇena, śārīrakanyāya kalāpasaṅgraha - tattvasāra-adhikaraṇasārāvali-adhikaraṇārthasaṅgrahākhyaiḥ śrībhāṣyādhyayana sahakāribhiḥ granthaiḥ, anyānyaiścaviśiṣṭairanubandhaiḥ sahite asmin saṃskaraṇe pramāṇatayā udāhṛtānāṃ śrutismṛtīti- hāsapurāṇānāṃ sthānopalabdhisaukaryāya svīkṛte saṅkhyā nirdeśe upayuktāni pustakāni adhoniddiśyā rītyā jñeyāni -
1. īśa- kena kaṭha praśna - muṇḍaka - māṇḍūkya - aitareya - chāndogyeṣu, (tathā) śvetāśvatara- saubala - kauṣītakī - mantrikā - atharvaśikhā - atharvaśiro - mahopaniṣatsu ca,
śrīmadbhiḥ tamilanāḍu prāntīyaiḥ śiromaṇi- tarkārṇavā dibiruda bhūṣitaiḥ uttamūra, śrī. u. ve vīrarāghavācāryasvāmimahodayaiḥ pariṣkṛtya sampāditeṣu taireva mudrāpiteṣu ca sabhāṣyeṣu pustakeṣu upāttā eva saṅkhyāḥ, pramāṇatayā svīkṛtāḥ |
2. bṛhadāraṇyakopaniṣadi taireva śrīvīrarāghavācāryaiḥ pariṣkṛtyasampādite tirumalai tirupati devasthānataḥ mudrā pite sabhādhye pustake upāttā saṃkhyaiva pramāṇatayā svīkṛtā |
3. taittirīya sambandhinīṣu ānandavallī - bhṛguvallī - nārāyaṇopaniṣatsu ca kāñcīpuryau sudarśanamudraṇālaye śrī. u. ve. pra. anantācāryasvāmimahodayaiḥ śāstramuktāvalyāṃ mudrāpitaṃ kūranārāyaṇīyavyākhyāyutaṃ taittirīyopaniṣat pustakaṃ svīkṛtam |
4. taittirīyāraṇyake_bhaṭṭabhāskarabhāṣyeṇa saha mahīśūranagara prācyavidyā saṃśodhanālayena (oriyaṇṭalū laibrerī) mudrāpitaṃ pustakaṃ svīkṛtam |
śrīviṣṇupurāṇe hindībhāṣānuvādena saha gorakhapura mudraṇālaye devanāgarīlipimudritaṃ pustakaṃ svīkṛtam |

10. svāpyayāt | svāpyayāt - ( jīvasya suṣuptikāle) svakāraṇe sacchabdavācye layasya śrutau kathanādapi, aśabdam - jīvakāraṇatvānvayāyogyaṃ pradhānaṃ, sacchanda. vācyaṃ na bhavitumarhati ||
