12. śrutattvācca śrutatvācca – (asyāmeva upaniṣadi 'ātmata evedaṃ sarva̭m ' iti) cetanādeva jagadutpatteḥ śravaṇādapi jñāpakāt aśabdam - anumānagamyatayā sāṃkhyā - bhimataṃ pradhānam, na jagatkāraṇapratipādaka sacchabdavācyaṃ na bhavati (api tu paraṃ brahmaiva ) ||

ānandamayādhikaraṇam
13. ānandamayo'bhyāsāt ānandamayaḥ taittirīyānandavallīgatānandamaya- śabdavācyaḥ arthaḥ, itaraḥ na brahmaṇo'tiriktaḥ jīvo na bhavitumarhati ; kintu brahmaiva, abhyāsāt —– abhyasyamānaniratiśayānandavattā yogyatvāt ||

14. vikāraśabdāneti cenna prācuryāt vikāraśabdāt - - ānandamayapade vikārārthakasya mayarūpaśavdasya śravaṇāt jñāpakāt na avikṛtaḥ paramātmā na ānandamaya- śabdabodhyaḥ, iti cet — evaṃ kathanaṃ tu, na yuktaṃ na bhavati ; prācuryāt – mayaṭaḥ bāhulya - vācitayā (ānandabahula: paramātmaiva ānandamayapadavācyaḥ ) ||

18. bhedavyapadeśācca bhedasya - praśāsitavye jīve, praśāsituḥ paramātmano bhedasya, (athavā ) ānandamaye, vijñānamayāt jīvāt bhedasya, vyapadeśācca śrutau spaṣṭaṃ kathanādapi, itara : jīvaḥ na ānandamayapadavācyo na bhavitumarhati ||

19. kāmācca nānumānāpekṣā kāmācca - ( paramātmakartṛkā jagatsṛṣṭi: ) 'bahu syām' ' iti saṅkalpādeveti śrutyā pratipatteḥ, na anumānāpekṣā - ānandamaya kartṛkasṛṣṭau pradhānāpekṣatā nā'stīti, ( ānandamayaḥ, na jīvaḥ) ||

10. jyotiradhikaraṇam

jyotiścaraṇa bhidhānāt (1 - 1 - 25) jyotiḥ - paro divo jyotirdīpyate ' iti śrutau 'jyotiḥ 'śabdanirddiṣṭaṃ vastu, brahma – jijñāsyatvenā'bhimataṃ paraṃ brahmava caraṇasya– paramātmāsādhāraṇasya sarvabhūtapādavatvasya, abhidhānāt – ' pādo'sya sarvā bhūtāni ' iti śruta sphuṭaṃ pratipādanāt ||

25.

26. chandobhidhānānneti cenna tathā ceto'rpaṇanigamāttathā hi darśanam chandasaḥ – 'gāyatrī vā idaṃ sarvam' iti pūrvaprakṛtasya gāyatryākhyasya chandasa eva, abhidhānāt - sarvabhūtapādatvena śrutau abhihitatvāt na - jyotiśśabdanirdiṣṭaṃ, paraṃ brahma na bhavitumarhati, iti cet — evaṃ kathanaṃ tu, nana samīcīnam ; tathā gāyatrīgatadharmasādṛśyaṃ prakārīkṛtya, ceto'rpaṇasya brahmaṇo dhyānasya, upāsanasya, nigamāt — phalārthamupadeśāt, tathā hi- chandovācinaḥ śabdrasya taditarasmin tatsāmyaṃ puraskṛtya hi, darśanam - anyatrāpi 'saiṣā virāṭ ' ityādiprayogopalambhaḥ ||

na vakturātmopadeśāditi cedadhyātmasambandhabhūmā hyasmin prāṇaḥ - indra prāṇaśabdābhyāṃ nirdiṣṭaḥ arthaḥ, na upāsyaḥ paramātmā na bhavati vaktuḥ – 'prāṇo'smi prajñātmā' iti vaktuḥ prajñātajīvabhāvasya indrasya, ātmanaḥ - svasyaiva ( upāsyatāyā:), upadeśāt - 'māmupāsva' iti śrutyā jñāpanāt iti cet — evaṃ kathanaṃ tu (ayuktameva), hi- yataḥ, asmin prakaraṇe'smin ( upakramamārabhya upasaṃhāraparyantam ) adhyātmasambandhasya-. jīvātmanaḥ sakāśādbhinnasya paramātmanaḥ asādhāraṇadharmāṇāṃ sambandhasya, bhūmā – bahutvamevo - palabhyate ||

30.

śāstradṛṣṭyā tūpadeśo vāmadevavat upadeśa: - jīvabhūtendrakartṛkaḥ 'māmu- pāsva' iti svātmakarmakopāsyatvopadeśaḥ, vāmadevavat - vāmadevakṛtaḥ svātmani paramātmābheda- vyavahāra iva śāstradṛṣṭyā tu - 'sarvātmā' 'tattvamasi' ityā diśāstraparidṛṣṭārthasya jīva- parayoḥ śarīrātmabhāvakṛtā bhedasya anusandhānenaiva pravṛttaḥ ||

31.

32. jīvamukhyaprāṇaliṅgānneti cennopāsātraividhyādāśritatvādiha tadyogāt jīva - mukhyaprāṇaliṅgāt – tvāṣṭrahananādeḥ jīvāsādhāraṇadharmasya, āyuḥ kāraṇībhūtaśarīravāsādeḥ, mukhyaprāṇāsādhāraṇadharmasya ca śrutau kathanāt na - adhyātmasambandhādhikyena paramātmatvaniścayaḥ na kārya:, iti cet — evamāśaṅkanaṃ tu na na yuktam, upāsātraividhyāt – paramātmanaḥ svarūpeṇa, bhoktavarga śarīrakatveta bhogya bhogopakaraṇa- śarīrakatveneti trividhamupāsanamupadeṣṭum (jīvaprāṇavācakaiḥ śabdaiḥ paramātmanaḥ) abhidhānāt, āśritatvāt asyaiva kramasya anyatrā'nyatra upaniṣatsu avalambitatvāt iha — pratardanavidyāyāmapi tadyogāt – upāsana- traividhyasya abādhitatvāt ||

.

|| iti prathame'dhyāye prathamaḥ pādaḥ ||

34. vivakṣitaguṇopapatteśva vivakṣitānām – vakṣyamāṇānām guṇānām- manomayatvādīnāṃ satyasaṅkalpatvamiśrāṇāṃ dharmāṇām, upapatteśva – paramātmanyeva abādhena sambhavā- dapi hetoḥ, sarvatra - sarvatādātmya pratiyogibhūtaṃ brahma jagatkāraṇatayā prasiddhaṃ brahmaiva ; (na jīvaḥ) ||

śārīraḥ – karmakṛtaśarīrasambandhavān jīva:,

35. anupapattestu na śārīraḥ -

na– 'sarvaṃ khalvidam ' ityuktasarvatādātmya pratiyogī na bhavitumarhati ; anupapattestu - vivakṣitānāṃ - guṇānāṃ śārīre jīve asambhavādapi hetoḥ ||

36. karmakartṛvyapadeśācca śārīraḥ – śarīrasambaddho jīvaḥ, na sarvatādātmya- pratiyogī na bhavati, karmakartṛvyapadeśācca – 'etam' iti paramātmanaḥ prāptikarmatayā, 'abhi- -saṃbhavitāsmi' iti jīvasya prāptikartṛtayā ca śrutau spaṣṭīkaraṇādapi hetoḥ ||
37. śabda viśeṣāt śārīraḥ - jīvaḥ, na - sarvatādātmya pratiyogī na bhavati ; kintu paraṃ brahmaiva ; śabdayoḥ – upāsyopāsaka vācakayoḥ 'eṣa ātmā, me' iti śabdayoḥ, viśeṣāt – 'eṣa:' iti prathamāntatathā upāsyasya, 'me' iti ṣaṣṭhyantatayā ca upāsa- kasya jīvasya, nirdeśarūpāt vailakṣaṇyāt ||
38. smṛteśca śārīraḥ - jīvaḥ, na - sarvatādātmya pratiyogitayā upāsyo na bhavati ; kintu tadanyaḥ paramātmaiva, smṛteśva – 'sarvasya cā'haṃ hṛdi' iti smṛtau upāsyasya hṛdi avasthitasya jīvādanyatvapratīteḥ ||

39. arbhakau kastvāttadvayapadeśācca neti cenna nicāyyatvādevaṃ vyomavacca arbhakau kastvāt – hṛdayāntaḥ pradeśarūpālpasthānāvasthitatvāt tadvayapadeśācca – 'aṇīyān ' iti alpaparimāṇatāyāḥ spaṣṭaṃ kathanācca, na - sarvatādātmyenopāsyatayoktaḥ, na paraṃ brahma, iti cet — evaṃ kathanaṃ tu, na—na samīcīnam evaṃ nivāyyatvāt - hṛdayāvasthitatvena upāsanīyatvāt "paramātmanaḥ ; na alpasvarūpatvam, vyomavacca - mahato'pi ākāśasya sūcīrandhraparicchinnatva- mitra, paramātmano'pi hṛdayasthānaparicchinnatvamiti ||

40. sambhogaprāptiriti cenna vaiśeṣyāt ( paramātmana: hṛdayākhyaśarīraikadeśa- - vṛttitve) sambhogasya - paramātmano'pi jīvavat sukhādyanubhavasya prāptiḥ prasaṅgaḥ, iti cet- evaṃ śaṅkanam, na – na saṅgatam ; vaiśeṣyāt – śarīravartitvādbhinnasya karmaparavaśatvasyaiva

13. attradhikaraṇam
attā carācaragrahaṇāt attā - 'yasyabrahma.... odanaḥ' iti brahmakṣatrau danaka- mṛtyūpasecana kādanakartṛtvena kaṭhavalyāmukto'rthaḥ, na śārīraḥ – jīvo na bhavitumarhati ; carācara- grahaṇāt–jaṅgamasthāvarātmakasya kṛtsnasyaiva jagataḥ upalakṣaṇībhūtena brahmakṣatreṇa bodhanāt ||

41.

prakaraṇācca attā - pūrvoktādana kriyākartṛtvavān (na jīva; kintu jagatsaṃhartā paramātmaiva) prakaraṇācca - 'mahāntaṃ vibhumātmānam' ityādinā paramātmana eva prakramādapi hetoḥ | ( na hi anyaṃ prakramya anyo varṇyate) || 43. guhāṃ praviṣṭāvātmānau hi tadarśanāt hi – yataḥ, guhāṃ praviṣṭau hṛdayaguhāṃ praviśyā'vasthitau, ātmānau - jīva - paramātmarūpau cetanāveba, taddarśanāt - tayoreka jīva- parayoḥ hṛdayaguhā praveśasya 'guhāhiṃta gahvareṣṭhaṃ purāṇam' iti, 'guhāṃ praviśya tiṣṭhantī' iti ca śrutau prakrāntatvāt, (ataḥ jīvadvitīyaḥ paramātmaiva atra attā ; na tu antaḥkaraṇa- dvitīyo jīvaḥ) ||
44. viśeṣaṇācca - attā asmin prakaraṇe adanakartṛtvenoktaḥ (paramātmaiva ), viśeṣaṇācca jīva- parayorubhayoreva prāptṛtva-prāpyatvābhyāṃ prāpakatva prāpyatvābhyāṃ ca asā- dhāraṇadharmābhyāṃ viśiṣya śrutau uktaḥ ||
14. antarādhikaraṇam
45. antara upapatteḥ antara: – ' ya eṣo'kṣiṇi puruṣa:' iti akṣyantarvartitayā uktaḥ puruṣaḥ, (paramātmaiva); upapatteḥ – vakṣyamāṇānāṃ saṃyadvāmatvādīnāṃ guṇānāṃ paramātmanyeva abādhitatayā sambhavāt ||

42.

sthānādivyapadeśācca sthānādīnām – cakṣuḥ sthiti-cakṣurniyamanādīnām vyapadeśācca - ' yaścakṣuṣi tiṣṭhan ' ' cakṣurantaro yamayati' ityādiśrutiṣu spaṣṭaṃ kathanādapi, antaraḥ — akṣyantarvartitayā uktaḥ puruṣaḥ, (paramātmaiva ; na jīvaḥ ) ||
47. sukha viśiṣṭābhidhānādeva ca sukhaviśiṣṭasya - aparicchinnasukhaviśiṣṭatayā agnibhirupadiṣṭasyaiva parasya brahmaṇa:, abhidhānādeva ca ya eṣo'kṣiṇi puruṣa : ' iti vākye ' 'ya eṣaḥ' ityanena upāsyatayā kathanādevā'pi, antara :- akṣyantaḥ puruṣaḥ, (paraṃ brahmava na jīvaḥ) | 48. ata eva ca brahma ata eva ca – aparicchinnasukhasya brahmatvābhidhānādeka hetorapi saḥ aparicchinnānandarūpaḥ akṣyantaraḥ puruṣaḥ, brahma paraṃ brahmaiva ; na jīvaḥ ||

46.

49. śrutopaniṣatkagatyabhidhānācca śrutopaniṣatkasya – adhigataparamapuruṣa - yāthātmyasya viduṣaḥ, gateḥ - śrutyantarapratipannāyāḥ arcirādigate:, abhidhānācca — akṣipuruṣaṃ śrutavate upakosalāya ācāryeṇa upadeśādapi jñāpakāt akṣyantarapuruṣaḥ paraṃ brahmaiva; na jīvaḥ | 50. anavasthiterasambhavācca netaraḥ anavasthiteḥ pratibimbādau niyamena akṣṇyavasthitatvāsambhavāt, asaṃbhavācca jīvādau nirupādhikāmṛtatvādīnāṃ bādhādapi hetoḥ, itara : brahmaṇo'nyaḥ prativimbādiḥ, na akṣyantarapuruṣo na bhavati ||

upanyāsācca bhūtayonyakṣarasambandhirūpatvena 'agnirmūdhā cakṣuṣī' ityādinā kathanādapi, (bhūtayonyakṣaraṃ paramātmaiva ; na pradhāna - puruṣau ) ||
17. vaiśvānarādhikaraṇam
57. vaiśvānarassādhāraṇaśabda viśeṣāt vaiśvānaraḥ - ' ātmānamevemaṃ vaiśvānaram ityatra vaiśvānaraśabdanirddiṣṭaḥ, ( na jāṭharāgnyāditrikam ; kintu paramātmaiva ), sādhāraṇaśabdasya- jāṭharāgri bhūtatṛtīyāgni devatāviśeṣa- paramātmā khyārthacatuṣṭaye'pi sāmānyataḥ prayogāīsya "vaiśvānara " śabdasya, viśeṣāt – paramātmāsādhāraṇaiḥ dharmaiḥ sarvātmakatvādiviśeṣaṇaiḥ, brahmaśabdādibhiśca viśeṣyamāṇatvāt asādhāraṇārthabodhakatayā jñāpanāt ||
58. smaryamāṇamanumānaṃ syāditi iti – ' agnirmūrdhā cakṣuṣī ' ityevaṃrītyā, smaryamāṇam – pratyabhijñāyamānam anumānaṃ syāt vaiśvānaraśabdavācyasya paramātmatve liṅga bhavet ||

59. śabdādibhyo'ntaḥ pratiṣṭhānācca neti cenna tathā dṛṣṭayupadeśādasambhavāt puruṣamapi cainamadhīyate śabdādibhyaḥ sa eṣo'gnirvaiśvānaraḥ ' iti vaiśvānaraśabdasamānādhi- karaṇāt agniśabdāt, (tathā) hṛdayādisthānasthasya vaiśvānarasya amitrayatvaparikalpanāt, evaṃ prāṇāhutyādhāratvācca (tathā) antaḥpratiṣṭhānācca - 'puruṣe'ntaḥ pratiṣṭhita veda' iti vaiśvānarasya puruṣāntaḥpratiṣṭhitatvaśravaṇādapi na vaiśvānaraśabdavācyaḥ paramātmā na bhavitumarhati iti cet- evaṃ cintanantu, na---yuktaṃ na bhavati, tathā - vaiśvānarasya brahmaṇa: jāṭharāgniśarīratayā, dṛṣṭaṭyupa- deśāt upāsanavidhānāt vaiśvānaraśabdavācyaḥ jāṭharāgniviśiṣṭaḥ paramātmaiva na kevalaṃ jāṭharaḥ, (asyārthasyāvagatistu ) asambhavāt – kevalasya jāṭharasya trailokyaśarīratvabādhāt | kiṃ ca, enam — vaiśvānaram, puruṣamapi ca nirupādhikapuruṣatvenā'pi, adhīyate vājasaneyinaḥ paṭhanti ' vaiśvānaro yat puruṣaḥ ' iti ||

60. ata eva na devatā bhūtaṃ ca ata eva vaiśvānarasya trailokyaśarīratvāt, (tathā) puruṣaśabdanirdiṣṭatvādeva ca devatā- agnyākhyā devatā, bhūtaṃ ca-- bhūtatṛtīyaṃ tejaśca, navaiśvānaraśabdavācyaṃ na bhavitumarhati ||
61. sākṣādapyavirodhaṃ jaiminiḥ sākṣādapi agnyādiśabdānām avyavadhāne- naiva avayavaśaktyaiva paramātmavācakatve'pi, avirodham - arthaviśeṣavācakasya śabdasya taditarārtha- vācakatvavarṇanarūpavirodhasyābhāvam, jaiminiḥ – jaiminināmā ācāryo manyate ||

62. abhivyakterityāśmarathyaḥ - ( aparicchinnasyāpi paramātmanaḥ vaiśvānarasya prādeśapramāṇaparimitatvoktiḥ) abhivyakteḥ upāsakānāṃ buddhisaukaryārtham iti — īdṛśamartham, āśmarathyaḥ—–āśmathyanāmā ācāryo manyate ||

63. anusmṛterbādariḥ – ('dyaurmūrdhā' ityādinā paramātmana: puruṣākāratā pari- kalpanam ) anusmṛteḥ - brahmaprāptihetubhūtopāsanasampayartham iti, bādariḥ bādarināmā ācāryo manyate ||
64. sampatteriti jaiministathā hi darśayati ( vaiśvānarasya paramātmanaḥ, upāsakābayave vedyādiparikalpanam ) sampatteḥ- tatkṛtāyāḥ prāṇāhuteḥ agnihotratā sampādanārtham, iti — evamartham, jaiminiḥ– jaiminināmā ācārya: manyate, tathā hi-agnihotratāsampattirūpaṃ tameva prakāram, darśayati – ' ya evaṃ vidvānagnihotraṃ juhoti' iti śrutiḥ viśadayati || 65. āmananti cainamasmin enam - pṛthivyādiśarīraṃ vaiśvānaraṃ paramātmānam, asmin —– upāsakasambandhini śarīre, āmananti - ( upāsanavelāyām anusandhānena ārādhya - svāya) tatsadṛśākāravattayā vadanti śrutayaḥ ||

|| iti prathame'dhyāyedvitīyaḥ pādaḥ ||

69. bhedavyapadeśāt bhedasya - jīvādbhedasya, vyapadeśāt – 'anyamīśam ' iti śratyā spaṣṭaṃ pratipādanāddhetoḥ, (ayaṃ pṛthivyādīnāmādhārabhūtaḥ, paramātmaiva na jīvaḥ) ||

70. prakaraṇāt prakaraṇāt - ' atha parā' ityādinā paramātmana eva prakṛta-- tvāddhetoḥ, (ayaṃ pṛthivyādyādhāraḥ, paramātmaiva; na aprakṛtaḥ itaraḥ ) ||

71. sthityadanābhyāṃ ca sthite:- 'anaśnannanyaḥ iti karmaphalabhogarāhityena - dīpyamānatayā sthiteḥ, adanāt ca- ' svādvatti' iti karmapāravaśyena tatphalāsvādanācca mitho bhedakāt śruyuktāt, (ayaṃ pṛthivyādyādhāraḥ, amṛtatvalambhakaśca paramātmaiva ; na jīvaḥ) || 19. bhūmādhikaraṇam

72. bhūmā samprasādādadhyupadeśāt bhūmā - bahutva ( mahatva ) guṇaviśiṣṭaḥ, 'niratiśayavaipulya viśiṣṭasukhasvarūpa : artha:, (paramātmaiva na jīva:) saṃprasādādadhi - jīvātmāpekṣayā adhikatayā, upadeśāt - ' eṣa tu vā ativadati yaḥ satyenā'tivadati ' iti śrutyā (satyarūpa- paramātmā tivādinaḥ, prāṇasaṃjñakajīvātivādyapekṣayā ādhikyasya) jñāpanāt ||
73. dharmopapatteśca dharmāṇām – (bhūmapadavācyārtha sambandhitayā uktānāṃ) svābhāvikā- mṛtatvādīnām dharmāṇām, upapatteśca – abādhitatayā paramātmanyeva sambhavādapi, (bhūmaguṇaviśiṣṭaḥ paramātmaiva na jīvaḥ) ||

20. akṣarādhikaraṇam
74. akṣaramambarāntadhṛteḥ akṣaram - - etadvai tadakṣaraṃ gārgi' iti śrutinirdiṣṭākṣara- padābhidheyam, paraṃ brahmava, ambarāntasya – vāyumadākāśakāraṇabhūtasya avyākṛtākāśaśabdavācyasya pradhānasya, dhṛteḥ - ('kasminnu khalvākāśa otaśca protaśca' iti śrutyuktāt) dhāraṇāt jñāpakāt || sāca praśāsanāt sā ca - pūrvamuktā ambarāntaśabdavācyapradhānadhṛtiśca, praśāsanāt - (akṣarakṛtāt ) prakṛṣṭāt śāsanāt apratihatājñārūpāt bhavatīti, ( tādṛśamakṣaraṃ, paraṃ brahmaiva ; na jīvaḥ) ||

75.

76. anyabhāvavyāvṛtteśca anyabhāvasya – (akṣaraśabdavācye) vastuni paramapuruṣā- bhedasya, vyāvṛtteśca – (paramapuruṣādbhedavati pradhāne jīve ca asambhavanīyārthasya sarvairadṛṣṭatve sati draṣṭṛtvātmakasya vākyaśeṣe pratipādanena ) nivāraṇāt |

21. īkṣatikarmādhikaraṇam

77. īkṣatikarmavyapadeśātsaḥ īkṣatikarma - 'puriśayaṃ puruṣamīkṣate ' ityukte-

kṣaṇakriyāviṣayabhūtaṃ, 'puruṣamabhidhyāyīta ' ityuktadhyānaviṣayabhūtaṃ ca vastu, saḥ paramātmā | vyapadeśāt – ( īkṣati dhyāyatikarmabhūtamarthamuddiśya paramātmadharmāṇāṃ śāntatvāmṛtatvādīnāṃ ) śrutau spaṣṭatayā kathanāt ||
22. daharādhikaraṇam
78. dahara uttarebhyaḥ daharaḥ - ākāśaśabdasamānādhikaraṇadaharaśabdābhidheyaḥ, saḥ paramātmaiva, uttarebhyaḥ – 'eṣa ātmā'pahatapāpmā' ityādyuttaravākyapratipādyebhyaḥ paramātmā.. sādhāraṇebhyo dharmebhyo jñāpakībhūtebhyaḥ ||

79. gatiśabdābhyāṃ tathā hi dṛṣṭaṃ liṃṅga ca gatyā - aharahaḥ sarvāsāṃ prajānāṃ daharākāśe jñānarāhityena gamanapūrvakaṃ prātyā, śabdena tasminneva viṣaye prayuktena brahma- lokaśabdena ca jñāpakībhūtena, dahara :- daharaśabdavācyo'rthaḥ paramātmeti jñāyate ; hi--yataḥ, tathā — parasmin brahmaṇi evaṃrūpaṃ gamanaṃ brahmalokapadaṃ ca dṛṣṭam - śrutau pratipāditam liṅgañca- idameva ca paryāptaṃ jñāpakam ||

80. dhṛteśva mahimno'syāsminnupalabdheḥ - (1 - 3- 15 ) asya - paramātmā sādhāraṇasya, dhṛteḥ – jagadvidharaṇarūpasya, mahimnaḥ – mahattvasya, asmin — daharākāśaśabdavācye'rthe ( vidya mānatayā ) upalabdheśva – 'eṣa seturvidharaṇa:' iti śrutau darśanādapi jñāpakāt (daharaḥ paraṃ brahma) || 81. prasiddheśva dahara : ( ākāśaḥ ) - daharākāśaśabdavācyo'rthaḥ paraṃ brahma, prasiddheśva – ākāśaśabdasya brahmarūpe arthe 'sarvāṇi ha vā.... ākāśādeva' ityādivākyeṣu 'havā, iti suviditatayā uktatvādapi hetoḥ ||

82. itaraparāmarśāt sa iti cennā'sambhavāt ( 1-3 - 17 jīvasya parāmarśāt- 'samprasādaḥ asmāccharīrātsamutthāya' iti pratipādanadarśanāt, saḥ jīva eva, daharākāśaśabdavācyaḥ, iti cet

itarasya - brahmaṇo'nyasya daharavidyā madhyasthavākye
— evaṃ kathanaṃ tu, na-

)

na yuktimat asambhavāt pūrvoktānāṃ guṇānāṃ jīve bādhāt ||

83. uttarāccedāvirbhūtastrarūpastu uttarāt - prajāpativākyāt, cet — jīvaḥ apahatapāpmatvādiguṇavattayā avagamyate iti kathanaṃ tu na samyakū, āvirbhūtasvarūpastu - parabrahmo- pāsanajanitatadupasampattyā nirastatirodhānatayā prakaṭatāpannasvasvarūpa eva jīvaḥ, prajāpati bākyoktaḥ ; ( na tu svabhāvato'pahatapāpmatvavattayā ) ||
84. anyārthaśca parāmarśaḥ parāmarśaśva – dahara vidyāvākye jīvātmana: prastāvastu,

anyārthaḥ – jīvasvarūpāvirbhāvakāritvarūpasya atiśayitasya paramātmavaibhavasya pratipāda- nārtha eva; na tu daharākāśatā pratipādanārthaḥ |
85. alpaśruteriti cet, taduktam alpaśruteḥ - daharaśabdabodhyasya alpasya parimāṇasya śrutyuktatvāt jīva eva alpaparimāṇa: daharavākyoktaḥ, iti cet — iti kathane tu, ityatra uttaritam ||

tat-

86. anukṛtestasya ca tasya – svabhāvata eva apahatapāpmatvādiviśiṣṭasya paramātmanaḥ, anukṛteśca anukārādeva hi, sāmyāpatyaiva hi ! svasvarūpāvirbhāvaḥ ; (ataḥ daharā- kāśaḥ jīvenānukāryaḥ paramātmā, anukarturjīvādanya eva ) ||

87. api smaryate tasya – paramātmanaḥ, anukṛtiḥ tatsāmyāpattirūpaṃ jīva- gatam anukaraṇam, smaryate'pi — 'idaṃ jñānamupāśritya mama sādharmyamāgatāḥ' iti smṛtā-

vucyata eva ||

22. pramitādhikaraṇam
88. śabdādeva pramitaḥ pramitaḥ - aṅguṣṭhapramāṇapramitatayā śrutyuktaḥ puruṣaḥ, (paramātmaiva), śabdādeva – (tasyaiva aṅguṣṭhamātratayā uktasya paramātmatvabodhakāt ) 'īśāno bhūta- bhavyasya' iti śrutirūpāt śabdādeva jñāpakāt ||

89. hṛdyapekṣayā tu manuṣyādhikāratvāt hṛdi – hṛdayākhye śarīrāntarvartini sthānaviśeṣe, (paramātmanaḥ avasthānāt ) apekṣayā tu tasmin sthāne paramātmanaḥ upāsakārtha- mabhivyaktimabhipretyaiva, pramitaḥ aṅguṣṭhaparimita iti vyapadeśaḥ, manuṣyādhikāratvāt - upāsanavidhāyakasya śāstrasya upāsakaṃ manuṣyamadhikṛtyaiva pravṛttatvāt, ( manuṣya hṛdayasthānasya tatta- • nmanuṣyāṅguṣṭhapramāṇatvāt ) ||
23. etaddarbhe devatādhikaraṇam
90. taduparyapi bādarāyaṇaḥ sambhavāt tat — brahmopāsanam, uparyapi— manuṣyebhyaḥ ūrdhvalokavāsiṣu devādiṣvapi sambhavatīti, bādarāyaṇaḥ - bādarāyaṇanāmā ācāryo manyate | sambhavāt - devādīnāmapi arthitva-sāmarthyayoḥ abādhitatvāt ||
91. virodhaḥ karmaṇīti cennānekapratipattedarśanāt (1 - 3 - 26 ) – ( devādīnām upāsana-

- yogyapaṭutara dehendriyādyabhyupagame ) karmaṇi - bahubhiḥ ekadā kriyamāṇe yāgādau karmaṇi, virodhaḥ- ekasya mūrtimataḥ yugapat vibhinnānekadeśavartitānupapattirūpo virodha: prasajyate iti cet- iti cintanaṃ tu, na – nocitam, anekapratipatteḥ– saubharyādiṣu ekadā anekaśarīraparigraheṇa anekatra saṃnidhānasya, darśanāt - purāṇādiṣu śravaṇāt ||

.92. śabda iti cennā'taḥ prabhavāt pratyakṣānumānābhyām śabde - vedaprayukte indrādiśabde, virodhaḥ - arthaśūnyatvānityatvādyātmakaḥ anabhyupagatārthaprasaṅgaḥ, iti cet — ityevamāpādanam, na na saṅgacchate, ataḥ vedapaṭhitādeva indrādiśabdāt, prabhavāt – indrādidevānāṃ sṛṣṭarabhyupagamāt (asyārthasya siddhistu), pratyakṣānumānābhyām- 'vedena rūpe vyākarot ' iti śrutyā (tathā) 'vedaśabdebhya evāsdau... nirmame ' iti smṛtyā ca bhavati ||

93. ata eva ca nityatvam ataśca yataḥ sraṣṭā prajāpatiḥ vaidikebhya eva śabdebhyaḥ arthākāraṃ smṛtvā sarva sṛjati, ataśca nityatvameva ( vasiṣṭhādīnāṃ mantrādikṛtvespi ) vedasya nityatvaṃ vartata eva ||

94. samāna nāmarūpatvāccāvṛttāvapyavirodho darśanāt smṛteśca āvṛttāvapi - mahāpralayānantaram avyākṛtasṛṣṭeḥ punarāvṛttāvapi samānanāmarūpatvācca – sṛjyeṣu vastuṣu prāktanasargasthavastugatanāma-rūpasusadṛśanāma-rūpavatvādeva, avirodhaḥ vedānāṃ nityatvavirodhyanitya- svābhāvaḥ ; (asyārthamya'vagatistu) darśanāt – 'yo brahmāṇaṃ vidadhāti pūrvam' ityādiśruteḥ, smṛteśva – ' yathatuṣvṛtuliṅgāni ' ityādismṛterapi ca jñāpakāt ||
25. madhvadhikaraṇam
95. madhvādiṣvasambhavādanadhikāraṃ jaiminiḥ madhvādiṣu - madhuvidyā dirūpāsu vidyāsu, anadhikāram — prāptava svādipadānāṃ cetanānām adhikārābhāvam, jaiminiḥ – jaimini- nāmā ācāryaḥ manyate, asaṃbhavāt - upāsyānām upāsakatvāyogāt vasvādipadasya prāptatvena

vasvādyupāsanādhikārasadbhāvameva, bādarāyaṇaḥ - bādarāyaṇo bhagavān manyate, hi-yata:, asti- prāptavasvādipadānāmapi kalpāntare vasvādipadasya prāpyatvam, brahmātmabhāvānusandhānena svātmanāmapi upāsyatvaṃ ca saṃbhavatyeva ||
26. apaśūdrā dhikaraṇam 98. śugasya tadanādaraśravaṇāttadādravaṇātsūcyate hi asya - —jānaśruteḥ rājñaḥ, śuk— brahmavidyāyāḥ alābhakṛtaḥ śokaḥ, tadanādaraśravaṇāt - haṃso tānādaravākyasya ākarṇanāt tadā anādaravākya-śravaṇasamanantarameva, ādravaṇāta – brahmopadeṣṭāramācāryaṃ prati anudhāvanācca, hi-yataḥ, - yataḥ, sūcyate - anumīyate, ataḥ jānaśruteḥ śūdratvaṃ śokādeva ; na śūdravarṇa jātatvāt ||

99. kṣatriyatvagateśca asya - brahmavidyāśuśrūṣoḥ jānaśruteḥ, kṣatriyatvasya - kṣatriyavarṇajātatvasya, gateśca - ( ' bahudāyī bahupākya : ' iti dānapatitva - bahuta rapakvānnadāyitvābhyāṃ rājanyāsādhāraṇadharmābhyāṃ ) pratīterapi jñāpakāt (nā'yaṃ caturthavarṇajaḥ) ||

100. uttaratra caitrarathena liṅgāt uttaratra - etadvidyāsambandhini paścāttane upaniṣadbhāge kīrtyamānena, caitrarathena — citrarathavaṃśyena abhipratārināmnā kṣatriyeṇa, asya - jāna- śruterapi kṣatriyatvagatiḥ - anumityātmakaḥ kṣatriyatvanirṇayo bhavati, (abhipratāriṇaḥ caitrarathatvaṃ kṣatriyatvaṃ ca) liṅgāt–kāpeyapadasāhacaryarūpāt jñāpakāt siddhyati ||

101. saṃskāraparāmarśāttadabhāvābhilāpācca (brahmavidyopadeśeṣu) saṃskārasya - upadeśāṅgabhūtasya, upanayanasaṃskārasya parāmarśāt – 'upa tvā neṣye' ityādivākyenopasthite; ( śūdre tu ) tadabhāvasya - upanayana saṃskārarāhityasya, abhilāpācca' na ca saṃskāramaīti ' ityādiṣu spaṣṭamukteśca śūdretyāmantritaḥ na caturthavarṇīyaḥ ||
102. tadabhāva nirdhāraṇe ca pravṛtteḥ tadabhāvasya — jabālāputre śūdravarṇīyatvā- bhāvasya, nirdhāraṇe ca – niścaye satyeva, pravṛtteḥ – vidyopadeśapravṛtteḥ ('naitadabrāhmaṇo vivaktumarhati samidhaṃ somyāhara' ityādiśrutau ) darśanāt, ( na śūdravarṇīyasya vidyopadeśa: śrutisiddha : ) ||

śravaṇādhyayanārthapratiṣedhāt śravaṇasya - vedākhyākṣararāśeḥ śrotreṇa grahaṇasya, pratiṣedhāt – 'tasmāt śūdrasamīpe nā'dhyetavyam ' iti śrutau vācyavṛttyā niṣiddhatvāt, adhyayanārthayoḥ - gurumukhoccāraṇānūccāraṇarūpasvādhyāyagrahaṇasya, yajñādirūpasvādhyāyārthānuṣṭhānasya ca kaimutikanyāyena niṣedhācca ( na śūdravarṇīyaḥ vidyādhikāritayā śrutyuktaḥ) ||

104 smṛteśva smṛteḥ ca vedaśravaṇa - vedagrahaṇa tadarthānuṣṭhānādīnāṃ niṣedhasya smṛtyuktatvādapi jñāpakāt (na śūdravarṇīyaḥ vedoktavidyādhikārī ) ||

pramitā dhikaraṇaśeṣaḥ

105. kampanāt pramitaḥ - aṅguṣṭhapramitatayā śrutyuktaḥ arthaḥ, (paramātmaiva ; na jīva:) kampanāt – 'yadidaṃ kiñca jagatsarvaṃ prāṇa ejati' iti śrutyukta sarvakampanahetubhūtabhayajanana- yogyatāvatvāt ||
106. jyotirdarśanāt pramitaḥ – aṅguṣṭhapramitatayā śrutyupapādito'rthaḥ, (paramātmā; na jīvaḥ ) jyotiṣaḥ - sūryādisakalatejo'bhibhāvakaṃ sarvatejovabhāsakaṃ ca yat jyotiḥ tatsambandhasya, darśanāt – 'na tatra saryo bhāti ... tasya bhāsā sarvamidaṃ vibhāti' iti śrutyā pratipādanāt ||

27. arthāntaratvā divyapadeśādhikaraṇam

107. ākāśo'rthāntaratvādivyapadeśāt ākāśaḥ – nāmarūpanirvodṛtvena śrutyuktaḥ ākāśapadārtha:, (paramātmaiva; na baddha: mukto vā ātmā) arthāntaratvādeḥ nāmarūpanirvo- dṛtvasya (tathā) nirupādhikabrahmatvādeśca pratyagātmāvṛttidharmasya jīvetaraparamātmamātravṛttidharmasya vā, vyapadeśāt – 'ākāśo ha vai nāmarūpayornirvahitā' ityādiśrutyā spaṣṭaṃ pratipādanāt

|| caturthaḥ pādaḥ |

samānādhikaraṇamahacchabda iva ( 'mahataḥ paramavyaktam ' ityatra 'avyakta' śabdo'pi na pradhānapara: ) |

122. jyotiṣaikeṣāmasatyanne ekeṣām - - kāṇvānāṃ (vākyaśeṣe), anne– annaśabde, asati – avidyamāne sati, jyotiṣā - vākyopakramagatena 'taṃ devā jyotiṣāṃ jyotiḥ ' iti jyoti śabdena jñāpakena (pañcajanaśabdavācyāni prakāśakāni indriyāṇi iti vijñāyate iti, na prākṛtatatvavicārotra prastuta : ) ||
31. kāraṇatvādhikaraṇam
123. kāraṇatvena cākāśādiṣu yathāvyapadiṣṭokteḥ ākāśādiṣu- ākāśa | dipadacihniteṣu 'ātmana ākāśaḥ sambhūtaḥ' ityādivākyeṣu, ākāśādīn prati, kāraṇatvena ca hetutayaiva, yathāvyapadiṣṭasya - sārvātmyādiyuktatayā asmābhiḥ kathanāt, ( abrahmātmakaṃ pradhānaṃ na kāraṇam ) |

vyapa-

diṣṭasya parabrahmaṇa eva, ukteḥ

124. samākarṣāt samākarṣāt - anuvartya viśeṣyatayā grahaṇāt, rūpā saṃspṛṣṭakāraṇāvasthaṃ brahmaiva 'asat ' iti śabdenocyate ; na tu asatkāraṇatāvādaḥ | )
32. jagadbācitvādhikaraṇam

nāma-

125. jagadvācitvāt jagadvācitvāt — ( kauṣītakināṃ ' yasya vaitatkarma' iti vākyagata'karma' śabdasya 'kriyate ' iti vyutpaśyā) sṛjyamānaprapañcavācakatvāt, ('sa vai veditavyaḥ' iti tacchabdārthaḥ samastajagatkartā paramapuruṣa eva jñeyatayopadiṣṭaḥ ) | |
126. jīvamukhyaprāṇaliṅgānneti cettadvyākhyātam jīvaliṅgāt - 'prajñātmaitai- rātmabhirbhuṅkte' iti bhoktṛtvarūpāt jīvaliṅgāt, mukhyaprāṇaliṅgāt – 'prāṇa evaikadhā bhavati' ityevaṃrūpāt mukhyaprāṇaliṅgācca na– 'sa vai veditavyaḥ' iti nirdiṣṭaḥ, na paramātmā ; kintu jīvaḥ prāṇo vā iti cet — evamākṣepe, tat - ākṣepaṇam, vyākhyātam - viśeṣeṇa parihārātmanā pratardanavidyāyāṃ vivṛtam ||

ད

127. anyārthaṃ tu jaiminiḥ praśna vyākhyānābhyāmapi caivameke anyārtha tu- bhoktṛtvādiliṅgena jīvādisaṅkīrtanam, tadatiriktasya paramātmanaḥ sadbhāvapratipādanārthameva, praśna- 'kaiṣa etadbālā ke puruṣo'śayiṣṭa' ityevaṃrūpeṇa anuyogena, vyākhyānena - ' athā'smin prāṇa evaikadhā bhavati' ityevamādinā prativacanena ca iti, jaiminiḥ – jaiminirācāryo manyate, api ca- kiṃca, eke vājasaneyinaḥ evam evameva spaṣṭamadhīyate ||

vākyānvayādhikaraṇam
128 vākyānvayāt vākyasya – 'yenedaṃ sarvaṃ vijānāti taṃ kena vijā- nīyāt ' ityādeḥ kṛtsnasya vākyasya, anvayāt - paramātmanyeva svārthadvārā anvayasambhavāt, 'ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ' iti draṣṭavyatayā nirdiṣṭaḥ ātmā, paramātmaiva ||

33.

pratijñāsiddherliṅgamāśmarathya : ( 1 - 4 -20 ) pratijñāyā :- eka vijñānena sarvavijñāna- pratijñāyāḥ, siddheḥ – niṣpatti prati, liṅgam – (jīvātmavācibhiḥ śabdaiḥ paramātmano'mi - dhānamityetat) jñāpakam iti, āśmarathyaḥ – āśmarathyanāmā ācārya : abhipraiti ||

129.

130. utkramiṣyata evaṃbhāvādityauḍulomiḥ utkramiṣyataḥ śarīraṃ vihāya ūrdhvaṃ kramiṣyataḥ niṣpanna sādhanasya jīvasya evambhāvāt — paramātmabhāvaprāpteḥ (śrutiṣu jīvavācinā śabdena paramātmanaḥ abhidhānam ) iti - evaṃrūpaṃ hetum, auḍulomiḥ – auḍulomi - nāmā ācāryaḥ manyate ||
131. avasthiteriti kāśakṛtsnaḥ avasthite: - jīvātmani antarātmatayā paramātmanaḥ avasthānam antaryāmibrāhmaṇoktaṃ hetukṛtya, ( śruteṣu jīvātmavācibhiḥ śabdaiḥ paramātmā- bhidhānam ) iti - evaṃrūpaṃ samādhānam, kāśakṛtsnaḥ - kāśakṛtsnanāmā ācāryaḥ manyate ||

135. ātmakṛteḥ ātmanaḥ parasya brahmaṇaḥ svasyaiva karaṇasya, upadeśāt – 'tadātmānaṃ svayamakuruta' iti śrutau spaṣṭaṃ jñāpanāt upādānaṃ nimittaṃ ca ||

kṛteḥ – jagadrūpeṇa ubhayam - brahmaiva

136. pariṇāmāt pariṇāmāt – avibhaktanāmarūpasūkṣmacidacidvastuśarīra kasya brahmaṇaḥ, vibhaktanāmarūpasthūla cidacidvastuśarīratayā pariṇāmasya 'avyākṛtamāsīt, tannāma- rūpābhyāṃ vyākriyata' iti śrutau upadiśyamānatvāt, ( apuruṣārthabhūtānāṃ vikārāṇāṃ viśeṣaṇa - gatānāṃ viśeṣye brahmaṇi na prasaktiḥ ) ||
137. yoniśca hi gīyate hi yata:, (brahma) yoni ḥ— 'bhūtayonim' iti, 'brahma yonim ' ca yonitvena, gīyate—- śrutauṃ kathyate, (ataḥ brahma) prakṛtiśca – nimittamiva upādānamapi ||

|| dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ ||
prathamaḥ pādaḥ

jñāpakāt (pṛthivyannavīt ' 'āpo vā akāmayanta' ityādiṣu pṛthivyādau cetanadharmavyavahāraḥ) abhimānivyapadeśastu – pṛthivyādyabhimāni devatā gatacetanadharmavyapadeśa eva na kevala pṛthi- vyādigataḥ ||

144. dṛśyate tu dṛśyate tu - loke kāraṇādvilakṣaṇasyā'pi tasya tasya kāryatvam upalabhyate eva khalu | ( yathā mākṣikādyapekṣayā vilakṣaṇā : krimayaḥ tebhyo jāyante iti ||
145. asaditi cenna pratiṣedhamātratvāt asat iti tet - ('kāryādvilakṣaṇaṃ kāraṇam' ityabhyupagamyamāne, ) ' utpatteḥ pūrva kāraṇam asat' iti prasajyeta, ityākṣepaṇaṃ tu na - na yuktimat pratiṣedhamātratvāt - ( ' dṛśyate tu' ityanena kāraṇavilakṣaṇa kāryotpattipradarśa- nasya tu) availakṣaṇyaniyamābhāvapratipādanamātre tātparyāt ||

146. apītau tadvatprasaṅgādasamañjasam apītau — jagato brahmaṇi layakāle, brahmaṇaḥ sakāśādutpattikāle vā tadvat prasaṅgāt - jagadgatavikārāpuruṣārthādīnāṃ brahmaṇyapi āpatteḥ, asamañjasam - jagata: brahmakāryatāvarṇena vedāntāsāmañjasya karam ||
147. na tu dṛṣṭāntabhāvāt na tu brahmaṇivikārādiprasaṅgaḥ tena vedāntā- sāmañjasyaprasaṅgaśca naivā'sti, dṛṣṭāntabhāvāt — ekasyaiva avasthādvayānvaye'pi guṇa-doṣayoḥ tattanmātra paryavasitatve dṛṣṭāntasya sadbhāvāt ('manuṣyo bālaḥ yuvā samabhavat' iti ) ||
148. svapakṣadoṣācca svapakṣadoṣācca — pradhānakāraṇatāvādapakṣe'pi viruddhārthā - bhidhānakṛtāsāmañjasya rūpadoṣasya sadbhāvādapi sāṃkhyapakṣaḥ tyājya eva ||
149. tarkāpratiṣṭhānādapi tarkāpratiṣṭhānādapi tarkasya apratiṣṭhitatvādapi heto:, tarkamūla: sāṃkhyapakṣaḥ na samāśrayaṇīya: ; api tu śrutimūlo brahmakāraṇatāvāda eva || 150. anyathā'numeyamiti cedevamapyanirmokṣaprasaṅgaḥ anyathā - pratikūla - tarkaiḥ parāhatiḥ yathā na bhavati tathā, anumeyam - pradhānakāraṇatvaṃ sāṃkhyoktam anumānena sthāpa- nīyam iti cet — evam āśaṅkanamapi na ghaṭate, (yataḥ ) evamapi - tarka rūpeṇānumānena sādha- nespi, anirmokṣaprasaṅgaḥ tarkāpratiṣṭhitatvarūpa doṣa vidūratvābhāvāpattiḥ ||

39. śiṣṭāparigrahādhikaraṇam

151. etena śiṣṭāparigrahā api vyākhyātāḥ etena – vedāparigṛhītasāṃkhya-

pakṣakṣapaṇena, śiṣṭāparigrahā api pariśiṣṭāḥ vedāparigṛhītāḥ kaṇādā dipakṣā api, vyākhyātāḥ – kṣapitāḥ veditavyāḥ ||

syāsatvasya akalpanarūpayukteḥ, śabdāntarācca-- sṛṣṭeḥ pūrvamasatvenoktasyaiva 'sadeva somyedamagra āsīt' iti sattvapratipādakasya anyasya śabdasya satvācca ||
157. paṭavacca paṭavacca - yathā pūrva 'tantu' vyapadeśabhākū yo'rthaḥ sa eva anantaraṃ tantūnāṃ vyatiṣaṅgaviśeṣabhāktatvena 'paṭa:' iti vyapadiṣṭaḥ, tadvadeva brahmaṇo'pi sūkṣmāvasthasya asadvyapadeśaḥ, sthūlāvasthasya sadvyapadeśaśca yujyata eveti kāraṇānanyatvaṃ kāryasya || 158. yathā ca prāṇādiḥ prāṇādiśca yathā -- prāṇāpānādyātmaka vṛttipañcakaṃ bhajamānaḥ kāryo vāyuḥ, ekasmādeva śarīrasthāt kāraṇabhūtāt vāyoḥ yathaiva ananyaḥ, tathā sthūlacidacidviśiṣṭabrahmātmakaṃ kāryāvasthaṃ jagat, sūkṣmacidacidviśiṣṭabrahmātmakāt kāraṇāvasthā - dvastuna: ananyadeva ||

163. devādivadapi loke loke - sve sve loke, devādivat - devagandharvādīnāṃ svasaṅkalpena svāpekṣitārthasraṣṭatvamiva brahmaṇo'pi svasaṅkalpādeva sarvasraṣṭṛtvaṃ yuktameva ||

171. lokavattu līlākaivalyam līlākaivalyam — kevalāyā eva līlāyāḥ sṛṣṭiprayojanarūpatā, lokattu loka rājani ( pūrṇa kāmepi kandukakrīḍādyārambhe) iva yujyate | 172. vaiṣamya nairghṛṇyena sāpekṣatvāt tathā hi darśayati vaiṣamyanairdhṛṇye— (deva - manuṣyādirūpeṇa sukha - dukhādibahulatayā ca jagat sṛjataḥ parasya brahmaṇaḥ ) sarveṣu jīvātmasu samatārāhityam (tathā) duḥkhiṣu dayāśūnyatvaṃ ca prasajyate iti tu ākṣepa:, na-nā''patati, sāpekṣa- tvāt sṛṣṭivaiṣamyasya tattatkṣetrajñānāṃ karmaviśeṣānubandhitvāt, tathā - devādidehayojanaṃ tattajjīva- karmasāpekṣamiti, darśayati hi śrutireva bodhayati khalu ||

173. na karmā'vibhāgāditi cennā'nāditvādupapadyate cā'pyupalabhyate ca avibhāgāt — sṛṣṭiprākkāle jīvasya brahmaṇā saha avibhāgaśruteḥ, nakarma - viṣamasṛṣṭiprayojakaṃ tattajjīvīyaṃ karma na sthātumarhati iti cet — evaṃ śaṅkanam na na saṅgatam, (yataḥ ) upapadyate ca - ( vaiṣamyahetubhūtānāṃ jīvānāṃ tatkarmaṇāṃ ca sṛṣṭeḥ prāk satre'pi ) avibhāgaśrutiḥ nāmarūpavibhāgābhāvamātrādeva yuktā bhavati | anāditvāt - ( jīvānāṃ tatkarmaṇāṃ ca sattāyā api) anidaṃprathamatvāt, upalabhyate ca – (jīvānāmanāditvamapi ) ' na jāyate mriyate vā ' ityādibhiḥ śrutibhiḥ jñāyata eva ||

174. sarvadharmopapatteśca (2 - 1 - 36) sarvadharmopapatteśca sarvajagattratvopayogināṃ sarveṣāmapi sarvajñatvādidharmāṇāṃ pradhāna - paramāṇvādiṣu anupapannānāṃ parabrahmaṇi yujyamānatvādapi hetoḥ paraṃ brahmaiva jagataḥ kāraṇam ||

|| iti dvitīyādhyāye prathamaḥ pādaḥ ||

176. payo'mbuvaccettatrāpi payo'mbuvat cet — kṣīranīrayoriva cetanānadhiṣṭhita- syāpi pradhānasya pravṛttiḥ ityuktiḥ, na na sambhavati, tatrā'pi - kṣīranīrayorapi, pravṛtteḥ- cetanena prājñena adhiṣṭhitayoreva kāryānuguṇapariṇāmātmikāyāḥ pravṛtteḥ sattvāt ||

vyatirekānavasthiteścānapekṣatvāt (2 - 2 - 3 ) anapekṣatvāt - ( kevalasya pradhānasya kāraṇatve ) pradhānasya cetanāpekṣatvābhāvāt vyatirekānavasthiteśca - sargavyatirekeṇa prati-- sargāvasthayā pralayarūpayā avasthānābhāvasya prasaṅgācca, pradhānaṃ na kāraṇam ||

177.

anyatrābhāvācca na tṛṇādivat tṛṇādivat - tṛṇajalādīnāṃ cetanā- nadhiṣṭhitānāmapi kṣīrādyātmanā pariṇāmavadapi ( pradhānasyā'pi cetanānadhiṣṭhitasya jagadrūpapariṇāmaḥ sambhavati, iti tu) na—na samañjasam, anyatra – nuvyatirikte aḍuhādau, abhāvāt - tṛṇa- jalādeḥ kṣīrādyātmanā pariṇāmābhāvāt ||

178.

179. puruṣāśmavaditi cet, tathā'pi puruṣavat - gamanaśakti vikale darśana śaktiyukte puruṣe svasannidhimātreṇa andhapuruṣasya gamanapravṛttijanakatvavat, (tathā) aśmavat - ayaskāntopale saṃnihitamātre ayaḥ pravṛttijanakatvavacca, pradhānasyā'pi pravṛttijanakatvaṃ puruṣe, iti cet — evaṃ cintanamapi, na-na yuktimat tathāspi - puruṣāyaskāntasādṛśyenā'pi

( pradhāne pravṛttyayogāt || )

180 aṅgitvānupapatteśca ( 2 - 2 - 6 ) anitvasya – utkarṣanibandhanasya aṅgapratisambandhi- tvasya, upakāryatvasya, anupapatteśca – pralayakālikasāmyāvasthāvati ekasminneva pradhāne, anupapatteśca kāraṇā saṃnidhānadaśāyām asambhavādapi hetoḥ, (pradhānasya jagatkāraṇatā na yujyate ) ||
181. anyathā'numitau ca jñaśaktiviyogāt anyathā - dūṣitaprakārādbhinnaṃ prakāramanurudhya, anumitau ca – pradhānānumāne'pi, jñaśaktiviyogāt – pradhāne jñātṛtvaśakteḥ asambhavāt, pradhānasya svatantrajagatkāraṇatā naiva sambhavati ||
182. abhyupagame'pyarthābhāvāt ( 2 - 2 - 8) abhyugame'pi pradhānākhyavastunaḥ aṅgī- kāre'pi, arthābhāvāt – nirvikāre puruṣe bhoga - mokṣarūpayoḥ prayojanayoḥ upapādayitumaśakyatvāt, ( pradhānasyānumānagamyatā na samasti ) ||
183. vipratibedhāccāsamañjasam vipratiṣedhācca – parasparaviruddha bhāṣaṇādapi, asamañjasam – sāṃkhyamataṃ na samīcīnam ||

47. mahaddīrghādhikaraṇam
184. mahaddīrghavadvā hrasvaparimaṇḍalābhyām hrasvaparimaṇḍalābhyām - vyaṇukaparamāṇubhyām hetubhūtābhyām, mahaddīrghavat vyaṇukadvyaṇukayoḥ utpattivat, vā- anyacca vaiśeṣikāṅgīkṛtaṃ prameyam, asamañjasam - yuktihīnameva ||

ubhayathā'pi na karmāstastadabhāvaḥ kriyotpādakatvenā'bhyupagatasya adṛṣṭasya) aṇugatatve jīvagatatve vā paridṛśyamānam aṇugataṃ spandātmakaṃ karma, na- adṛṣṭakāritaṃ na bhavitumarhatīti, ataḥ paramāṇu- gatasya vā, ātmagatasya vā adṛṣṭasya kriyāhetutvāyogāt, tadabhāvaḥ- jagadutpatteḥ aṇugata - karmapūrvakatvābhāvaḥ ||

ubhayathā'pi - ( aṇuṣu
karma - kādācitkatayā

185.

samavāyābhyupagamācca sāmyādanavasthiteḥ samavāyābhyupagamācca- (jātiguṇādiviśiṣṭapratītinirvāhakatvena ) samavāyākhyasambandhasyā'ṅgīkṛtatvādapi, asamañjasam- vaiśeṣikamataṃ niyuktikameva | sāmyāt - ( paṭaḥ samavetaḥ iti viśiṣṭapratītinirvāhakatayā ) samavāyanirūpitasyānyasyāpi samavāyasya abhyupagamanīyatvasāmyāt, anavasthiteḥ – (tathā sambandhāntarābhyupagame) anavasthāprasaṅgāt ||

186.

187. nityameva ca bhāvāt nityameva bhāvāt ca - ( nityasambandhākhya- samavāyasya) sarvadaiva satvādapi ( anāyatyā sambandhinorapi nityatāyāḥ prasaṅgādapi, paramāṇu- kāraṇatāvarṇanam ) asamañjasam - yuktihīnameva ||
188. rūpādimaccācca viparyayo darśanāt rūpādimacācca - ( paramāṇūnāṃ ) rūparasādimattvāṅgīkārāt, viparyayaśca teṣāṃ nityatvaviparītamanityatvaṃ niravayavatvaviparītaṃ sāvayavatvaṃ ca prasajyeta, darśanāt - rūpādimatsu ghaṭādiṣu anityatva - sāvayavatvayoḥ upalabdhatvāt ||

ubhayathā ca doṣāt ubhayathā ca - paramāṇūnāṃ rūpādirāhitye, sāhitye ca, doṣāt kāryaguṇānāṃ kāraṇaguṇapūrvakatvābhāvasya anityatvasya ca doṣasya anirāsāt, ( paramāṇu kāraṇatāvarṇanam asamañjasameva ) ||
190. aparigrahāccātyantamanapekṣā atyantamaparigrahācca - (vaiśeṣikamatasya kasminnapyaṃśe śiṣṭairvaidikaiḥ) asvīkṛtatvādapi, atyantamanapekṣā - (niḥśreyasārthibhiḥ) sarvaprakāreṇa apekṣaṇīyatvābhāvaḥ, upekṣaṇīyataiva ||

48. samudāya| dhikaraṇam
samudāya ubhayahetuke'pi tadaprāptiḥ ubhayahetuke'pi -- paramāṇuhetu- katvābhimate, tathā pṛthivyādihetukatvā'bhimate'pi ca, samudāye - pṛthivyādibhūta samudāye, tathā śarīrendriyādisamudāye ca tadaprāptiḥ samudāyatvāsiddhiḥ ; (sarveṣāṃ kṣaṇikatvābhyupagamāt ) || 192. itaretara pratyayatvādupapannamiti cenna saṃghāta bhāvānimittatvāt itaretarapratyayatvāt – avidyāyāḥ rāgadveṣādeśca parasparaṃ kāryakāraṇabhāvāt upapannam – loka- vyavahārādikaṃ sarvamavādhita sampannam iti cet — ityāśaṅkanaṃ tu, na-na ghaṭate, saṅghātabhāvā- nimittatvāt - (viparītabuddhayātmikāyāḥ avidyāyā:) pṛthivyādīnāṃ samudāyatvaṃ prati kāraṇatvāsambhavāt, samudāyatvasyā'siddhireva ||

191.

198. anusmṛteśca anusmṛteśca – 'tadevedam' iti pratyābhijñābalādapi, (kṣaṇi- katvavirodhinaḥ sthiratvasya siddhayā) kṣaṇikatvaṃ saugatasammatam asamañjasameva ||
199 nā'sato'dṛṣṭatvāt - asataḥ niranvayavinaṣṭasya dharmiṇaḥ (dharmasya anyatra saṅkramaḥ) na - sarvathā asambhāvitaḥ, adṛṣṭatvāt tasya kutrā'pi anupalabdhatvāt ||
200. udāsīnānāmapi caivaṃ siddhi: evam sarvamapi bhāvānāṃ kṣaṇika- tvābhyupagame sati, udāsīnānāmapi ca - niṣprayatnānāmapi ca puruṣāṇām, siddhiḥ aihikāmuṣmikasarvārthaniṣpattiḥ syāt, (ataḥ sautrāntrika - vaibhāṣikayoḥ ubhayorapi matam asamañjasameva ) ||
49. upalabdhyadhikaraṇam
201. nābhāva upalabdheḥ abhāva : - jñānetarasya bāhyārthasya asattvam, na - abhyupagantuṃ na śakyate, upalabdheḥ jñānaviṣayībhūtasya ghaṭādyarthasya pratyakṣa dṛṣṭatvena anapa- lapanīyatvāt ||
202. vaidharmyācca na svapnādivat svapnādivat – svāpnajñānānāmiva na - (jāgaritajñānānāmapi mityābhūtārthaviṣayakatvam ) na sambhavati, vaidharmyācca ( kāraṇadoṣa- bādhakapratyayarāhityābhyāṃ svāpnajāgaritajñānayoḥ) vaiṣamyādapi, ( ataḥ jāgaritajñānāni yathārthaviṣayāṇyeva ) ||
203. na bhāvo'nupalabdheḥ bhāvaḥ - ( arthaśūnyasya kevalasyaiva jñānasya) sattvam, nava sambhavati, anupalabdheḥ - ( akartṛkasyā karmakasya ca jñānasya sattāyāḥ) ananubhavāt ||

206. evaṃ cātmākātsyam evam – ekasmin viruddhadharmābhyupagama iva, ātmākātsnye ca jīvātmanaḥ aparipūrṇatvamapi (sarvasmin dehe jīvasya tattaddeha samaparimāṇatvaṃ "jainasammatam ) nana yuktam ||

207. na ca paryāyādapyavirodho vikārādibhyaḥ paryāyādapi - ātmani kālabhedena saṅkova-vika'sātmakaparimāṇavaividhyāṅgīkaraṇādapi, asāmānādhikaraṇyābhāva:, na ca naiva saṃmpādayituṃ śakyaḥ, pariṇāmātmakavikārasya tatprayuktānāṃ doṣāṇāṃ ca aparihārāt || 208. antyāvasthiteśvobhayanityatvāda viśeṣaḥ ubhayanityatvācca mokṣā- vasthasya jīvīyaparimāṇasya avināśena sthiteḥ satvāt, ubhayanityatvācca - jīva-tatparimāṇo - yasyāpi avināśitvādeva, aviśeṣaḥ – mokṣa tatpūrvāvasthayoḥ parimāṇavailakṣaṇyāsiddhi: (iti ārhataṃ matamayuktameva) ||

avirodhaḥ – parimāṇadvayasya vikārādibhyaḥ svarūpa-

52. paśupatyadhikaraṇam

patyurasāmañjasyāt patyuḥ – paśupateḥ matam, (anādaraṇīyam ) asāmañjasyāt – vedaviruddhatatvācā rāvabodhitvena asamīcīnatvāt ||

209.

210. adhiṣṭhānānupapatteśca adhiṣṭhānasya — upādānakāraṇaṃ pradhānamadhiṣṭhāyā'va- sthānasya, anupapatteśca - aśarīre īśvare ayogādapi, ( paśupatermatam asamañjasam ) ||
211. karaṇavaccenna bhogādibhyaḥ karaṇavat cet — aśarīsya kṣetrajñasya svīyakaraṇakalebarādyadhiṣṭhātṛtvamiva, aśarīrasya paśupaterapi prakṛtyadhiṣṭhānaṃ yujyate iti samādhānaṃ tu) na saṅgataṃ na bhavati | bhogādibhyaḥ - ( tathā sati jīve puṇya-pāpaphalabhūtasukha-duḥkhopabhoga iva) paśupatāvapi bhogādiprasakteḥ ||

antavattvamasarvajñatā vā ( 2 - 2 - 38) antavattvam – (paśupaterapi puṇyāpuṇyayoḥ abhyu- pagame, tasyā'pi ) sṛṣṭisaṃhārāspadatvam, asarvajñatā vā - sarvajñatāyāḥ abhāvaśca prasajyeyātām (iti paśupatermatam asamañjasameva ) ||
53. utpantyasambhavādhikaraṇam
213. utpatyasambhavāt utpatteḥ - pāñcarātrāgamoktāyāḥ jīvotpatteḥ, asambhavāt- ( utpatti vināśarahitatayā śrutisiddhe jīve) ayogāt, ( jīvotpattipratipādakaṃ pāñcarātram apramāṇam) |

212.

na ca kartuḥ kāraṇam karaṇam – jñānādikaraṇībhūtaṃ manaḥ kartuḥ kartṛ- bhūtajīvasakāśāt, na ca–naiva uttumaītīti ( jīvāt manasa utpattipratipādakaṃ pāñcarātram apramāṇam) |
215. vijñānādibhāve vā tadapratiṣedhaḥ vijñānādibhāve saṅkarṣaṇādīnāṃ śuddhajñānātmakaparabrahmasvarūpatve eva siddhe sati tadapratiṣedhaḥ - ( svecchāvatārātmakabrahmotpattiprati- pādakasya) pāñcarātrasya prāmāṇyapratiṣedhaḥ naiva sidhyati ||
216. vipratiṣedhācca vipratiṣedhācca - ( asminnapi pāñcarātre) jīvotpatteḥ viśeṣeṇa niṣiddhatvādapi, ( na śrutiviruddhārthapratipādana miti, pāñcarātraprāmāṇyasya na pratiṣedhaḥ ) || || iti dvitīyādhyāyedvitīyaḥ pādaḥ ||

214.

222. śabdebhyaḥ śabdebhyaḥ sṛṣṭeḥ pūrva brahmaṇa ekasyaiva satvapratipāda - kebhyaḥ śrutivākyebhyaḥ, (ākāśasya kāryatā niścīyate) ||
223. yāvadvikāraṃ tu vibhāgo lokavat yāvadvikāram — vikārabhūtānāṃ yā vatāmapyarthānām, vibhāgastu — brahmaṇaḥ sakāśādeva utpattirityetat, lokavat - laukikeṣu. vyavahāreṣu vācā anuktasyāpi yasya kasyacidaṃśasya arthātsiddhiriva, arthādeva sidhyati ||

224. etena mātariśvā vyākhyātaḥ etena ākāśotpattisādhakanyāye- naiva, mātariśvā - vāyurapi, vyākhyātaḥ – utpantyāśrayatayā pratipādito bhavati ||

225. asambhavastu sato'nupapatteḥ asambhavaḥ - utpatteḥ satastu — sacchabdavācyasya brahmaṇa eva, anupapatteḥ – eka vijñānena brahmātiriktasarvavijñānaprati- jñānānyathānupapasyaiva sidhyati ||

asambhavaḥ,

55. tejo'dhikaraṇam

226. tejo'tastathā hyāha tejaḥ agniḥ, utpadyate, tathā hi - ' agneḥ vāyoḥ sakāśādutpattiḥ' śrutiḥ spaṣṭayati ||

ataḥ kevalāt vāyoreva

ityeva hi

āha - ' vāyoragniḥ ' iti

227. āpaḥ ataḥ - tejasa eva kevalāt tathā hi- 'tejasaḥ sakāśāt jalasyotpattiḥ ' ityeva hi śrutirvadati ||

āpaḥ - jalam utpadyate,
āha - ' agnerāpa: ' iti

228. pṛthivī pṛthivī - pṛthivītatvamapi, ataḥ kevalāt jalādeva utpadyate, tathā hi - pṛthivyāḥ jalādutpattimeva hi, āha' adbhayaḥ pṛthivī ' iti śrutirvarṇayati ||

229. adhikāra - rūpa- śabdāntarebhyaḥ adhikārāt - mahābhūtasṛṣṭiprastāvāt, rūpāt–mahābhūtāntargatapṛthivīgatasya kṛṣṇarūpasya saṅkīrtanāt śabdāntarāt - samānaprakaraṇe 'adbhyaḥ pṛthivī ' iti, annaśabdasthāne 'pṛthivī' śabdasya śrutau nirdeśācca jñāpakāt, ('tā annamasṛjanta' ityādau annaśabdārthaḥ pṛthivyeva ) ||

230 tadabhidhyānādeva tu talliṅgātsaḥ talliṅgāt – asādhāraṇatayā paramātmatvajñāpakāt tadabhidhyānāt - 'bahu syām' ityevaṃrūpasṛṣṭiviṣayaka saṅkalpātmakāddharmāt

sa eva - teja ādiśarīrakaḥ paramātmaiva tattadanantarabhūtānāṃ kāraṇabhūtaḥ ; ( ' natu kevalāni tāni bhūtāni iti niścīyate) ||
231. viparyayeṇa tu kramo'ta upapadyate ca atastu - tattadbhūtadvārā paramātmana eva upādānatvādeva, viparyayeṇa kramaśca - ' etasmājjāyate prāṇa : ' ityādiśrutyuktaḥ viparītaḥ kramo'pi, upapadyate saṅgato bhavati ||

antarā vijñāna- manasī krameṇa talliṅgāditi cennā'viśeṣāt antarā - bhūta-prāṇayormadhye, vijñāna- manasī indriyāṇi manaśca krameṇa – pāramparyakṛtena paurvāparyeṇa utpadyete ; na tu brahmaṇaḥ avyavadhānena ( ityetatpratipādakam ' etasmājjāyate prāṇaḥ ityādi vākyam ) talliṅgāt - ( śrutyantarāvagatakramayuktakhā (ākāśā) disahapāṭha rūpāt ) bhūtasṛṣṭi- kramapratyabhijñāpakāt 'khaṃ vāyuḥ' ityādeḥ, iti cet — iti kathanaṃ tu, na-na yuktimat ! aviśeṣāt prāṇa-mana ādiṣu brahmā vyavahitotpatteḥ pratipādanasya samatvena tatparityāgā- naucityāt ||
233. carācaravyapāśrayastu syāt tadvyapadeśo'bhāktaḥ tadbhāvabhāvitvāt carācaravyapāśrayaḥ – jaṅgamasthāvarātmakavastvabhidheyakaḥ, tadvyapadeśaḥ - brahmaparyantābhidhāyi- tattacchabdanirdeśaḥ, abhāktaḥ syāt - brahmaṇi anaupacārikaḥ mukhyo bhavedeva, tadbhāvabhāvitvāt- carācareṣu antaryāmitayā paramātmanaḥ praveśānantarabhāvitvāt ||

232.

yevai .... sarve gacchanti ' iti gamanaśruteḥ āgati (śruteḥ) - ' tasmāllokāt punareti' iti āgamanasyā'pi śrutipratipannatvācca ||
237. svātmanā cottarayoḥ uttarayoḥ - utkrānteranantaraṃ śrutayoḥ gatyāgatyoḥ, svātmanā ca svātmasvarūpeṇaiva, nirvāhyatvāt, ( gamanāgamanāśrayībhūtaḥ jīva: aṇureva ; na vibhuḥ) ||
238. nā'ṇuratacchruteriti cenetarādhikārāt ātmā —– jīvaḥ, aṇuḥ na- aṇuparimāṇavān na bhavitumaīti atacchūte:- aṇutvaviruddhasya mahattvasya 'mahāna ātmā

ityādiṣu śravaṇāt iti cet — evaṃ kathanaṃ tu, nana yuktimat itarādhikārāt- jīvā ditaraṃ paramātmānaṃ prastutya vibhutvasyokteḥ ||

239. svaśabdonmānābhyāṃ ca ātmā - jīvaḥ, aṇuḥ - aṇuparimāṇa eva, svaśabdonmānābhyāṃ ca – sākṣāt aṇuparimāṇavācakena ' aṇu 'śabdena ' eṣo'ṇurātmā' iti, tathā ārāgrarūpam aṇubhūtaṃ vastu pṛthaṅnirdiśya tatsamānaparimāṇatāyā: ' ārāgramātrodyavara : ' iti ca sphuṭīkaraṇācca ||

240. avirodhaścandanavat candanavat — dehaikadeśasthasya candanasya yathā sarvāṅgīṇāhlādajanakatvaṃ tathā, avirodhaḥ -- aṇutvena dehaikadeśasthasyā'pyātmanaḥ svīyadharmabhūta- jñānataḥ sarvāṅgīṇasukhādyanubhavitṛtve virodhābhāvaḥ ( cintanīyaḥ ) ||
241. avasthitivaiśeṣyāditi cennā'bhyupagamādvṛdi hi avasthiti- vaiśeṣyāt – ātmanaḥ avasthiteḥ, candanādeḥ dehaikadeśa (vasthiteśca vailakṣaṇyasatvāt ( ātmani candanasāmyaṃ na yujyate, ) iti cet — evaṃ cintanaṃ tu, nana saṅgatam, abhyupagamāt – ātmanaḥ dehaikadeśāvasthānamya aṅgīkṛtatvāt, hṛdi hi - hṛdayadeśe hi jīvaḥ avasthita iti ' hṛdyanta- jyotiḥ' iti śrutau spaṣṭam ||
242. guṇādvā''lokavat ālokavat – aneka deśavyāpinī ghumaṇi-maṇyādīnāṃ prabheva, guṇādvā – svadharmabhūtena sarvadehavyapinā jñānenaiva (dvārabhūtena ), ( ātmā dehaikadeśastho'pi sarvadeha sambaddho bhavati) ||
243. vyatireko gandhavattathā ca darśayati vyatirekaḥ - ātmadharmasya jñānasya svarūpe ātmanaḥ sakāśāt bhedaḥ, gandhavat pṛthivīguṇabhūtasya gandhasya pṛthavīto bheda ika

svīkaraṇīyaḥ, tathā ca – āśrayāśrayibhāvanibandhanaṃ svarūpabhedameva, darśayati- 'jānātyevāsyaṃ puruṣa: ' ityādirūpaśrutiḥ spaṣṭaṃ pratipādayati ||
244. pṛthagupadeśāt pṛthaka - ātmānaṃ tadāśritaṃ jñānaṃ ca tattadvācakaiḥ śabdaiḥ pratyekaṃ nirdiśya, upadeśāt - ' vijñātuḥ vijñāteḥ ' iti śrutau upadeśanāt, (jñātṛ-jñānayo: bhedaḥ niyataḥ ) ||

245. tadguṇasāratvāttu tadvyapadeśaḥ prājñavat tadguṇasāratvāttu - jīvātmanaḥ pradhānabhūtajñānākhyaguṇavatvādeva tadvyāpadeśa: - 'vijñānam' iti padena jīvasya śāstreṣu vyavahāra: yujyate ; ( na tu jñātṛ - jñānayoḥ abhedāt ), prājñavat prājñe paramātmani ānandātmaka. guṇaprādhānyāt ' ānanda: ' iti, jñānaguṇaprādhānyāt ' satyaṃ jñānam ' iti ca vyavahāra iva || 246 yāvadātmabhāvitvācca na doṣastadarśanāt yāvadātmabhāvitvācca- jñānātmakasya dharmasya, ātmanaḥ sattāmavyabhicarya vidyamānatvāt (tathā) svaprakāśatvādapi ca ātmanaḥ, na doṣaḥ - dharmiṇo jīvasya dharmavācakena jñānaśabdena vyapadeśakṛtadoṣasyābhāvaḥ, taddarśanāt- - khaṇḍa-muṇḍā divyaktīḥ avyabhicarya vartamānagotvādidharmāśrayeṣu khaṇḍādidharmiṣu gavādiśabdavyapadeśasya loke dṛṣṭatvāt ||
247. puṃstvādivattvasya sato'bhivyaktiyogāt sataḥ - ātmani jāgradādyāsu sarvāsvapyavasthāsu avyabhicāreṇa vidyamānasya, asya -- jñānātmakasya dharmasya, puṃstvādivattu - saptadhātumaye śarīre janmata eva vartamānasyā'pi saptamadhātoḥ yuvatve eva abhivyaktiriva, abhivyaktiyogāt-indriyasaṃprayogakāle prakaṭāvasthatāmātrasya yujyamānatvāt (ātmanaḥ na jñānaṃ āgantuko dharmaḥ ) ||
248. nityopalabdhyanupalabdhiprasaṅgo'nyatara niyamo vā'nyathā anyathā - uktavaiparītyena ātmanaḥ jñānamātratāyāḥ, sarvatra vidyamānatāyāśca aṅgīkāre, nityopalabdhyanupa- labdhiprasaṅgaḥ – upalabdhyanupalabdhī nityam, sahaiva prasajyeyātām anyataraniyamo vā- athavā ātmanaḥ niyamena upalabdhireva vā, niyameva anupalabdhireva vā prasajyeyātām ||
58. katradhikaraṇam

249. kartā śāstrārthavatvāt kartā - ātmā kartṛtvavānapi (na sāṃkhyamate iva kartṛtvaśūnyaḥ ityabhyupagamanīyam ), śāstrārthavatvāt – yā gopāsanāditraivargikāpavargika- kriyāvidhāyināṃ śāstrāṇāṃ saprayojanatvasya avaśyaṃ vaktavyatvāt ||

250. upādānādvihāropadeśācca kartā - ātmā kartṛtvavāneva, upādānāt - śrutau ātmanaḥ indriyaparigṛhītṛtvopadeśāt jñāpakāt (tathā) vihāropadeśācca – śarīre sañcaraṇa- kartṛtvasya ca śrutau upadeśācca jñāpakāt ||

251. vyapadeśācca kriyāyāṃ na cennirdeśaviparyayaḥ kriyāyāṃ vyapa- deśācca jīvātmani yajñakriyāṃ prati kartṛtvasya 'vijñānaṃ yajña tanute ' iti śrutau spaṣṭaṃ vyavahṛta- svādapi jñāpakāt ātmā - jīvaḥ, kartā kartṛtvavāneva | na cet —' vijñāna ' padena antaḥkaraṇarūpā buddhirevoktā, nātmā iti kathane tu, nirdeśaviparyayaḥ – ' vijñāna' pade prathamāntatvavaiparītyena tṛtīyāntanirdeśaḥ śrutau abhaviṣyat ityetaduttaraṃ cintanīyam ||

252. upalabdhivada niyamaḥ - (ātmanaḥ kartṛtāmanabhyumagamya, pradhāne eva sarvān prati sādhāraṇe kartṛtā cet nikṣipyeta tadā) upalabdhivat – ātmanaḥ sarvagatatvābhyupagame, ātmana upalambhasyeva, aniyamaḥ – karmaphalabhūtasya sukhāde: ayameva bhoktā ; netaraḥ iti niyamasyābhāvaprasaṅgaḥ ||
253. śakti viparyayāt (2 - 3 - 37) – (buddhereva kartṛtvasyā'bhyupagame ) śaktiviparyayāt - ātmano bhoktṛtvaśakteḥ hāniprasaṅgāt ( kartṛtva-bhoktṛtvayoḥ vaiyadhikaraṇyāyogāt ) ātmā - jīvaḥ, kartā kartṛtvāśrayaḥ ||

samādhyabhāvācca - (buddhereva kartṛtvābhyupagame ), samādheḥ – mokṣasādhana- tayā sāṃkhyābhimatasya sāṃkhyā bhimatasya 'prakṛteranyo'hamasmi ityevamākārasya prakṛtiviviktātmajñānasya, abhāvācca – āśrayānupapattiprasaṅgādapi (tadṛśa / nusandhāna kartṛtvamapi nā'tmaniṣṭhamiti, prakṛtau svānyatvasya bādhāt ) ātmā jīvaḥ, kartā kartṛtvāśrayaḥ ||

254.

255. yathā ca takṣobhayathā takṣā - takṣaṇoṃpakaraṇasampannaḥ vardhakiḥ, yathā ca- yenaiva prakāreṇa ubhayathā - icchāyāṃ satyāṃ takṣaṇaṃ karoti, no cet na karoti iti ubhaya- prakāravān tathā ātmā - vāgādyupakaraṇavān jīvaḥ, ubhayathā— saṅkalpe sati kartṛtvavān, - tadabhāve tadabhāvavāṃśceti kartṛtva- tadabhāvobhayavāneva, (ataḥ na sadā kartṛtvanaiyatyam ) ||
59 - parāyattādhikaraṇam
256. parātu tacchruteḥ tat — jīvātmagataṃ kartṛtvam, parāttu - niyantṛbhūtāt

`parasmāt brahmaṇa eva, (paramātmāyattameva na tu pūrṇatayā svāyattam ityarthaḥ) śruteḥ - 'antaḥ praviṣṭaḥ śāstā janānām ' iti parasyaiva śāsitṛtvaśravaṇāt ||

257. kṛtaprayatnāpekṣastu vihitapratiṣiddhāvairthyādibhyaḥ ( 2 - 3 - 41) paraḥ - paramātmā, kṛta prayatnāpekṣastu - jīvātmakṛtaṃ prayatnam prathamapravṛttihetubhūtam udyogaṃ nimittatayā nirīkṣamāṇa eka pravartakaḥ, vihitapratiṣiddhāvaiyarthyādibhyaḥ – śāstragatayoḥ vidhi - niṣedhayoḥ avaiyarthyāvaśyaṃbhāvāt, tathā bhagavatkartṛkanigrahānugraha | bhyāmapi ca ||

asantateścā'vyatikaraḥ asantateca - - ( śarīrabhedena bhedavatāṃ jīvānām aṇutayā) sarvatra anusyūta sambandhābhāvāt, avyatikaraḥ - (vibhunā sarvatra anusyūtatayā sambaddhena paramātmanā saha ) bhogāsāṅkaryam (ata: jīva: brahmāṃśa eva, na brahma ) ||
265. ābhāsa eva ca ābhāsa eva ca - parasya brahmaṇa eva avidyayā svarūpatirodhāne, jīvabhāvakalpane ca ucyamāno hetuḥ, vastuto na hetuḥ ; kintu duṣṭa eva, tathā kalpanaṃ niravadyatvaśrutiviruddhañca (ataḥ jīva: brahmāṃśa eva ; na brahma) ||

264.

266. adṛṣṭāniyamāt ( 2 - 3 50 ) adṛṣṭāniyamāt - (ekasminneva akhaṇḍe brahmaṇi) bhoga- niyāmaka dṛṣṭaniyamasya vaktumaśakyatvāt, avidyākṛtā: doṣā : brahmaṇa: avarjanīyāḥ syuḥ, (ataḥ jīvaḥ brahmāṃśa eva, na brahma) ||
267. abhisandhyādiṣvapi caivam ( 2 - 3 51 ) abhisandhyādiṣvapi ca- adṛṣṭahetutayā ukteṣu saṅkalpādiṣvapi ca, evam adṛṣṭe cintisya aniyamasya avarjanīyatvāt brahmaṇi doṣa - niyāmakā bhāva eva, ( ataḥ jīva: brahmāṃśa eva ; na brahma) ||
268. pradeśabhedāditi cennāntarbhāvāt pradeśabhedāt- (brahmaṇi avidyā - kalpitajīvabhāve) upādhyupahita brahmapradeśaḥ, tadunupahitabrahma pradeśa: iti pradeśabhedasyāṅgīkārāt, bhogavyavasthā yujyate, iti cet evaṃ kalpanaṃ tu na – saprayojanaṃ na bhavati, antarbhāvāt- upādheḥ sthānāntaragamane anupahitabrahmapradeśo'pi upahitabrahmapradeśa eva bhavatīti, pradeśabhedātmaka- niyāmakasyābhāvāt (bhogavyavasthāyāḥ abhāva eva, ataḥ jīvo, brahmāṃśa eva; na brahma) ||

_

|| iti dvitīyādhyāye tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ ||

270. gauṇyasambhavāttatprākchuteśca gauṇī - ' prāṇā:' iti śabde sarvaprāṇana- hetuparamātmapare bahuvacanaśrutiḥ aupacārikī, asambhavāt — sṛṣṭiprākkā like paramātmani mukhyasya bahutvasya bādhāt tatprākteśca – tasya - paramātmanaḥ sṛṣṭiprākkālavṛttitvaśrutereva ||

271 tatpūrvakatvādvācaḥ 2-4 - 3) vācaḥ - paramātmavyatiriktasya nāmnaḥ, tatpūrvakatvāt - viyadādisṛṣṭipūrvakatvāt || ataḥ prāṇaśabdanirdiṣṭaḥ paramātmaiva ) ||

jīvopakaraṇameva, tatsahaśiṣṭyādibhyaḥ – jīvopakaraṇabhūtaḥ cakṣurādibhiḥ saha sāhacaryeṇopadeśāt mukhyaprāṇaśabdena viśiṣyopadeśācca jñāpakāt ||
278. akaraṇatvācca na doṣastathā hi darśayati ( 2 - 4 - 10 ) (asya śreṣṭha prāṇasya) akaraṇatvāt –akriyatvāt, (jīvaṃ prati upakāraviśeṣātmaka kriyārāhityanibandhanatayā udbhāvya- mānaḥ ), doṣa: cakṣurādisajātīyatvābhāvarūpo doṣaḥ, na na āpatati, hi yataḥ, tathā- śarīrendriyadhāraṇātmakopakāraviśeṣarūpāṃ kriyāmeva darśayati' yasminnutkrānte ' ityādinā śrutireva bodhayati ||

279. pañcavṛttirmanovavyapadiśyate śreṣṭhaḥ prāṇaḥ - mukhyaḥ prāṇaḥ, pañcavṛttiḥ - prāṇādivṛttipañcakādhīnanā mapañcakavān vyapadiśyate - śrutiṣu nirdiśyate, manovat - yathā ekameva manaḥ vṛttibhedena kāma-saṅkalpādinānānāmabhi: 'kāmaḥ saṅkalpaḥ iti śrutau spaṣṭaṃ nirdiśyate tadvat ||

sarvaṃ mana eva

65. śreṣṭhāṇutvādhikaraṇam

aṇuśca śreṣṭhaḥ prāṇaḥ ayaṃ prāṇaḥ mukhyaḥ, aṇuśca - indriyavat aṇuparimāṇavānapi gateḥ śrutau utkrāntyādeḥ śravaṇāt ||
66. jyotirādyadhiṣṭhānādhikaraṇam
281. jyotirādyadhiṣṭhānaṃ tu tadāmananāt prāṇavatā śabdāt prāṇavatā- jīvena saha, jyotirādyadhiṣṭhānam – agnyādidevatānām indriyeṣu svasvakāryābhimukhyā pādānarūpaṃ kartṛtvam, tadāmananāttu–tasya paramapuruṣasya saṅkalpādeva bhavati, śabdāt —– tatsaṅkalpādhīnatva - pratipādakāt śāstrāt ||

280.

tasya ca nityatvāt tasya - cetanā cetanātmakānāṃ sarveṣāṃ paramātmā- viṣṭhitatvasya, nityatvācca niyatatvena avarjanīyatvādeva ukto'rtha eva samīcīnaḥ ||
67. indriyādhikaraṇam
283. ta indriyāṇi tadvyapadeśādanyatra śreṣṭhāt śreṣṭhāt anyatra - mukhyaṃ prāṇaṃ varjayitvā prāṇāḥ prāṇaśabdavācyāḥ ye vidyante, te indriyāṇita eva indriyasaṃjñakā ni bhavanti, tadvyapadeśāt — teṣveva indriyaśabdasya ' ta etasyaiva sarve rūpamabhavan' iti śrutau

282.

284. bhedaśrutervailakṣaṇyācca bhedaśruteḥ prāṇe manassahitebhya indriyebhyo bhedasya ' etasmājjāyate prāṇo manaḥ sarvendriyāṇi ca ' iti śrutau pratipannatvāt, (tathā) vailakṣaṇyācca indriyavṛttivilakṣaṇāyāḥ vṛtteḥ prāṇeṣu darśanādapi te - mukhyaprāṇātiriktāḥ prāṇā eva, indriyāṇi - indriyapadena vyapadeśyāni ; ( na tu mukhyaḥ prāṇaḥ ) ||
68. saṃjñāmūrtikḷtyadhikaraṇam
285. saṃjñā mūrtiklaptistu trivṛtkurvata upadeśāt saṃjñāmūrtiklaptiḥ - nāmarūpavyākaraṇātmikā vyaṣṭisṛṣṭiḥ, trivṛtkurvataḥ tu — trivṛtkaraṇātmaka kriyānirvoḍhuḥ paramātmana eva; na caturmukhasya, upadeśāt - ' trivṛtaṃ trivṛtamekaikāṃ karavāṇi ' iti saṅkalpayitraiva 'nāmarūpe vyākaravāṇi ' iti saṅkalpitatvasya śrutau upadeśadarśanāt ||
286. māṃsādi bhaumaṃ yathāśabdamitarayośca yathāśabdam - ' annamaśitaṃ tredhā' iti śrutivākyānusāreṇa, māṃsādi-sthūlabhūtāt purīṣāt minnaṃ māṃsam, aṇiṣṭham mano'pi ca, bhaumam —– pārthivameva, itarayośca – ' āpaḥ pītā: ' ' tejo'śitam' iti vākyayorapyevameva ||

287. vaiśeṣyāttu tadvādastadvādaḥ vaiśaiṣyātu ekaikasya bhūtasyā'pi viśeṣa- bhāvasya satvādeva, tadvādaḥ - teṣu teṣu annādīnāṃ pṛthakśabdānāṃ śrutiṣu vyapadeśaḥ ||

- caturthaḥ pādaḥ -
(iti dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ)

289. jyātmakatvāttu bhūyastvāt tryātmakatvāt — apāṃ trivṛtkaraṇena bhūta- trayātmakatvāt, (tathā) bhūyastvātu - apām ādhikyādapi, ( pañcamyāmāhutāvāpa:' ityādau sarveṣām ' ap' śabdenā'bhidhānam ) ||

prāṇagateśca prāṇānām – indriyāṇām, gateśva - ' prāṇo'nūtkrāmati iti gacchatā jīvena saha gamanaśruterapi jñāpakāt jīvaḥ sampariṣvaktaḥ dehatā māpannaiḥ bhūtasūkṣmaiḥ saṃśliṣṭa eva san, raṃhati dehāddehāntaraṃ gacchati ||

291. agnyādigatiśruteriti cenna ; bhāktatvāt agnyādigatiśruteḥ- vāgādirūpāṇāmindriyāṇām agnyādiṣu apyayasya agniṃ vāgapyeti' ityādiṣu śravaṇāt, prāṇagatiḥ indriyāṇāṃ jīvena sākaṃ gamanaśrutiḥ (na mukhyā ; kintu anyathā neyā) iti cet - evaṃ kathanaṃ tu, na—na yuktam, bhāktatvāt - vāgādiśabdānāṃ vāgādyabhimānidevatāsu upacārata: prayuktatvāt ||

292 prathame'śravaṇāditi cenna ; tā eva hyupapatte: prathame – pañcāgnividyāyāṃ dyulokātmakāmgnyadhikaraṇa ke home, aśravaṇāt - apāṃ homakarmatvena śrutau apaṭhanāt ( āpa: bhūtaḥ ntarasaṃsṛṣṭatayā gacchanti ityetat na suvacam ) iti cenna - idaṃ kathanaṃ na yuktam, hi yataḥ, tā eva - āpa eva (dhulokā nihomyaśraddhāpadenocyante) ; upapatteḥ prativacane praśnānuguṇyasya tathā satyeva upapādanīyatvāt ||
293. aśrutatvāditi cennaḥ ; iṣṭādikāriṇāṃ pratīte: aśrutatvāt- (apāmeva gamanaśrutyā) adbhiḥ saṃpariṣvaktajīvagamanasya śrutau apaṭhitatvāt, (jīvo gacchatīti durvacam ) iti cet na – evaṃ kathanaṃ na saṅgacchate, iṣṭādikāriṇām – yāgadānādikāmya- karmakṛtāṃ jīvānām, pratīteḥ - vākye pratyabhijñānāt, 'ap'śabdaḥ apsaṃyukta jīvavācyeva ; na kevala bvācī ||

294. bhāktaṃ vā'nātmavittvāttathā hi darśayati – ('taṃ devā bhakṣayanti' iti, dhūmapathena gatasya devaiḥ bhakṣyatvavacanaṃ ) bhāktaṃ vā aupacārikameva, (devopakaraṇatvābhiprāyameva), anātmavittvāt – (paramapurudhārādhanaveṣatāṃ vinā kevaleṣṭā dikāriṇām ) ātmaveditvābhāvāt tathā hi darśayati śrutiḥ ( ' yathā paśuḥ evaṃ sa devānām ' ) iti devopakaraṇatvameva bodhayati ||

kṛtātyayādhikaraṇam 295. kṛtātyaye'nuśayavān dṛṣṭa-smṛtibhyāṃ yathetamanetraṃ ca kṛtātyaye-

pūrvakarmaṇāṃ bhogena vināśe sati, anuśayavān bhuktaśiṣṭa karmavān cetanaḥ yathetam - ārohaṇaprakāreṇa, anevaṃ ca prakārāntareṇa ca (svargāde: nivartate iti) dṛṣṭa-smṛtibhyām - śruti smṛtibhyāmavagamyate ||

296. caraṇāditi cenna tadupalakṣaṇārtheti kārṣṇājini: caraṇāt- śīlācārādinaiva, ( ramaṇīyādiyoniviśeṣaprāptiḥ ; na tu anuśayāt bhuktaśiṣṭāt karmaṇaḥ) iticet — evaṃ kathanaṃ tu, na-na yuktam, tadupalakṣaṇārtheti - ' ramaṇīyacaraṇā:' iti śrutiḥ) puṇya-pāpātmakakarmajñāpanārthaṃ pravṛtteti, kāṣrṇājiniḥ kāṣrṇājinināmā ācāryo manyate ||

297. ānarthakyamiti cenna tadapekṣatvāt ānarthakyam – (yoni viśeṣa- prāptau puṇyāpuṇyoreva prayojakatve ) sadācāranai phalyam iti cet — evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ tu, na nocitam tadapekṣatvāt – puṇyātmakānāṃ karmaṇāṃ sadācārasāpekṣatvāt, (na ācārānarthakyam ) || 298. sukṛtaduṣkṛte eveti tu bādariḥ sukṛta- duṣkṛte eva - puṇya pāpe eva ' caraṇa 'śabdābhidheye, iti tu ityeva, bādariḥ - bādarināmā ācāryo manyate ||

i

71. aniprādikāryadhikaraṇam
aniṣṭādikāriṇāmapi ca śrutam aniṣṭādikāriṇāmapi - iṣṭāpūrtadi akurva pāpināmapi śrutaṃ ca - ( ' candramasameva te sarve gacchanti' iti ) candragamanameva śrutau bodhitam |
300. saṃyamane tvanubhūyārohavarohau tadgatidarśanāt ārohāvarohau - ( aniṣṭādikāriṇāṃ pāpināṃ ) candrāroha: tasmādavarohaśca, saṃyamane—yamaśāsanaviṣaye pradeśe, anubhūya tu — pāpaphala saṃvedanaṃ prāpyaiva bhavati, tadgatidarśanāt - yamasadanaṃ prati gateḥ (vaivasvataṃ saṅgamanaṃ janānām ) iti śrutyā pratipatteḥ ||
301 smaranti ca smaranti ca parāśarādayo maharṣayaḥ ' sarve caite vaśa - yānti yamasya ' iti yamasadanaprāpti smṛtau bodhayanti ||

302. api sapta saptā'pi - rauravādīn sapta narakānapi smaranti pāpināṃ gantavyatayā munayaḥ smṛtiṣu bodhayanti ||
303. tatrā'pi tavyāpārādavirodhaḥ tatrā'pi - rauravādiṣu narakākhyeṣu saptaṣu lokeṣvapi tadvyāpārāt - yamājñayaiva gamanāt, avirodhaḥ - yamasadanaprāptibodhakavākya- virodho nā'sti ||

304. vidyā - karmaṇoriti tu prakṛtatvāt vidyākarmaṇoḥ arcirādinā brahmagamanasya, vidyāyāḥ phalatvāt dhūmādinā candragamanasya ca iṣṭāpūrtādikarmaṇa: phalatvāt, ( tatphalatvaṃ ca ) iti prakṛtatvāt - itthaṃ vidyā- karmaṇī prakṛtya pratipāditatvāt (vidyā- karmarahitānāṃ pāpināṃ brahmagamanasya candragamanasya cā'sambhavaḥ iti jñāyate ) ||
305. na tṛtīye tathopalabdheḥ tṛtīye - tṛtīyasthānasaṃjñakānāṃ pāpakarmiṇa dehārambhe, pañcamātyapekṣā na vidyate, tathā - pañcamā hutyanapekṣatvena, upalabdheḥ - ' tenā'sau loko na sampūryate ' iti śrutau darśanāt ||
306. smaryate'pi ca loke loke'pi - śiṣṭaloke'pi, smaryate ca- draupadyādīnāṃ puṇyakarmiṇāṃ dehārambhe pañcamāhuteranapekṣā mahābhāratādau smaryata eva ||
307. darśanācca ( svedajodbhijjayoḥ dehārambhe pañcamā hutyanapekṣāyāḥ) darśanācca- ' trīṇyeva bījāni bhavanti āṇḍajaṃ jīvajamudbhijjam' iti śrutau paṭhanādapi jñāpakāt ||
308. tṛtīyaśabdāvarodhaḥ saṃśokajasya saṃśokajasya - svedajasya, tṛtīya- śabdena - udbhijjaśabdena, avarodhaḥ – saṃgraho bhavati ataḥ svedajo'pi śrutipaṭhita eva, ( tasmāt kevala pāpināṃ na candrārohāvaroha sambhavataḥ ) ||

vrīhyādiśarīre, (avarohatāṃ jīvānāṃ saṃśleṣamātrameva ) pūrvavat - ākāśādimeghaparyantavat, abhilāpāt— kevalatadbhāvasyaiva śrutau kathanāt, (bhogakāraṇībhūtakarmākathanāt trīhyādiśarīrā- vacchinnabhogo nāsti ityarthaḥ) ||

312. aśuddhamiti cenna śabdāt aśuddham – ( yāgādijanyaṃ svargādikaṃ phalam - paśuhiṃsājanyatvāt ) aśuddhimiśram, (tena trīhyādyaśuci janma heturbhavati) iti cet- evaṃ śaṅkanam na na saṅgatam, śabdāt - hiṃsātvābhāvabodhakāt ' na vā u vetantriya se na riṣyasi ' ityevamādeḥ śrautāt śabdāt ||
313. retassigyogo'tha atha - - tadanantarabhāvī, retassimyogaḥ - avarohatāṃ jīvānām annena saṃsṛṣṭānāṃ retassigbhiḥ dehibhiḥ saha uktaḥ sambandhaḥ, yathā kevalaṃ yogamātraṃ, tatha vrīhyādiyogo'pi sambamdhamātrameva ||
314. yoneḥ śarīram yone: - avarohato jīvasya yoniprāpteranantarameva, śarīram - śarīravattā bhavati; na tu tataḥ prākū ||

|| iti tṛtīyādhyāye prathamaḥ pādaḥ ||

318. parābhidhyānātu tirohitaṃ tato hyasya bandhaviparyayau || parābhidhyānātu- paramapuruṣa saṅkalpādeva, asya jīvasya svābhāvikaṃ rūpam, tirohitam – anādikarmapravāheṇa sāparādhatayā ācchāditam, tato hi paramapuruṣasaṅkalpādeva hi, asya jīvasya, bandhaviparyayau – saṃsārāpavargoṃ śrutau yete ||

319. dehayogādvā so'pi so'pi - jīvātmanaḥ tirodhānasambandho'pi dehasya - devādyākārapariṇatasyā'citaḥ, (tathā) nāma-rūpavibhāgānarhasūkṣmadaśāpannasyā'citaśca yogāt- (sṛṣṭi-pralayakālikāt ) saṃsargāt bhavati ||

sūcakaśca hi śruterācakṣate ca tadvidaḥ hi – yataḥ sandhyam - sandhyapadavācyaḥ svapnaḥ, sūcakaścana kevalaṃ puruṣaviśeṣasya anubhava viṣayaḥ, bhāvinoḥ śubhāśubhayoḥ arthayoḥ sūcako'pi iti śruteḥ – 'striyaṃ svapneṣu paśyati | samṛddhiṃ tatra tadvidaśva – svapnādhyāyavido'pi, ācakṣate - enamevārtha

320.

jānīyāt' iti śruteḥ jñāyate |

vadanti | (īśvarasyaiva svāpnārthasraṣṭṛtve eva idaṃ sarvamupapadyate ) ||

mugdhādhikaraṇam

78.

324. mugdhe'rdhasampattiḥ pariśeṣāt mugdhe - mūrcchite puruṣe vidyamānā avasthā ardha sampattiḥ – ardhamṛtireva, pariśeṣāt - jāgrat - svapna suṣupti-maraṇātmakāvasthā bhinnāvasthāviśeṣa- rūpatvāt ||

āha ca tanmātram tanmātram - tāvanmātram - 'brahma jñānasvarūpam ityetāvanmātram āha ca ' satyaṃ jñānam' iti vākyaṃ pratipādayati ca ( na tu brahmaṇa: kalyāṇaguṇākaratāṃ niṣedhati) ato brahma ubhayaliṅgameva ||

330.

331. darśayati cāthoṃ api smaryate darśayati ca brahmaṇaḥ ubhayaliṅgatvaṃ niṣkalaṃ niravadyaṃ nirañjanam' 'svābhāvikī jñānabalakriyā ca' ityādiḥ vedāntavākyagaṇaḥ spaṣṭaṃ pratipādayati ca, smaryate'pi – 'yo māmajamanādi ca vetti lokamaheśvaram' ityādismṛtāvapi brahmagatam ubhayaliṅgatvaṃ pratipādyate ||

||

332. ata eva copamā sūryakādivat ata eva brahmaṇaḥ ubhayaliṅgatvena pṛthivyādyavasthānaprayuktadoṣagandhābhāvādeva, sūryakādivat — jala - darpaṇādipratibimbita ravimaṇḍalā- divat iti, upamā - ' jalādhāreṣvivāṃśumān' ityā dinā sādṛśya varṇanaṃ śāstreṣu kriyate ||

ambuvadagrahaṇāṇu na tathātvam - ( paramātmanaḥ ) tathātvam - sūrya prati- bimbādisāmyam, na - na sambhavati, ambuvat - jale vastutaḥ avidyamānasya ravimaṇḍalasyeva, agrahaṇāttu — grahaṇābhāvādeva ; ( paramātmā hi pṛthivyādiṣu vastuto vidyamāna eva khalu gṛhyate) || 334 vṛddhihāsabhāktvamantarbhāvādubhayasāmāñjasyādevaṃ darśanāca anta- rbhāvāt - pṛthivyādivikāreṣu paramātmanaḥ avasthānamātrāt vṛddhihāsabhāktvam - paramātmanaḥ vikārabhūtavastugatopacayāpacayabhāgitvam na-na āpatati ubhayasāmañjasyāt — ghaṭākāśa- jalādhāragatasūryātmaka dṛṣṭāntadvayasāṅgatyāya evaṃ varṇanīyatvāt evaṃ vivakṣitāṃśe eva sāmya- mityatra, darśanācca – 'siṃha iva māṇavaka:' ityādau parākramādyaṃśe eva sāmyapradarśanasya dṛṣṭānto- papādakavākyeṣu upalambhādapi ||

333.

335. prakṛtaitāvaccaṃ hi pratiṣedhati tato bravīti ca bhūyaḥ hi - yataḥ prakṛtatāvatvaṃ - mūrta mūrtarūpātmakapūrvaprakāntaviśeṣavattākṛtā yā iyattā tāvanmātram, prati- ṣedhati - 'neti neti' iti śrutiḥ niṣedhati, tato bhūyaḥ - tadapekṣayā adhikaguṇayogitvamapi bravīti ca - 'na hyetasmāditi' ityādinā śrutirapi pratipādayatyeva khalu ||

tadavyaktamāha hi tat - brahma, avyaktam — pramāṇāntarāprajñātamiti, āha hi - ' na sandṛśe tiṣṭhati rūpamasya, iti śrutireva dṛḍhaṃ vadati khalu ||
337. api saṃrādhane pratyakṣānumānābhyām api api ca, saṃrādhane- samyakprītiyukta bhajane, upāsane kṛte satyeva, tat - brahma, sākṣātkriyate; nā'nyathā iti,

pratyakṣeṇa - 'nāgyamātmā .... yamevaiṣa vṛṇute tena labhyaḥ' ityādikayā śrutyā, anumānena - 'bhaktyā tvananyayā śakyaḥ' iti smṛtyā ca jñāpyate ||

81. parādhikaraṇam

344. paramatasse tūnmānasambandhandhabhedavyapadeśebhyaḥ ataḥ - śāstrārambhataḥ etadantaṃ paramakāraṇatayā nirṇītāt asmāt parasmāt brahmaṇo'pekṣayā, param – utkṛṣṭamapi kiñcit tattvam asti ; setuvyapadeśāt - 'etaṃ setuṃ tīrtvā' iti paramātmanaḥ setutvavyapadeśapūrvakaṃ taritavyatvajñāpanāt, unmānavyapadeśāt – 'catuṣpāt brahma' iti pādacatuṣṭayātmakapramāṇapari- mitatvavyapadeśapūrvakaṃ aparimitavastvantarasattāyā: jñāpanāt, sambandhavyapadeśāt – jīvātmana: prāpyaṃ sthānāntaraṃ prati prāpayitṛtvasya 'amṛtasyaiṣa setuḥ iti kathanena jīveśvarayoḥ jīvaprāpyā- mṛtatvaprāpakatvarūpasambandhakathanapūrvakaṃ etaditarasya prāpyatvajñāpanāt, bhedavyapadeśāt- sarvatra pūrṇavastvapekṣayā anyasmin vastuni bhedasya ' tato yaduttarataram' ityādinā vyapadeśādapi jñāpakāt ||
345. sāmānyācu | setuvyapadeśaḥ paramātmani ' sa setu: ' iti setupadavyavahāraḥ, sāmānyāttu - vidhṛtirūpāt setusādṛśyādeva, (asāṅkaryeṇa sarvapadārtha svabhāva- saṃrakṣaṇakṛta setusāmyavatvāt ||
346 buddhayarthaḥ pādavat || unmānavyapadeśa: - ' catuṣpādbrahma' ityādiṣu brahmaṇaḥ yatkiñcitpramāṇaparimitatvavyavahāraḥ, pādavat - ' vāk pāda: ' ityādinā brahmaṇi akhaṇḍe vāgādīnāṃ pādatvavyavahāra iva, buddhyarthaḥ upāsanaphalaka :, ( na tu paricchinnatva- dyotanaphalakaḥ |

347. sthānaviśeṣāt prakāśādivat unmānavyapadeśa: - akhaṇḍasya pūrṇasya paramātmanaḥ vāgādisthānānāṃ kalpanena paricchinnatvavyapadeśaḥ, sthānaviśeṣāt — vāgādisthāna- viśeṣātmakopādhisambandhāt sambhavati, prakāśādivat sarvatra vitatasyāpi ākāśaprakāśādeḥ yathā vātāyanaghaṭādisthānasambandhaḥ tadvat ||
348. upapatteśca || ( ' amṛtasyaiṣa setuḥ' iti prāpyaprāpakabhāvātmakasya atiriktatājñāpakatvābhimatasya sambandhasya vyapadeśo'pi ), upapatteśca – (svasyaiva svaṃ prati prāpakatvasya ' yamevaiṣa vṛṇute ' iti śrutijñāpyasya) ekasminneva abādhena sambhavādapi hetoḥ yujyata iti, saḥ na atiriktatvajñāpakaḥ ||
349. tathā'nyapratiṣedhāt || anyasya - jagatkāraṇatayā pratipāditāt

brahmaṇo'nyasyā'dhikasya, pratiṣedhāt – ' yasmātparaṃ nāsparamasti' ityādinā abhāvabodhanāt, tathā—' brahmaṇaḥ paraṃ nā'sti ; brahmaiva param' ityeva samyak ||
350. anena sarvagatatvamāyāmaśabdādibhyaḥ || anena - parabrahmaṇā, sarvagatatvam – sarvasya jagata: vyāptatvam āyāmaśabdādibhyaḥ - sarvavyāptivācinā ' tenedaṃ pūrṇa puruṣeṇa sarvam' ityādinā śrutivākyena (tathā) brahmaivedaṃ sarvam' ityādinā ca vijñāyate ||

codanāde: - codanā - saṃyoga-rūpa ākhyānām, aviśeṣāt - samānatvāt ||
356. bhedānneti cedekasyāmapi bhedāt — aviśeṣapunaḥ śrutyā bhedasyāva - gamāt, na-daharā dināṃ vidyānām aikyaṃ na bhavati, iti cet — evaṃ kathanaṃ tu na samyakū ; ekasyāmapi — ekatvena avipratipannāyāmapi vidyāyām bhedāt- pratipattaṇāṃ bhedāt (śākhāntare aviśeṣapunaḥ śravaṇaṃ sārthakaṃ sat vidyābhedaṃ na niyamayati ) ||

357. svādhyāyasya tathātve hi samācāre'dhikārācca savavacca tanniyamaḥ svādhyāyasya - paramparāyātasya adhyeyaśākhā viśeṣasya, tathātve hi tathātvasiddhyarthaṃ hi, adhyayanajanyasaṃskāra bhāktvasiddhayartha hi, tanniyamaḥ - ātharvaṇikeṣu śirasiṃ aṅgārapātradhāraṇa- rūpasya vrataviśeṣasya adhyayanāṅgatayā adhyetṝṇāṃ niyamaḥ ; na upāsitṝṇāṃ niyamaḥ, samācāre- samācārākhye ātharvaṇikānāṃ granthaviśeṣe, adhikārāt ca- 'vedavrata' śabdena śirovratasya prastāvādapi, (cakārāt ) ' naitadacīrṇavrato'dhīyīta' iti muṇḍakānte vratānuṣṭhānasyā'dhyayanaprābhā- vitā niyamokterapi jñāyate, (tathā) savavacca - savanāmakeṣu ādharvaṇi kaikāgnisambandhiṣu home- vivaca, ( tasmāt na niyama viśeṣakṛno vidyā bhedaḥ) ||
358. darśayati ca— (śruti: upāsanasya sarvavedāntapratyayatvam ) darśayati ca- 'dahaṃ .. . stadupāsitavyam' ityevaṃrūpā taittirīyaśrutirapi spaṣṭaṃ prakāśayati ca ||
359. upasaṃhāro'rthāṃ bhedādvidhiśeṣavatsamāne ca samāne ca sarveṣvapi vedānteṣu daharādyupāsane abhinne, ekatvavati sati, samāne abhinna tasmiṃtasmin upāsane, upasaṃhāraḥ–vedāntāntare upāsyadharmatayā śrutānāṃ guṇānāmupāsyatāvacchedakatayā svīkāra : kārya: ; arthābhedāt – upakārātmakasya prayojanasya ekarūpatvāt vidhiśeṣavat - tattadvidyāvidhiśeṣatayā tattadvidyāyāṃ vihitaguṇānāmiva ||

na vā prakaraṇabhedāt parovarīyastvādivat navā - aviśeṣaṃ hetukṛtya vidyakyopapādanaṃ) naiva saṅgatam prakaraṇabhedāt- (udvīthāvayavabhūtapraṇava prastāvena udvīthopāsanaṃ, praṇavopāsanam, praṇavamaprastutya kṛtsnasya udgīthasya prastāvena udvīyopāsanaṃ na praṇavasyopāsanam ; kintu udgīthasyaiva, iti) prastāvabhedāt upāsthasvarūpe bhedo'styeveti na upāsanaikyam, parovarīyastvādivat - yathā udgīthāvayavabhūte praṇave hiraṇmaya puruṣadṛṣṭividhānaṃ kvacit, anyatra parovarīyastvādiguṇaviśiṣṭadṛṣṭividhānamiti ekaśākhāgate eva udgīthopāsane, paramātma- dṛṣṭitvāviśeṣe'pi rūpabhedāt bhedaḥ, tadvat viśeṣasyaiva vaktavyatvāt ||

361.

362. saṃjñātaścettaduktamasti tu tadapi saṃjñātaḥ - ' udgīthavidyā iti ekasaṃjñārūpāt jñāpakāt, tat — vidheyabhūtodgīthopāsanayoḥ abhinnatvam uktam - asmābhi- varṇitam, cet — ityevaṃ śaṅkanaṃ tu, na vā naiva samañjasam (yataḥ ) tadapi - karmakāṇḍīye nityāgnihotre māsāgnihotre ca vidheyabhede'pi 'agnihotram' iti saṃjñaikyamapi, asti tu- vartata eva khalu ||

363. vyāpteśca samañjasam || vyāpteśtha - (chāndogyaprathamaprapāṭhakasthodgītha- vidyāsu upakrame iva ante'pi udgīthāvayavapraṇavasyaiva ) upāsyatva sambandhādapi, samañjasam - tanmadhyapatite ' devā udgīthamājahuḥ' ityasmin udgīthaśabde'pi praṇavaviṣayakatvameva yuktatamam ||

śiraḥpakṣapucchādirūpe mithovyāvṛtte viśeṣe'pi brahmasvarūpāntarbhūte sati hi upacayāpacayau— (brahmasvarūpe'pi ) śrutiviruddhau vṛddhi - hāsau prasajyeyātām ||
367. itare tvarthasāmānyāt itare tu — ānandādayaḥ brahmasvarūpanirūpakā dharmāstu, (sarvavidyāsūpasaṃhāryā eva), arthasāmānyāt - sarvavidyāsu upāsyaḥ brahmarūpo yo'rthaḥ tasya samānatvāt ||
368. ādhyānāya prayojanābhāvāt ( priya śirastvādirūpaṇam ) ādhyānāya - anucintana siddhyarthameva, prayojanābhāvāt - (anucintanopayogi buddhayāroha| tiriktasya) prayojanasya virahāt ||

369. ātmaśabdācca ātmaśabdācca (priyaśirastvā dinā ānandamaye upāsye) ' ātma 'śabdasya anyontara ātmānandamayaḥ iti vyapadeśādapi jñāpakāt ( priyaśirastvādiḥ na brahmasvarūpanirūpako dharmaḥ ) ; kintu anucintanārtha eva ||

rūpite

370 iti

ātmagṛhītiritarakhaduttarāt ātmagṛhīti : ' anyo'ntara ātmā ātma 'śabdena paramātmana eva grahaṇam, itaravat – ' ātmā va idameka eva ityādinā anyenānyena ātmaśabdena paramātmanaḥ grahaṇavat, uttarāt — ānandamayaviṣayāt

' so'kāmayata' ityādyuttaravākyājjñāpakāt ||

371. anvayāditi cet; syādavadhāraṇāt anvayāt – (prāṇamayādi- vākyeṣu) anātmasvapi ātmaśabdānvayāt ( uttaravākyena na ātmaśabdasya paramātmaparatājñaptiḥ) iti cet — evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ tu na yuktam avadhāraṇāt — sarvatra tatprakaraṇasthena ātmaśabdena 'paramātmaiva gṛhyate iti nirṇayāt syāt — uttaravākyasya tajjñāpakatā siddhayedeva ||

88. samānādhikaraṇam

373. samāna evaṃ cā'bhedāt evaṃ ca - yathā ammirahasye evaṃ vṛhadāraṇya - ke'pi, samāne – upāsyasya brahmaṇo guṇabhūte manomayatvādau abhinne sati, abhedāt- adhikasya vaśitvādeḥ satyasaṅkalpatvābhedāt (rūpabhedābhāvena ubhayatra pāṭhe'pi śāṇḍilyavidyāyāḥ aikyameva ||
89. sambandhādhikaraṇam
374. sambandhādevamanyatrā'pi evam samāna guṇaviśiṣṭasya upāsyasya samānatve vidyaikyavat, anyatrāpi - akṣyā dityomayādhiṣṭhānake brahmaṇi upāsyespi, sambandhāt- ekasyaiva satyasya brahmaṇaḥ akṣyādityākhyasthānadvayasambandhāt, (rūpabhedābhāvena vidyāyā apya- bhedāt 'ahaḥ ' ' aham' iti dvayorapi nāmnoḥ aniyamena ubhayorapyupāsanayoḥ

upasaṃhāraḥ ) ||

375- na vā viśeṣāt navā - ( vidyāyā aikyat ubhayorapyaharahaṃ nāmnoḥ) ubhayatropasaṃhāraḥ ) ityetanna sambhavati, viśeṣāt – vidyābhedakasya upagamyagatarūpabhedasya akṣyā- dityasthānātmakasya spaṣṭaṃ pratīteḥ ||
376. darśayati ca darśayati ca – (akṣyādhāre, ādityādhāre ca brahmaṇi upāsye mitho guṇopasaṃhārābhāvaṃ ) śrutiḥ sphuṭaṃ pratipādayati khalu . ( ' tasyaitasya tadeva rūpaṃ yat amuṣya rūpam ' iti rūpātideśena ) ato vidyābheda eva atreti ||

bāva yajñaḥ' itīdṛśyāṃ ca puruṣavidyāyām, (rūpabhedāyattaḥ vidyābhedaḥ astyeva ) itareṣām --- anyaśākhoditānāṃ yajamāna - patnyādirūpāṇām, tathā savanādīnāṃ ca anāmnānāt - - itarāsu śākhāsu apratipādanāt ||

92. vedhādyadhikaraṇam

379. vedhādyarthabhedāt adhyayanārambhe adhīyamānāḥ mantrāḥ adhyayanāṅgabhūtāḥ eva; na vidyāṅgabhūtāḥ, vedhādyarthabhedāt – śukraṃ pravidhya hṛdayaṃ pravidhya' ityādyuktaṣu śatruvīryahṛdaya- vidāraṇādiṣvartheṣvava 'śaṃno mitraḥ' ityādimantragateṣu adhyayana sambandhādirūpeṣvartheṣvapi bhedasya susthatvāt ||

taritavyasya kasyā'pi bhogaśeṣasya abhāvāt, anye – chandogāḥ, tathā hi-uktayā rītyaiva, adhīyate—'tasya tāvadeva ciraṃ yāvanna vimokṣye atha sampatsye' iti paṭhanti ||

382. chandata ubhayāvirodhāt ubhayāvirodhāt – 'aśva iva romāṇi vidhūya pāpam' iti, 'tamya tāvadeva ciram' iti ca dvayoḥ śrutyoḥ virodhābhāvaṃ sañcintya, chandataḥ - iṣṭānurodhena (tatsukṛta- duṣkṛte dhūnute' iti vākyagaṇḍa : saṅgamanīyaḥ ; 'etaṃ devayānam ' iti vākyāt prākū yojayitavyaḥ) ||
383. gaterarthavattvamubhayathā'nyathā hi virodhaḥ gateḥ – devayānena pathā viduṣo gatiśruteḥ, arthavattvam — saprayojanatvam ubhayathā - dehaviyogakāle adhvani ceti dvayyā rītyā karmakṣayāṅgīkāre eva yujyate, anyathā - dehaviyogakāle eva sarvakarmakṣayāṅgīkāre sati, virodho hi devayānagatiśruteḥ vyāghāto hi prasajyate ||
384. upapannastallakṣaṇārthopalabdherlokavat (anyathā'pi - dehaviyogakāle eva sarvakarmaṇāṃ kṣaye'pi ), upapannaḥ - ( bhagavantamupāsīnānāṃ devayānagatividhiḥ, saṅgata eva ) tallakṣaṇārthasya – akarmalabhyajātīyasya arthasya, upalabdheḥ 'sa svarāḍ bhavati' ityādiśrutyā tasmin avagamāt lokavat — lokadṛṣṭanarendropāsa ke puruṣe yathā prākṛtapuruṣasādhāraṇyaṃvinā acintitānāṃ bhogānāmapi sambhavaḥ, tadvat ||

385. yāvadadhikāramavasthitirādhikārikāṇām ādhikārikāṇām – karma- viśeṣeṇa adhikāraviśeṣaṃ prāptānāṃ jñānināmapi vasiṣṭhādīnām yāvadadhikāram - karmalabdhādhi- kārasamāptiṃ yāvat avasthitiḥ– adhikāre avasthānameva ; na dehapālānantarameva arcirādikā gatiḥ ||

95. aniyamādhikaraṇam

386. aniyamaḥ sarveṣāmavirodhaḥ śabdānumānābhyām sarveṣām – sarvavidhopā- sananiṣṭhānāmapi, upakosalādividyotā arcirādikāgatiḥ asti (ataḥ ) aniyamaḥ —upakosala- vidyāniṣṭhānāmeveti niyamābhāva:, ( evaṃ satyeva ca ) śabdānumānābhyām – 'ye ceme'raṇye ' -ityādiśrutyā, 'abhijrjyotiraha: śukla:' iti smṛtyā ca saha, avirodhaḥ virodhābhāvaḥ ||

ityuktadharmaprakārakadhiyām, avarodhastu - sarvāsvapi vidyāsu saṅgrahaṇam kāryameva, sāmānya-tadbhāvābhyām – brahmaṇaḥ upāsyasya sarvāsu vidyāsu ekatvāt, (tathā) tāsāṃ sarvāsāṃ dhiyāṃ brahmānusandhāne antarbhāvācca, aupasadavat – upasadākhyakarmāṅgabhūtamantravat, ( guṇānāṃ pradhānānuvartitvāt tasya sāmavedapaṭhita- tvena 'uccaiḥ sāmnaḥ ityuktasya uccanasya āvaśyakatve'pi yajurvedikopasatkarmāṅgatvena- • upāṃśu yajuṣā' ityuktopāṃśusvaraprāptivat ) tat - guṇānāṃ pradhānānuvartitvam uktam - karma- kāṇḍe pūrvamīmāṃsāyāṃ ' guṇamukhya vyatikrame ityatra spaṣṭīkṛtam ||

akṣaradhiyām - bhakṣaraparabrahma sambandhinīnām

asthūlamanaṇu

,.

iyadāmananāt iyat – (sarvavidyāsu brahmasambandhitayā anusandheyam ) etāvadeva ; āmananāt -- brahmaṇa ābhimukhyena mananāt — brahmānusandhānarūpatvāt heto: (yāvad- guṇajātaṃ vinā brahmānusandhānameva na niṣpadyate tāvanmātram anusandheyam ityarthaḥ) ||
