97. antaratvādhikaraṇam

antarābhūtagrāmavat svātmano'nyathā bhedānupapattiriti cennopadeśavat antarā - (upasta praśna ' ya ātmā sarvāntara: ' iti sarvāntaratvena pṛṣṭasya ) ' eṣa ta ātmā sarvāntara: ' iti prativacane sarvāntaratvena nirdeśaḥ, bhūtagrāmavatsvātmanaḥ - bhūtagrāmaśabditā- cetanāntaryāmijīvātmaviṣayakaḥ i na paramātmaviṣayakaḥ anyathā - paramātmaviṣayakatve.. bhedasya - ' yaḥ prāṇena prāṇiti' iti, ' yo'śanāyā pipāsādyatītaḥ' iti ca pratyuttaradvayasya, anupapattiḥ - asāṅgatyam (upāsyasya brahmaṇa: ekatvāt ) iti cet — evaṃ cintanaṃ tu, na- nocitam upadeśavat - sadvidyāgate śvetaketuṃ pratyupadeśe 'bhūya eva mā bhagavān vijñāpayatu ' iti praśnāvṛttivat atrā'pi prativacanabhedasya saṅgatatvāt ||

389.

390. vyatihāro viśiṣanti hītaravat hi yataḥ, viśiṣanti (yājñavalkyavacanāni ekameva paramātmānaṃ sarvaprāṇiprāṇana hetutva- aśanāyādyatītatvādibhiḥ dharmaiḥ ) pratyagātmano vyāvṛttatayā avagamayanti ; ataḥ, vyatihāra : - ( brāhmaṇadvaya śratānām ) itaretaratra upasaṃhāraḥ kartavyaḥ, itaravat — itarasyāṃ sadvidyāyāṃ sarvaprativacanagatānāṃ sarveṣāṃ dharmāṇāṃ sarvatropasaṃhāravat, (ato nā'tra vidyābhedaḥ) ||
391. saiva hi satyādayaḥ hi yataḥ, saiva - sacchandābhihitā parā devataiva, (' tatsatyaṃ sa ātmā' iti sarvatrānuvṛttā') satyādayaḥ – satyatvādayaśca dharmāḥ (navasvapi khaṇḍeṣu anuvartamānāḥ upāsyābhedaṃ bodhayantīti, ataḥ 'itaravat' iti pūrvasūtroko dṛṣṭāntaḥ sustha eva) ||

98. kāmādyadhikaraṇam

392. kāmādītaratra tatra cā''yatanādibhyaḥ ( 3 3 - 38 ) itaratra tatra ca - vājasaneyake chāndogye ca kāmādi - satyakāmatvādiviśiṣṭaṃ brahmaiva upāsyam (dahara vidyāyām ) āyatanā- dibhyaḥ – hṛdayāyatanatva - satyasaṅkalpatva viśeṣarūpavaśitvādibhyaḥ (vidyaikyapratyabhijñānāt jñāpakāt ) | 393. ādarādalopaḥ ādarāt - śrutyekasamadhigamyasya vaśitvādeḥ brahmaṇi ādareṇa bhūyo bhūyaḥ pratipādanādeva, alopaḥ- dahara vidyādiṣu paṭhitānāṃ brahmaguṇānāmapratiṣedhaḥ bhāvanīyaḥ ||
394. upasthite'tastadvacanāt upasthite – brahmopasampanne, ataḥ brahmopa- sampareva hetoḥ, tadvacanāt —— sarvalokakāmacārasya śrutāvuktatvāt (saguṇopāsanaṃ na sāṃsārika phala- pradam ) ||

upalambhāt, (ayam anuvākaḥ sarvaparavidyopāsyaparatatvanirdhāraṇaparaḥ), taddhi – liṅgameva hi, balīyaḥ - vākya pramāṇāpekṣayā atiśayena balavat, tadapi - vākyāpekṣayā liṅgasya balīyastvamapi, uktam - karmakāṇḍe pūrvamīmāṃsāyām -' śrutiliṅgavākya' ityatra samyak pratipāditam ||
102. pūrvavikalpādhikaraṇam
398. pūrvavikalpaḥ prakaraṇāt syāt kriyā mānasavat ( 3 3 - 34 ) ( vājasaneya kā mi- rahasyoktaḥ manaścitādiḥ manoniṣpādyo'pyagniḥ ) kriyā syāt – kriyāmayakratvanupraviṣṭa eva bhavitumarhati ; na vidyāmayakatvanupraṣṭhiṣṭaḥ, prakaraṇāt – kriyāmayakratvaṅgasya iṣṭakacitā: pūrva- prakṛtatvāt tatsaṃnihitatvāccāsya manaścitādeḥ, (tathā ca) pūrvavikalpaḥ -- pūrvaprastutena iṣṭakacitāgninā vikalpyamānaśca bhavati ayaṃ manaścitādiḥ, mānasavat - dvādaśāhāntargate vividhavākyarahitatayā avivākyasaṃjñake daśame'hani śrutaḥ, manoniṣpādyasvarūpatvāt ' mānasa śabdavācyaḥ grahaviśeṣo yathā kriyāmayakatvanupraviṣṭaḥ, tadvat, (nidarśanamidaṃ vidyāmayasyāspi kriyāmayatve ) ||

399. atideśācca pūrvavikalpaḥ manaścitādīnāmagnīnāṃ pūrveṇa iṣṭakaci- tenāgninā sākaṃ vikalpaḥ, atideśācca iṣṭakacitāgnikāryasya manaścitādau ekaikasminnagnau atidiśyamānatvādapi sidhyati ; ( samāna eva hi vikalpyate | ataḥ manaścitādiḥ ) kriyā syāt - kriyāmaya eva ||
400 vidhaiva tu nirdhāraṇāddarśanācca vidhaiva - manaścitādirabhi: vidyāmaya- ṛtvanupraviṣṭa eva ; na kriyāmayakratvanupraviṣṭaḥ, nirdhāraṇāt - 'te haite vidyācita eva' iti vidyāsampādyatvanirṇayajñāpanāt darśanācca – 'manomayamakriyata' iti śrutivacanācca ||

401. śrutyādibalīyastvācca na bādhaḥ śrutyādeḥ - śrutiliṅgayoḥ vākyasya ca, balīyastvācca prakaraṇāpekṣayā prabalatvena, na bādhaḥ – 'te haite vidyācita eva' iti śrutyāgatasya vidyārūpatvasya iṣṭakacitatvaprakaraṇena kriyāmayatvajñāpakena bādhitatvaṃ na bhavati iti vidyāmayatvameva ||
402. anubandhādibhyaḥ prajñāntara pṛthaktvavaddaṣṭaśca taduktam prajñānta- rāṇām - daharādisaṃjñakānām itarāsāṃ vidyānām, pṛthaktvavat — tatra tatra śrūyamāṇebhyaḥ bhedajñāpakebhyo dharmebhyaḥ bhedasiddhivat, anubandhādibhyaḥ – yajñopayogibhyo mānasebhya eva

graha stotra - śāstrebhyaḥ, tathā śrutiliṅgavākyebhyaśca jñāpakībhūtebhyaḥ pṛthaktvam - manaścitādeḥ kriyāmayakatvapekṣayā bhedaḥ avagamyate iti vidyāmayakratuvidhiḥ atra kalpanīyo bhavati ; dṛṣṭaśca – arthavādasadṛśe'pi vākye kalpito vidhiḥ, 'yadeva vidyayā karoti' ityādau śrutivākye jñāta eva, tat — anuvādasarūpe'pi vākye vidhikalpanam uktam– 'vacanāni tvapūrvatvāt iti karmamīmāṃsāsūtre pratipāditam ||

403. na sāmānyādapyupalabdhermṛtyuvanna hi lokāpattiḥ sāmānyādapi

sa eṣa mṛtyuḥ ya eṣa etasmin maṇḍale puruṣa : ' ityatra maṇḍalapuruṣe mṛtyutvātideśasya saṃhartṛ- tvātmakasamānadharmasambandhamātrādapi upalabdheḥ dṛṣṭatvāt na – kriyāmayakatupraveśarūpaviśeṣa- dharmātideśo na bhavati, mṛtyuvat — mṛtyau vidyamānaṃ sarvasaṃhatṛtvaṃ khalu tatrā'tidiśyate, tadvat atrāpi manaścitāderapi iṣṭakacitavadeva phalavattāyāḥ atideśaḥ iti, na hi lokāpattiḥ- maṇḍalapuruṣe mṛtyulokāpattiḥ na hi atideśena labhyate ||
404. pareṇa ca śabdasya tādvidhyaṃ bhūyastvāccanubandhaḥ pareṇa ca - anantaravartinā ca 'ayaṃ vāva loka:' ityādinā brāhmaṇena, śabdasya – manaścitādyabhidhāyinaḥ śabdasya, tādvidhyaṃ tadvivatvam vidyāmaya pratipādakatvam avagamyate, anubandhastu – manaścitā - dīnāṃ vidyāsaṃpādyānāṃ kriyā prakaraṇe saṃyojanaṃ tu bhūyastvāt - agnyaṅgānāṃ kriyāmayaprakaraṇe ādhikyāt ||

103. śarīre bhāvādhikaraṇam (sū 51, 52 )

405. eka ātmanaḥ śarīre bhāvāt ātmanaḥ -- upāsituḥ pratyagātmano jīvasya, śarīre—dehe, bhāvāt – vartamānatvāt, (kartṛtva- bhoktṛtvādiviśiṣṭameva svasvarūpam upāsanavelāyām anusandheyamiti, eke pūrvapakṣiṇaḥ, (pañcāgnividyādhyāyino vā ) manyante || 406 . vyatirekastadbhāvabhāvitvānna tūpalabdhivat ātmanaḥ – upāsituḥ pratyagātmanaḥ, vyatirekaḥ muktasvarūpe vidyamānaḥ sāṃsārika svarūpādviviktaḥ nirupādhikaḥ apa- hatapātmatvādiḥ ākāra eva upāsyaḥ, na tu śarīre vidyamānasyākāraḥ naivā'nusandheyaḥ, tadbhāve- tādṛśopāsanasadbhāve eva bhāvitvāt vyatiriktasya svasvarūpasya prāpsyamānatvāt, upa- labdhivat-yathāvasthitabrahmānusandhānaniṣṭhasyaiva brahmasvarūpa sākṣātkāralābhavat ||

104. aṅgāvabaddhādhikaraṇam
aṅgāvabaddhāstu śākhāsu hi prativedam aṅgāvabaddhāḥ – tvaṅga-

bhūtodgīthādyāśritā:- tvaṅgā vinābhāvinyaḥ upāsanā:, na śākhāsu – tattadupāsanavidhāyaka- śākhāmātraniyatā na bhavanti ; tu-kintu, hi yataḥ, aṅgāvabaddhā: - śrutyaiva aṅgamātrāvabaddhāḥ, ( ataḥ ) prativedam - sarvāsvapi śākhāsu anvitāḥ bhaveyuḥ ||

mantrādivadvā'virodhaḥ mantrādivadvā- ekaikaśākhāmihitānāṃ ṛtvaṅga- bhūtānāṃ mantrāṇām tanmantrapāṭhaśūnyaśaḥ khāntara vihitatattvanvaya iva ca, avirodhaḥ – udgīthādhu- pāsanānāṃ śākhāntarānvaye'pi virodhābhāva eva ||
105. bhūmajyāyastvadhikaraṇam

bhūmna: – (vaiśvānara-

409 bhūmnaḥ kratuvajjyāyastvaṃ tathā hi darśayati vidyāyāṃ) samastopāsanasya, jyāyastvam - śraṣṭyam, prāmāṇikatvam (na vyastopāsanasya ; ekavākyatvasyāvagamāt ) ṛtuvat - 'vaiśvānaraṃ dvādaśakapālaṃ nirvapet putre jāte ' iti vihite kratau (' yadaṣṭākāpālo bhavatti' iti, tadekadeśasya anuvāda, iva, atrāpi mūrdhādyupāsanaika- deśasya anuvāda eva kṛtaḥ ; na tu vyastopāsanavidhānam | tathāhi darśayati- samastopāsanam, vyastopāsanasyānartha karatvaṃ ca śrutiḥ spaṣṭayati hi ' mūrdhā te vyapatipyat' ityādi kā ||

106. śabdādibhedādhikaraṇam

nānā śabdādibhedāt vidyāḥ sadviyā dahara vidyādyāḥ upāsanātmikā: nānāpadairbodhyā vidyā:, nānā - mitho bhinnasvarūpa : śabdādibhedāt — vidheyabhedahetūnāṃ śabdānta- rādīnāṃ bhinnānāṃ darśanāt | ( ataḥ upāsyamaikye'pi upāsanānāṃ bheda eva ) |
107. vikalpādhikaraṇam

410.

412. kāmyāstu yathākāmaṃ samuccīyeran na vā pūrvahetvabhāvāt kāmyāstu - brahmaprāptivyatiriktaphalā : vidyāstu yathākāmam – yatheṣṭam, samuccīyeran - ekasminneva puruṣe militā bhaveyuḥ ; na vā vikalpyeran vā, pūrvahetvabhāvāt tatphalasyā'parimitaphalatvābhāvāt ( kāmyaphalakeṣu karmasu phalabhūyastvāpekṣayā samuccayaḥ sambhavati ityarthaḥ) ||

yathāśrayabhāvādhikaraṇam
413. aṅgeṣu yathāśrayabhāva: aṅgeṣu – karmāṅgeṣu udgīthādiṣu, āśritatayā varta- mānānām upāsanānām, yathāśrayabhāvaḥ - āśrayībhūtodgīthādivat karmāṅgabhāva eveti, niyamena udgīthopādānaṃ kāryam ||
414. śiṣṭeśca śiṣṭeśva - śāsanādapi ' udgīthamupāsīta ' iti vidhānādapi jñāpakāt, udgīthādyāśritānām upāsanānām, yathāśrayabhāvaḥ – svāśrayavadeva aṅgabhāvaḥ aviruddhaḥ iti, udgīthādyupādānaṃ niyatam |

108.

samāhārāt | udgīthavedanam avaśyaṃ saṃpādyam, samāhārāt - ( udgātṛ- kartṛkavedane naṣṭe hotṛkartṛkena udgīthapraṇavayoḥ ekatva vijñānena ) samādhānasya - taddoṣaparihārasya (' hotṛṣadanādvaivā'pi durudgīthamanusamāharati' iti ) śrutyā uktatvādapi jñāpakāt ||

415.

416. guṇasādhāraṇyaśruteśva guṇasya - upāsanaguṇabhūtasya - upāsanārthasya praṇavasya sādhāraṇyaśruteśca – āśrāvaṇaśaṃsanodvāneṣu sāmānyataḥ anuvṛtteḥ ' teneyaṃ trayī vidyā vartate ' ityādivākye śrutatvādapi jñāpakāt ( udgīthādeḥ karmāṅgatvena upādānaṃ niyatam ) || 417. na vā tatsahabhāvāśruteḥ navā – udgīthādyupāsanānāṃ ṛtvaṅgatayā upādānaniyamo nāstyeva, tatsahabhāvasya – udgīthādeḥ kratuṃ prati aṅgatvasya, aśruteḥ - śruti- siddhatvābhāvāt, (vīryavattaratvarūpaṃ ṛtugataṃ phalaṃ pratyeva aṅgatvasya ' yadeva vidyayā karoti ' ityādivākyāvagatatvena tvaṅgatvābhāvāt ) ||

418. darśanācca darśanācca - sarveṣām ṛtvijāṃ rakṣaṇasya śrutipaṭhitatvadarśanādapi na vā- udgātrāśritavedana niyamābhāva eva ||

|| iti tṛtīyādhyāye tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ ||

vidvānamṛta iha bhavati' ityādiśrutyātmakāt śabdāt avagamyate iti - etādṛśamartham, bādarāyaṇaḥ - bādarāyaṇanāmā ācāryo manyate ||

420. śeṣatvātpuruṣārthavādo yathā'nyeṣviti jaiminiḥ śeṣatvāt vidyāyāḥ kartṛsaṃskāratayā karmāṅgatvena ṛtuśeṣatvāt anyeṣu yathā -- dravya - saṃskāra-karmasviva, puruṣārtha- vādaḥ – ' vidyayā puruṣarthaḥ ' ityayaṃ vādaḥ, arthavādaḥ - vidyāpraśaṃsābhiprāyakaḥ, iti - imamartham, jaimini:- jaiminināmā ācāryaḥ manyate ||

421. ācāradarśanāt ācārasya - karmapradhānasya brahmavidāmanuṣṭhānasya, darśanāt- aśvapatijanakādiṣu vartamānatayā śruti smṛtiṣu jñāpanāt, vidhaiva karmāṅgam ; na vidyāṅgaṃ karma ||
422. tacchruteḥ tat - vidyāyā: karmāṅgatvam śruteḥ– (yadeva vidyayā karoti ' ityatra 'vidyayā iti tṛtīyā vibhaktiśrutirūpāt pramāṇāt, ('karoti' padabodhya kiyāyāṃ viniyogabodhakāt avagamyate) ||
423. samanvārambhaṇāt samanvārambhaṇāt – ekasminneva adhikāriṇi vidyā- karmaṇorubhayorapi sāhityasya taṃ vidyā karmaṇī samanvārabhete' iti śrutyā jñāpanāt ( karmasamā- nādhikaraṇībhūtā vidyā karmāṅgameva ) ||

428. asārvatrikī ( 'yadeva vidyayā karoti' iti śrutiśca), asārvatrikī- sarvavidyā viṣayiṇī na bhavati ; kintu udgīthavidyamātraviṣayiṇīti, ( śrutyā vidyāyāḥ na karmāṅgatā siddhi) ||
429. vibhāgaśśatavat vibhāga: – aṅgāṅgibhāvānāpannayoreva vidyā - karmaṇoḥ ekasmin puruṣe pṛthagavasthitiḥ, (taṃ vidyā karmaṇī samanvārabhete ' ityatrocyate | ) sa ca vibhāgaḥ, śatavat -- kṣetraratnavikrayiṇi puruṣe śatadvayasyānvaye kṣetrārthamekaṃ śatam, ratnārthamekaṃ śatam iti vibhāgavat (sidhyati ) ||

adhyayanamātravataḥ adhyayanamātravataḥ - kevalaṃ niyamavadakṣararāśigrahaṇavata eva, (vidhānāt - 'vedamadhītya ' iti śrutyā karmaṇo vidhānāt ) ' vidyā na karmāṅgam' iti bādarāyaṇamatameva yuktam | ( vidyā khalu na adhyayanamātram ; kintu smṛtipratyayā vṛttirūpā anyaiva ) || 431 nā'viśeṣāt ( 3 - 4 - 13 ) - ( īśāvāsyopanipadi ' kurvanneveha karmāṇi ' iti yāvadāyuṣaṃ karmānuṣṭhānaniyamavidhānam), na – svatantra vidyāṅgaka kāmyaphalasādhanībhūta karmaviṣayakaṃ na bhavitumarhati aviśeṣāt tādṛśārtha viśeṣajñāpakasya viśiṣṭahetorabhāvāt | (' karmāṇi kurvanneva iti sāmānyataḥ karmānuṣṭhānaniyamasya vidyotpattisahakārikarmaviśeṣaviṣayatve'pi vidhi

430.

sārthakyena ; svatantrakarmaviśeṣaviṣayatāyāṃ vinigamakābhāvāt ) ||

432. stutaye'numatirvā stutaye - ( prastutāyāṃ vidyāyāṃ ) praśastatvajñānotpāda- nāya, anumatirvā karma kurvataḥ karmānuṣṭhānābhyanujñānameva ('kurvanneveha karmāṇi ' iti ) kriyate, (praśastatā ca saṃśleṣasvabhāvānāmapi karmaṇāṃ prakṛtavidyāsāhitye sati saṃśleṣābhāva iti | ataḥ vidyāyā: karmāṅgatvābhāva eva ) ||
433. kāmakāreṇa caike eke - vājasaneyinaḥ kāmakāreṇa ca - icchayaiva, (gārhasthya tyāgam - gṛhasthāśramakarmaṇāṃ tyāgapūrvakaṃ pratrajanam, adhīyate - ' kiṃ prajayā kariṣyāmaḥ ' ityādinā paṭhanti ; na hi kadācit aṅgī tyajyeta | ataḥ vidyānāṃ na karmāṅgatvamiti ) || 434. upamarda ca - ( upāsanātmikayā vidyayā ) upamarda ca sarvakarmavināśa- mapi, eke_ātharvaṇikā: ' kṣīyante cā'sya karmāṇi tasmin dṛṣṭe' iti adhīyate, (na hi aṅginaḥ aṅgabhūtena nāśo yuktaḥ iti, vidyāyāḥ na karmāṅgatvam ) ||

435. ūrdhvaretassu ca śabde hi ūrdhvaretassu ca – panyāsināmāśrameṣu ca, (brahmavidyāyāḥ darśanāt teṣu ca karmaṇām agnihotrādīnāmabhāvāt vidyāyāḥ na karmāṅgatā ;

na hi aṅginaṃ vinā aṅgānāmanuṣṭhānaṃ sambhavati), ūrdhvaretasaḥ pravrājināmāśramāśca santyeva ; hi yataḥ śabde – 'etameva pratrājinaḥ' ityevaṃrūpe aupaniṣade śabde śrūyate ||

436. parāmarśa jaiminiracodanāccāpavadati hi acodanāt – ( ' trayo dharmaskandhāḥ yajño'dhyayanaṃ dānam ' ityādiṣu) pravrājināmāśramasya avihitatvāt parāmarśam - śrutau upāsanastutyarthaṃ pravrajyāśramasya anuvādam, na tu vidhānam ) jaiminiḥ – jaiminināmā ācāryaḥ manyate, hi—–yataḥ, ( śabda: śrauta : ' vīrahā vā eṣa devānāṃ yo'gnimudrāsayate ' ityayaṃ śabda: ), apavadati agnihīnāṃ sthiti nindati |

437. anuṣṭheyaṃ bādarāyaṇaḥ sāmyaśruteḥ anuṣṭheyam - gṛhasthāśramavat āśramānna- rasyā'pi anupreyatām bādarāyaṇaḥ - bhagavān vyāsaḥ manyate, sāmyaśruteḥ - āśramāntarasyā'pi gāīnthyena sākam aikarūpyeṇa saṅgīrtanāt ||

438. vidhirvā dhāraṇavat vidhirvā – (yo dharmaskandhā : ityādiḥ) sarve- pāmapyāśramāṇāṃ vidhireva, ( na anuvādaḥ kasyā'pi ) dhāraṇavat - upari hi devebhyo dhārayati ' iti anuvādarūpepi vākye dhāraṇāṃśe vidhitvāṅgokāravat | ataḥ etābhyo yuktibhyaḥ 'vidyāta eva puruṣārtha:' iti siddham ||

vidhopākhyānapāṭhopayogina eva, na vidyāvidhyarthā:, iti cet — evaṃ śakanaṃ tu, na-nocitam, viśeṣitatvāt – viśeṣataḥ manvādīnāmākhyānānāṃ pāriplavapāṭhārthatayā uktatvāt ||
442. tathā caikavākyopabandhāt tathā ca- 'tejo vai ghṛtam ' ' so'rodīt ' ityādiṣu yathā tathaiva, yājñavalkyādyākhyāneṣvapi, ekavākyopa bandhāt- 'ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ' ityādividhinā ekavākyatayā anvayāt, sarvāṇyākhyānāni pāriplavārthāni na bhavanti, kintu, vidyāvidhyarthānyeva ||

eva na tu sarvadā taddarśanāt tasyārthasya sarvānna bhakṣaṇasya cākrāyaṇe uṣastināmake tādṛśadaśāyāmeva śrutyā bodhanāt ||

abādhācca abādhācca -- tathāsatyeva ' āhāraśuddhau satvaśuddhiḥ ' iti āhāraśuddhipratipādakaśrutya virodhasya sampādayituṃ śakyatvādapi hetoḥ ||

.447.

api smaryaṃte prāṇātyaye ca prāṇāpāyaprasaktāveva, sarvānnānumatiḥ avyavasthayā sarvānnabhakṣaṇābhyanujñānam, smaryate'pi prāṇasaṃśayamāpannaḥ yo'nnamatti yatastataḥ | lipyate na sa pāpena ' iti smṛtyā'pi spaṣṭīkriyate ||

śabdāta kāmakāre ataḥ yataḥ prāṇa pāyaprasaṅge eva sarvānnānumati: ata eva, akāmakāre – svacchandakaraṇapratiṣedhakaḥ, śabda – ' tasmādbrāhmaṇo na surāṃ pivati' ityādirūpaḥ śrautaḥ śabdo'pi dṛśyate ||

116. vihitatvādhikaraṇam
450. vihitatvāccāśramakarmāpi vihitatvācca yāvajjīvamagnihotram ' ityādiśrutyā vihitatvādapi hetoḥ, āśramakarmā'pi kevalāśramiṇaḥ prati vihitaṃ yajñādikaṃ karmā'pi, vidyādhikāriṇā'pi anuṣṭheyameva ||

451. sahakāritvena ca sahakāritvena ca - vidyotpatau yajñādikarmaṇāṃ sahakāritāmavalambyāpi, āśramakarma - kevalāśramiṇaḥ prati vihitaṃ yajñādikaṃ karma, anuṣṭheyameva || 452. sarvathā'pi ta evobhayaliṅgāt sarvathā'pi - vidyārthatayā tathā āśramārtha- tathā anuṣṭhāne'pi, te eva kṛtā: yajñādayaḥ abhinnā eva, ( na uddeśya bhedakṛta svarūpabhedavantaḥ ) ubhayaliṅgāt – ubhayatrā'pi ekenaiva agnihotrādiśabdena pratyabhijñānarūpāt jñāpakāt || 453. anabhibhavaṃ ca darśayati anabhibhavaṃ ca - vidyāyā utpattau pāpakarmabhiḥ prabalatarai: pratibandho yaḥ sambhāvitaḥ tadabhāvamapi (yajñādikarmānuṣṭhātuḥ puruṣasya ) darśayati- ' dharmeṇa pāpamapanudati' itīyaṃ śrutiḥ spaṣṭīkaroti, (ataḥ mumukṣuṇā'pi āśramiṇāṃ vihitaṃ karma anuṣṭheyameva ) ||

455. api smaryate antarācā'pi - anāśramiṇāmapi vidyāsiddhiḥ, smaryate'pi ' japyenāspi ca saṃsidhye brāhmaṇa:' ityevamādismṛtāvapi bodhyate, (ataḥ anāśramiṇāmapi brahmavidyāyāmadhikāraḥ abādhitaḥ) ||
456 viśeṣānugrahaśva viśeṣeṇa - āśramāṇāmaniyatena tapobrahmacaryādinā dharmaviśeṣeṇa, anugrahaśca – anāśramiṇāṃ vidyāyāḥ upakāro'pi (tapasā ātmānamanvicchet iti śrutau paṭhitaḥ, ataḥ antarācā'pi - anāśramiṇāmapi, 'vidyāyāḥ anumatiḥ) ||

457. atastvitarajjyāyo liṅgācca ataḥ - anāśramitvāpekṣayā, itarat - āśramitvam jyāyaḥ–dharmabhūyastvakṛtaśraiṣṭhyavat liṅgācca – ' anāśramī na tiṣṭhettu ' ityādismṛtisatvādapi ||

bahirbhūnā eva, smṛteḥ – pūrvoktāt patanasmṛteḥ, ācārācca bhraṣṭa varjanarūpāt śiṣṭānā-

manuṣṭhānācca ||

121. anāviṣkārādhikaraṇam

467. anāviṣkurvannanvayāt anāviṣkurvan – pāṇḍityaprayuktasya svamāhā - tmyasya aprakaṭīkārarūpaṃ bālasvabhāvamādadānaḥ, (vidvān varteta ) anvayāt - pāṇḍityaṃ nirvidha bālyena tiṣṭha|set ' ityevaṃ vidyānvayayogyatāyāḥ anāviṣkārātmakāyā eva viduṣi sambhavāt ||

śāstratātparyaviṣayī bhūto'rthaḥ ( ' vedanaṃ dhyānāparaparyāyam' ityarthaḥ) upadeśāt - muktisādhanopa- deśaparavākye dhyānaparyāyatayā 'vid' dhātoḥ prayogāt ||
471 liṅgācca - (mokṣasādhanībhūtasya vedanasya) asakṛt āvṛttiḥ nairantarya - paunaḥpunyābhyāṃ bahuvāramanuṣṭheyataiva śāstrārtha : ; liṅgācca – 'tadrūpapratyaye caikā santatiścā'nya- nispṛhā | taddhyānam ' ityādismṛtirūpādapi jñāpakāt ||

477. dhyānācca dhyānāt ca - ( ' nididhyāsitavyaḥ' iti vihitasya upāsanasya) vijātīyapratyayāntarā vyavahitaikāgracintanātmakatvāt ( tādṛśaṃ dhyānam ) āsīna eva kuryāt ||

478. acalatvaṃ cāpekṣya acalatvaṃ ca – dhyātari puruṣe niṣkampāvasthānameva, apekṣya - abhipretya, acetaneṣvapi parvatādiṣu ' dhyāyantīva parvatāḥ ' ityevamādayo vyapadeśā dṛśyata iti, (dhyānopayogi niścaladehāvasthitisiddhaye, dhyānam āsīna eva kuryāt ) ||
479. smaranti ca ( munayaḥ dhyānakarma āsīnasyaiva puruṣasya yuktamiti), smaranti ca – ' upaviśyāsane yuñjyādyogam ' ityādismṛtyā bodhayantyapi ||

yatraikāgratā tatrā'viśeṣāt ekāgratā - cittavṛtteḥ śubhāśrayaikāvalambitā, yatra - yādṛśe deśe yādṛśe kāle vā siddhyati, tatra - tasminneva deśe tasminneva kāle, (upāsakena dhyānaṃ kāryam ) aviśeṣāt - yogānuṣṭhānaṃ prati śrutyādiṣu ekāgratātiriktadeśakālaviśeṣāśravaṇāt || 129. āprayāṇādhikaraṇam

480.

āprayāṇāttatrāpi hi dṛṣṭam ( upāsakena kriyamāṇaṃ dhyānam ) āprayāṇāt —– dehaviyogaparyantam aharahaḥ kāryam hi yataḥ, tatrā'pi - upāsanārambha- prabhṛti dehaviyogāvadhike madhyakāle'pi (dhyānasya aharahaḥ kartavyatvam ), dṛṣṭam – ' sa khalvevaṃ vartayan yāvadāyuṣam' iti śrutyā vihitam ||

481.

130. tadadhigamādhikaraṇam

482. tadadhigama uttarapūrvāghayoraśleṣavināśau tadvyapadeśāt tadadhigame - dhyāne darśanasamānākāratayā (vidyāyāḥ) prāptau satyām uttarapūrvāghayoḥ – vidyā siddheḥ prāktanasya paścāttanasya ca pāparāśeḥ, aśleṣavināśau lopābhāva:, svarūpavilayaśca yathāyathaṃ bhavataḥ, tadvyapadeśāt – ' evaṃvidi pāpaṃ karma na lipyate ' iti ' evaṃ hāsya sarve pāpmānaḥ pradūyante ' iti ca aśleṣavināśayoḥ śrutau spaṣṭuṃ kathanāt |

131. itarādhikaraṇam

483. itarasyā'pyevamasaṃśleṣaḥ pāte tu evam - uktenaiva nyāyena, itarasyā'pi - —— puṇyasyāspi, asaṃśleṣaḥ – vidyādhigame sati lepābhāva:, vināśaśca bhavataḥ, tadvayapadeśāt-

sukṛta - duṣkṛte dhūnute ' iti, ' sarve pāpmāno to nivartante ' iti ca tayoḥ spaṣṭaṃ kathanāt,
tu paraṃtu, pāte – śarīrapāte sati, tadanantarameva āyurannādyupayoginaḥ puṇyasya nāśaḥ ||

132. anārabdhakāryādhikaraṇam

484. anārabdhakārya eva tu pūrve tadavadheḥ pūrve vidyotpatteḥ prāktane puṇyapāpe, anārabdhakārye eva tu phalapradānāpravṛtte eva, vināśaṃ prāpnutaḥ itīdameva niścitam | tadavadheḥ – ' yāvanna vimokṣye ' iti, śarīramokṣāvadhitvaśruteḥ ||

dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ

494. naikasmin darśayato hi prāṇaḥ - jīvasaṃyuktaḥ prāṇaḥ, ekasmin na sampadyate - bhūtāntarā saṃsṛṣṭe kevale tejasi na saṃśliṣṭo bhavati hi yataḥ darśayataḥ - śrutismṛtī ubhe api bhūtāntarāsaṃsṛṣṭasya ekaikasya bhūtasya svasvakāryajananāsāmarthya 'trivṛtaṃ trivṛtam' iti, tathā ), saṃhati vinā . nāśaknuvam' iti ca spaṣṭīkurutaḥ ||

500 pratiṣedhāditi cenna śārīrāt spaṣṭo hyekeṣām ( etāvadbhiḥ sūtra- rukto'rthaḥ bādhitaḥ) pratiṣedhāt – viduṣaḥ dehādutkrānterabhāvasya 'na tasya prāṇā utkrāmanti' iti śrutyā jñāpanāt, iti cet — evaṃ śaṅganam na – naiva saṅgatam, śārīrāt prāṇānāṃ jīva- sakāśādutkrāntereva, (na tu dehādutkrānteḥ) pratiṣedhāt – 'na tasya prāṇā, ityanena niṣedhāt, hi yataḥ, ekeṣām — mādhyandinaśākhināṃ 'na tasmāt prāṇā utkrāmanti' iti pāṭhe, spaṣṭaḥ ayamarthaḥ sphuṭaṃ pratipannaḥ ||

_

501. smaryate ca ( viduṣo'pi mūrdhanyanāḍyā utkrāntiḥ smaryate ca 'ūrdhvamekaḥ sthitasteṣām sa yāti' iti smṛtyā spaṣṭameva pratipādyate | ( ataḥ ) āmṛtyupakramāt - nāḍīpraveśāt pūrvam, samānā ca - ( viduṣo'viduṣaśca gatiḥ) ekarūpaiveti ||

143. raśmyanusārādhikaraṇam

505. raśmyanusārī (4 - 2 - 17) śatādhikayā - mūrdhanyayā nāḍyā (nirgataḥ vidvān ), raśmyanusārī – 'athaitaireva raśmibhirūrdhvamākramate ' iti śrutyuktayā rītyā ali rātrau vā yadākadāpi ) āditya raśmyanusāreṇaiva gamanavān (ūrdhvaṃ sthānaṃ prāpnoti ||

144. niśādhikaraṇam

niśi neti cenna sambandhasya yāvadeha bhāvitvāddarśayati ca niśi- rātrau (mṛtasya viduṣaḥ), na brahmaprāptina sambhavati, (niśāmaraṇaniṣedhāt ), iti cet — evaṃ śaṅkanaṃ tu, nana saṅgataṃ bhavati sambandhasya - phalapradāna pravṛtta karma sambandhasya yāvaddeha- ; karmasambandhasya, bhāvitvāt caramadehamātraparyantatvāt, (adhogatihetukarmaṇāṃ sarvathā vinaṣṭatvāt ; brahmaprāptiḥ siddhaiva), darśayati ca - imamarthaṃ śrutiḥ ' tasya tāvadeva ciraṃ yāvanna vimokṣye atha sampatsye ' iti ca prakaṭayati ||

147. vāyvadhikaraṇam

510. vāyumabdādaviśeṣa- viśeṣābhyām abdāt - arcirādimārge saṃvatsarāda- nantaram, vāyum - vāyumekameva niveśayet, aviśeṣa viśeṣābhyām - aviśeṣeṇa, 'devānāṃ lokaḥ' iti sāmānyaśabdena, (tathā) viśeṣeṇa, tasyaiva 'vāyuḥ' iti viśeṣaśabdena ca ekasyaiva tatra tatra nirdeśasya avagantavyatvāt ||

516. sāmīpyācu tadvayapadeśaḥ tadvayapadeśa: - ( brahmāṇaṃ gamyatayā anirdiśya, parabrahmaṇo gamyatābodhakaḥ) ' sa enān brahma gamayati' iti, napuṃsakaśabdanirdeśaḥ sāmīpyātu- hiraṇyagarbhātmake kārye brahmaṇi parabrahma prati avyavahita kāryatvanibandhanasya samīpavartitvasya saccādeva |
517. kāryātyaye tadadhyakṣeṇa sahātaḥ paramabhidhānāt ( 4 3 2) kāryasya - sṛjyasya- hiraṇyagarbhalokasya, atyaye - avasāne, tadadhyakṣeṇa saha -- brahmalokādhikārikeṇa avasitādhikāreṇa hiraṇyagarbheṇa saha, ataḥ param - kāryāt brahmalokāt utkṛṣṭamunnataṃ vā parabrahmaṇo lokam, (āpnoti), abhidhānāt -- te brahmaloke tu parāntakāle ' iti śrutau amṛtatvaprāpteḥ apunarā- vṛtteśca pratipādanāt jñāpakāt ||

518. smṛteśca ( 4 3 10) kāryabrahmopāsakaḥ hiraṇyagarbheṇa saha paraṃpadamāpnoti ityayamarthaḥ) smṛteśva' brahmaṇā saha te sarve.... praviśanti paraṃ padam ' iti smṛterapi pramāṇādavagamyate || 519. param jaiminirmukhyatvāt param paraṃ brahmopāsīnameva puruṣam ) ātivāhikaḥ - arcirādiḥ ātivāhiko gaṇa:, (nayati) mukhyatvāt - brahmaśabdasya para- brahmaṇyeva mukhyavṛttatvāt, anupacaritavṛttikatvāt iti, jaimini: jaiminināmā ācāryaḥ ( manyate ) ||

darśanācca paramparaṃ brahmopāsīnameva, (ātivāhiko gaṇa: nayati iti ) darśanācca - ' paraṃ jyotirūpasampadya.... niṣpadyate ' iti śrutirūpāt pramāṇādapi (sthirībhavati ) || 521. na ca kārye pratyabhisandhiḥ pratyabhisandhiḥ -- 'prajāpateḥ sabhāṃ veśma prapadye' ityādikamanusandhānam, na ca kārye kāryahiraṇyagarbhaviṣayakaṃ ca na bhavati ( prajāpati śabdasya 'patiṃ viśvasye' iti rītyā paramātmanyeva mukhyatvāt ) ||

520.

522. apratīkālambanānnayatīti bādarāyaṇa ubhayathā ca doṣāttatkratuśca apratīkālambanānū pratīkālambanatryatiriktān, nāmā diprāṇaśabdanirdiṣṭapratyagātmasvarūpa paryantaṃ sarva cidacidvastujātaṃ brahmadṛṣṭyā svarūpeṇa vā ye upāsate tadbhinnān (pañcāgnividaḥ parabrahmo- pāsakāṃśca) nayatīti - ātivāhiko gaṇaḥ paraṃ padaṃ prāpayati, iti ; ( imamartham ) bādarāyaṇaḥ - bādarāyanāmā ācāryoṃ manyate ; ubhayathā ca ' kāryamupāsīnāneva nayati' iti pakṣe, 'paraṃ jyotirupāsīnāneva nayati' iti pakṣe ca doṣāt sakalopaniṣadvirodha rūpadoṣaprasaṅgāt, tatkratuśca - tatkratunyāyo'pi 'yathākratura smilloke' iti śrutyuktaḥ viruddho bhavet ||

viśeṣaṃ ca darśayati viśeṣaṃ ca - pratīkālambanānāṃ gatyanapekṣaṃ parimita- phala viśeṣamapi, darśayati – 'yāvannāmno gatam ' ityādikā śrutiḥ spaṣṭaṃ pratipādayati ||

|| iti caturthādhyāye tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ ||

nivṛttatirodhāno jīvaḥ, brāhmaṇa - brahmasambandhitayā śrutipratipannena apahatapāpmatvādinā dharmeṇa ( āvirbhavati), upanyāsādibhyaḥ - brahmaguṇānāmeva teṣāṃ jīvaguṇatayā śrutau upanyāsāt (tathā) satyasaṅkalpatvaprayojyānāṃ jakṣaṇādikriyāviśeṣāṇāṃ kathanādeśca iti, jaiminiḥ—jaiminināmā ācāryo manyate ||

529. cititanmātreṇa tadātmakatvādityauḍalomiḥ cititanmātreṇa— caitanyamātrātmakena svarūpeṇa ( jīva: āvirbhavati), tadātmakatvāt - asya pratyagātmanaḥ 'vijñāna - ghanaeve' tyuktacaitanyaikasvarūpatvāt iti - evamāśayam auḍulobhiḥ – auḍulomināmā ācāryaḥ manyate ||

530. evamapyupanyāsāppūrvabhāvāda virodhaṃ bādarāyaṇaḥ evamapi - vijñānaghanatve satyapi, upanyāsāt –' ya ātmā apahatapāpmā ' ityādyau paniṣadopanyāsarūpāt pramāṇāt, pūrva bhāvāt - pūrveṣāmapahatapāpmatvādiguṇānāmapi jīvātmani sadbhāvāt, avirodhaṃ – vijñāna- ghanatvāpahatapāpmatvādyoḥ mitho vaiyadhikaraṇyānāpādakatvam bādarāyaṇaḥ - bhagavān bādarāyaṇa- nāmā ācāryaḥ manyate ||

535. dvādaśāhavadubhayavidhaṃ bādarāyaṇo'taḥ dvādaśāhavat - dvādaśāhanāmnaḥ kratoḥ, 'dvādaśāhamṛddhikāmā upeyuḥ ' iti 'upaiti' codanayā saṅkalpavailakṣaṇyāt satrātmakatvam, 'dvādaśāhena prajākāmaṃ yājayet' iti yajati ' codanayā saṅkalpavailakṣaṇyāt ahīnātmakatvaṃ cetyubhayavidhatvabhiva, ubhayavidham - muktātmano'pi saśarīratvāśarīratvobhayaprakāravattvam, ataḥ - muktagatāt saṅkalpādeva hetoḥ sidhyatīti, bādarāyaṇaḥ - bādarāyaṇanāmā ācāryaḥ abhipraiti ||

536. tanvabhāve sandhyavadupapatteḥ tanvabhāve – svasaṅkalpena sṛṣṭasya śarīrendriyā- chupakaraṇasya rāhitye, sandhyavat - svapne iva, upapatteḥ paramapuruṣasṛṣṭaireva tattanmātrānubhāvyaiḥ dehādyupakaraṇaiḥ bhogasiddheḥ, (satyasaṅkalpo'pi jīvaḥ svabhogopakaraṇaṃ na sṛjedapi ) ||

5.38. pradīpavadā veśastathā hi darśayati pradīpavat – ekadeśavartino dīpasya svaprabhayā anekadeśapraveśa iva, āveśa : – ekadeśasthasyātmana: svaprabhārūpeṇa jñānena sarvadeheṣu yathāsaṅkalpaṃ vyāptiḥ sambhavati iti | tathā - tameva prakāram darśayati hi - ' vālāgra .... ānantyāya kalpate' iti śrutiḥ spaṣṭayati khalu ||

156. jagadvyāpāravajadhikaraṇam
540. jagadvayāpāravarja prakaraṇādasannihitatvācca (āvirbhūtasvarūpasya muktasyaiśvaryam ) jagadvayāpāravarjam – nikhilacetanā cetanasvarūpasthitipravṛttibhedaniyamanaṃ varjayitvā itaradeva, nirvyāja brahmānubhavamātrarūpam ; prakaraṇāt - sarvatra jagadvyāpārādeḥ paraṃ brahma prakramyaiva śrutyādiṣu kathanāt, asaṃnihitatvācca – jagadvyāpāraprakaraṇe kutrā'pi śrutau muktaprastāvasyaivā'- bhāvādapi jñāpakāt ||
541 pratyakṣopadeśāditi cennādhikārikamaṇḍalasthokteḥ pratyakṣeṇa - 'imā~llokān kāmānnī' ityādirūpayā śrutyā, upadeśāt - muktasyā''tmanaḥ jagadvyāpārasya spaṣṭa

jñāpanāt, ('jagadvyāpāvarjam' ityetat na yujyate ), iti cet — evaṃ śaṃkanaṃ tu, na—na samyak, ādhikārikamaṇḍalasthokteḥ - bhagavatā lokaniyamanādhikāre niyuktānāṃ hiraṇyagarbhā dīnāṃ lokeṣu vidyamānāḥ ye bhogāḥ, tadanubhavasyaiva tacchrutyuktatvāt, na jagadvyāpārasaṃbhavaḥ | 542. vikārarāvarti ca tathā hi sthitimāha vikārāvarti ca – nirdhūtanikhila- vikāraṃ paraṃbrahma ca savibhūtikam (muktaścetanaḥ anubhavati), tathā hi- anubhavitṛtvena hi, sthitim - nirvikāre niratiśayānande parebrahmaṇi muktātmanaḥ avasthānam, āha— 'abhayaṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ vindate', ' labdhvā''nandī bhavati' ityādiśrutiḥ spaṣṭīkaroti ||
543. darśayatacaivaṃ pratyakṣānumāne pratyakṣānumāne—śratismṛtī ubhe api, evaṃ ca - 'jagadvyāpāraḥ parapuruṣāsādhāraṇa:' ityevameva darśayataḥ - spaṣṭīkurutaḥ |

11 ЯT: 11

namo hiraṇyagarbhāya haraye śaṅkarāya ca |
vāsudevāya tārāya sṛṣṭisthityantakāriṇe || ekānekasvarūpāya sthūlasūkṣmātmane namaḥ |
avyakta vyaktarūpāya viṣṇave mukti hetave || sarga-sthiti- vināśānāṃ jagato yo jaganmayaḥ |
mūlabhūto namastasmai viṣṇave paramātmane ||
ādhārabhūtaṃ viśvasyāpyaṇīyāṃsamaṇīyasām |

praṇamya sarvabhūtasthamacyutaṃ puruṣottamam ||

jñānasvarūpa matyanta nirmalaṃ paramārthataḥ |

tamevārthasvarūpeṇa bhrāntidarśanataḥ sthitam ||

viṣṇu siṃṇu viśvasya sthitau sarge tathā prabhum ' |

praṇamya jagatāmīśamaja makṣaya mavyayam ||

kathayāmi yathā pūrvaṃ dakṣādyairmunisattamaiḥ |

parasya brahmaṇo rūpaṃ puruṣaḥ prathamaṃ dvija |
vyaktāvyakte tathaivānye rūpe kālastathā'param ||
pradhānapuruṣavyaktakālānāṃ paramaṃ hi yat |
paśyanti sūrayaśśuddhaṃ tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam |
pradhānapuruṣavyaktakālāstu pravibhāgaśaḥ |
rūpāṇi sthitisargāntavyakti sadbhāvahetavaḥ || vyaktaṃ viṣṇustathā'vyaktaṃ puruṣaḥ kāla eva ca |
krīḍato bālakasyeva ceṣṭāṃ tasya niśāmaya |

' ityeva | sanniveśo yaḥ pṛthivyā jyotiṣāṃ tathā |
dvīpānāmudadhīnāṃ ca parvatānāṃ ca kīrtitaḥ ||
varṣāṇāṃ ca nadīnāṃ ca ye ca teṣu vasanti vai |
teṣāṃ svarūpamākhyātaṃ saṃkṣepaḥ śrūyatāṃ punaḥ || yadambu vaiṣṇavaḥ kāyastato vipra vasundharā |
padmakārā samudbhū parvatābdhyādi saṃyutā ||
jyotīṃṣi viṣṇurbhuvanāni viṣṇurvanāni viṣṇurgirayo diśaśca |
nadyaḥ samudrāśca sa eva sarvaṃ yadasti yannāsti ca vipravarya || jñānasvarūpa bhagavānyato'sāvaśeṣamūrtirna tu vastubhūtaḥ |
tato hi śailādimedā jānīhi vijñānavijṛmbhitāni || yadā śuddhaṃ nijarūpa 'sarva karmakṣaye" jñānamapāstadoṣam |
tadā hi saṅkalpataroḥ phalāni bhavanti' no vastuṣu vastubhedāḥ || vastvasti kiṃ kutracidrādimadhyaparyanta' hīnaṃ satataika' rūpam |
yaccānyathātvaṃ dvija yāti bhūyo na tattathā ' tatra' kuto hi tattvam ||
mahī ghaṭatvaṃ ghaṭataḥ kapālikā kapālikā cūrṇarajastato'ṇuḥ |
janaiḥ svakarmastimitātmaniścayairālakṣyate brūhi kimatra vastu ||

" tasmānna" vijñānamṛte'sti kiñcitkacitkadāciddija vastujātam |

vijñānamekaṃ nijakarmabheda vibhinna cittaibahu" dhā'bhyupetam " ||
jñānaṃ " viśuddhaṃ vimalaṃ ca nityamaśeṣalobhādi " nirastasaṅgam |

" ekassadaikaṃ" paramaḥ pareśaḥ saḥ vāsudevo na yato'nyadasti || ( 2 - 12- 44)

13.

sadbhāva evaṃ bhakto mayokto jñānaṃ yathā satyama satyamanyat | etatu yatsaṃ tryahārabhūtaṃ ' tatrāpi coktaṃ bhuvanāśritaṃ te || yajña: paśurvanhiraśeṣaṛtvik somaḥ surāḥ svargamayaśca kāmaḥ | ityādikarmāśritamārgadṛṣṭaṃ bhūgadi bhogāśca phalāni teṣām || ( 2 - 12 46 ) yaccaitadbhuvanagataṃ mayā tavoktaṃ sarvatra vrajati hi tatra karmavaśyaḥ |
" jñātvaivaṃ dhruvamacalaṃ sadaikarūpaṃ tatkuryādviśati hi 'yena' vāsudevam ||

kiṃ hetubhirvadatyeṣā vāgevāhamiti 'svayam' | 'ataḥ pītrānasītyetadvaktumitthaṃ na yujyate ||
piṇḍa : pṛthagyataḥ puṃsaśśira:- pāṇyādilakṣaṇaḥ |
tato'hamiti kutraitāṃ saṃjñāṃ rājan karomyaham ||
yadyanyo'sti paraḥ ko'pi mattaḥ pārthivasattama |
tadaiṣo'hamayaṃ cā'nyo vaktumevamapīṣyate ||
yadā samasta deheṣu pumāneko vyavasthitaḥ |
tadāhi ko bhavān 'so'shamityetadviphalaṃ vacaḥ ||
tvaṃ rājā śivikā ceyaṃ vayaṃ vāhāḥ purassarāḥ |
ayaṃ ca bhavato loko na sadetannṛpocyate ||
pumāṃstrī ' gaurajo' vājī kuñjaro vihagastaruḥ |
deheṣu lokasaṃjñeyaṃ vijñeyā karmahetuṣu || pumānna devo na naro na paśurna ca pādapaḥ
śarīrākṛtibhedāstu ‘bhūpaite ṃ karmayonayaḥ ||
vastu rājeti yaloke yacca rājabhaṭā 'dikam ' |
tathā'nyacca nṛpetthaṃ tanna ”satsaṅkalpanā 'mayam || yattu kālāntareṇāpi 'nānyasaṃjñāmupaiti vai |
pariṇāmādisambhūtāṃ tadvastu nṛpa tacca kim ||
tvaṃ rājā 'sarvalokasya pituḥ putro ripo ripuḥ |
patnyāḥ patiḥ pitā sūnoḥ "kaṃ tvaṃ bhūpa vadāmyaham || tvaṃ kimetacchiraḥ " kiṃnu grīvā tava tathodaram" |
kimu pādādikaṃ tvaṃ vai" tavaitaki mahīpate ||

samastāvayavebhyastvaṃ pṛthagbhūpa' vyavasthitaḥ |
"ko' hamityatra nipuṇo bhūtvā cintaya pārthiva |
evaṃ vyavasthite tattve mayā'hamiti bhāṣitum |

pṛthakkaraṇa niṣpādyaṃ śakyate nṛpate katham ||

||

saṃjñāyate yena tadastadoṣaṃ śuddhaṃ paraṃ nirmalamekarūpam |
sandṛśyate vā'pyava (vi) gamyate vā tajjñānamajñānamato'nyaduktam ||

āśrayaścetaso brahmadvidhā tacca svabhāvataḥ |
bhūpa mūrtamamūrta ca paraṃ cāparameva ca ||

trividhā bhāvanā bhūpa viśvametannibodhatām' |
brahmākhyā karmasaṃjñā ca tathācaivomayātmikā |
karmabhāvātmikā hyekā brahmabhāvātmikā parā |
ubhayātmikā tathaivānyā trividhā bhāvabhāvanā ||
sanandanādayo ye tu brahmabhāvanayā yutāḥ |

karmabhāvanayā cānye devādyāssthāvarā varā : " ||
hiraṇyagarbhādiṣu ca brahmakarmātmikā dvidhā |
bodhādhikārayukteṣu vidyate bhāvabhāvanā ||

akṣīṇeṣu samasteṣu viśeṣajñānakarmasu |

viśvametatparaṃ cānyadbhedabhinnadṛśāṃ nṛṇām ||

pratyastamitabhedaṃ yatsattāmātramagocaram |

" vacasā 'mātmasaṃvedyaṃ tajjñānaṃ brahmasaṃjñitam ||

- tayā tirohitatvācca śaktiḥ kṣetrajñasaṃjñitā | sarvabhūteṣu bhūpāla tāratamyena 'lakṣya' ||

aprāṇavatsu svalpā sā sthāvareṣu tato'dhikā |

sarīsṛpeṣu tebhyo'pi hyatiśaktyā patatraṣu ||

patatribhyo mṛgāstebhyastacchaktyā paśavo'dhikāḥ |
paśubhyo manujāścāti śaktyā puṃsaḥ prabhāvitāḥ ||
tebhyo'pi nāgagavarvayakṣādyā devatā nṛpa ||
śakrassamasta devebhyastataścāti prajāpatiḥ |

hiraṇyagarbho'pi tataḥ puṃsaśaktyupalakṣitaḥ ||
etānyaśeṣarūpāṇi tasya rūpāṇi pārthiva |
yatastacchaktiyogena yuktāni nabhasā yathā ||
dvitīyaṃ viṇusaṃjñasya yogidhyeya 'mahāmate |

amūrta brahmaṇo rūpaṃ yatsadityucyate budhaiḥ || samastāraśaktayaścaitā nṛpa yatra pratiṣṭhitāḥ |

* yadviśvarūpavairūpyaṃ rūpamanyaddharermahat ||

samastaśaktirūpāṇi tatkaroti janeśvara |

devatiryaṅ 'manuṣyādi ceṣṭāvanti svalīlayā ||

jagatāmupakārāya na sā karmanimittanā |

ceṣṭā tasyāprameyasya vyāpinyavyāhatātmikā ||
tadrūpaṃ viśvarūpasya tasya yogayujā nṛpa |
cintyamātmaviśuddhyarthaṃ sarvakilviṣanāśanam ||
yathā'niruddhataśikhaḥ kakṣaṃ dahati sānilaḥ |
tathā cittasthito viṣṇuḥ yogināṃ sarvakilbiṣam || tasmātsamastaśaktīnāmādhāre tatra cetasaḥ |
kurvīta saṃsthitiṃ sā tu vijñeyā śuddhadhāraṇā ||

bhaktaprapannabhedena sa tu mokṣaparo dvidhā | dvidhā prapanna ekāntipara maikāntibhedataḥ || 9 || dṛpta ārta iti dvedhā para maikāntyudāhṛtaḥ | īśvaraḥ pañcadhā bhinnaḥ paravyūhādibhedataḥ || 10 || para eka caturdhā tu vyūhaḥ syādvāsudevakaḥ | saṅkarṣaṇaśca pradyumno'niruddha iti bhedataḥ || 11 ||
matsyādayastu vibhavā anantāśca prakīrtitāḥ | antaryāmī tu bhagavān pratidehamavasthitaḥ || 12 |
avatāra : śrīraṅgaveṅkaṭa drayādiṣu sthitaḥ | keśavādi tu tattvajñairvyūhāntaramudāhṛtam || 13 |
satvaṃ rajastamaḥ śabdasparśarūparasāntathā | gandhasaṃyogaśaktī cetyadravyaṃ daśadhā matam || 14 ||

etadindriyasāpekṣamanapekṣaṃ ca dṛśyate |
anapekṣaṃ svatassiddhaṃ divyaṃ veti dvidhā matam || 20 || yogajaṃ tu svatassiddhamanyatsvāmiprasādajam | arvācīnamidaṃ sarvamāmananti vicakṣaṇāḥ || 21 || nityamukteśvarajñānamanarvā cīnamucyate |
smṛti: pramaiva prācīnānubhavājjāyate hi sā || 22 || sadṛśādṛṣṭacintādyaiḥ saṃskārodbodhane sati | smṛtivatpratyabhijñā'pi pratyakṣe'ntarbhavatyasau || 23 || puṇyapūruṣaniṣṭhā'pi pratibhā'traiva saṃmatā |
'yathārtha sarvavijñānamiti yāmunabhāṣitam || 24 || 'bhūtale tu ghaṭābhāvo bhūtalātmaiva netaraḥ |
mṛd ghaṭasya prāgabhāvo dhvaṃsastasya kapālakam || 25 || svāsādhāraṇa dharmo hi bhedaśabdena kīrtyate |
śabdasvābhāvyajā kvā'pi pratiyogitvadhīḥ kṛtā || 26 || antaḥkaraṇacaitanyaṃ tadvṛttyā viṣayeṇa ca |
3 caitanyaṃ samata | sspannaṃ sākṣātkāramajījanat || 27 ||
ityādi vacanaṃ sarva paroktaṃ nā'tra sammatam |
sāmānyaṃ samavāyaśca viśeṣo nā'tra sammataḥ || 28 ||
saṅkhyā diguṇavargasya guṇapārthakya kalpanam |
sūtrakāraviruddhaṃ yattatsarvaṃ parihāsyate || 29 ||
|| iti pratyakṣanirūpaṇam ||

sādhyābhāvādhikaraṇāvṛttitvaṃ vyāptiracyate | vyāpyasya pakṣavṛttitvadhīḥ parāmarśanāmabhāk || 2 ||
tajjā pakṣe sādhyamatiranumityātmikā matā | bhūyiṣṭhasāhacaryaikajñānena vyāptidhīrbhavet || 3 || tatpakṣe sapakṣastu pūrvaṃ niścitasādhyakaḥ | sandigdhasādhyakaḥ pakṣo, vipakṣastadabhāvavān || 4 || anvayī vyatirekī ca kiñca heturdvilakṣaṇa:' | vyabhicārī viruddhaścāsiddhaḥ satpratipakṣakaḥ || 5 || bādhitaśceti pañcaite hetvābhāsā na sādhakāḥ | evaṃ svārthānumānasya prapañcastu nirūpitaḥ || 6 || nyāyajanyaḥ parāmarśaḥ parārthānumiteḥ kṛte |
nyāyosvayava vākyāni pratijñādīni pañca tu || 7 ||
pratijñā sādhyanirdeśo hetustadvacanaṃ matam |
vyāyuktipūrvadṛṣṭāntavā gudāharaṇaṃ bhaveta || 8 ||

vyāpyasya pakṣavṛttitva bodhaścopanayo mataḥ | upasaṃhāravacanaṃ bhavennigamanaṃ " punaḥ ' || 9 ||
|| iti anumānanirūpaṇam ||

karmabrahmābhidhāyitvātsa ca bhāgadvayātmakaḥ | pūrvabhāga: karmapara uttaro brahmagocaraḥ || 3 || tadaikyātpūrvaparayorvyākhyayore kī śāstratā | adhyāyabhedava'dbhede' śāstraikyaṃ na viruddhayate || 4 || vidhyarthavādamantrātmā trividhaḥ sa pratīyate | anuṣṭheyārthagamako mantraḥ syādarthavāda 'gī : ' || 5 || pravṛttyutta 'mbhikā yā' syādvidhirvākyaṃ pravartakam | satridhāspūrva niyama parisaṅkhyā' vibhedataḥ ' || 6 || 'nityā - naimittikāḥ kāmyā iti te bahudhā matāḥ | "teṣāṃ svarūpalakṣmāṇi mantavyāni' nayāntare || 7 | chandaḥ kalpaśca śikṣā ca niruktaṃ jyautiṣaṃ tathā | vyākṛtiśceti vedasya ṣaḍaṅgāni pracakṣate || 8 ||

anuṣṭubādikaṃ chandaḥ kalpaḥ śrautādi" bodhakaḥ ' varṇanirṇāyakā śikṣā, niruktaṃ svārthabodhakam || 9||
anuṣṭhānādikālasya nirṇaye jyautiṣaṃ bhavet |
sauśabdyāya vyākaraṇamiti sāṅge pramāṇatā || 10 ||

etanmūlatayā smṛtyādīnāṃ prāmāṇyamīritam |

etadviruddhaṃ yatkiñcinnāśnuvīta pramāṇatām ||| 11 ||
ākāṅkṣādikametacca śābdabodhaikakāraṇam |
tadvicārotra saṃkṣipto granthavistara bhīruṇā || 12 ||
mukhyapacārikatvābhyāṃ sa śabdo dvividho mataḥ |
abhitrā mukhyavṛttiḥ syād vṛttiranyaupacārikī || 13 ||

śarīravācakāḥ śabdāḥ śarīrikṛtavṛttayaḥ | sarvaśabdakavācyatvaṃ hareriti ' gadiṣyate ' || 14 ||
|| iti śabdapramāṇanirūpaṇam ||

rūpamātragrāhi cakṣustvak sparśagraha kāraṇam |
gandhakagrāhakaṃ ghrāṇaṃ rasanaṃ rasabhāsakam || 10 ||
eṣāṃ viṣayamambandhaḥ saṃyogādiḥ prakīrtitaḥ |

uccāraṇādikamaikaśaktaṃ karmendriyaṃ matam || 11 ||
pañcadhā vākpāṇipādapāyūpasthaprabhedataḥ |
varṇoccāraṇaheturvāk pāṇiśśilpādikāraṇam || 12 ||
sañcārakāraṇaṃ pādaḥ pāyurmala nivṛttikṛt |
upasthaḥ paramānandahetuḥ strīpuṃsayormataḥ || 13 || rājasāhaṃ kriyā yuktatā masāhaṃkṛteḥ punaḥ | jāyate śabdatanmātrādikaṃ bhūtādikāraṇam || 14 || bhūtānāmeva 'sūkṣmaika pūrvāvasthā viśeṣavat' |
dravyaṃ tanmātramityāhuḥ pañcadhābhūtapañcabhiḥ || 15 ||
tanmātra pañcakaṃ śabdādya śrayatvena saṃgatam |
bhūtānāṃ syādupādānaṃ śabdatanmātramādimam || 16 || sparśatanmātrakaṃ rūparasatanmātra ke api |
gandhatanmātrametatsyāt khānilajyotirabbhuvaḥ || 17 ||
pañca bhūtāni tanmātrasvarūpantu nirūpyate | tāmasāhaṃkṛtidvayormadhyāvasthā yugādimam || 18 ||
śabdatanmātramasmācca viyadutpadyate tathā |
khameva sūryarūpandena digiti vyapadiśyate || 19 | dravyaṃ tadākāśavāyvormadhyāvasthā susaṃyutam | sparśa tanmātrasmācca vāyurutpadyate kramāt || 20 || madhyāvasthāyutaṃ vāyute jasordravyamucyate |
rūpannātramityasmāttena utpadyate kramāt || 21 ||

madhyāvasthāyutaṃ tejaḥ payasordravyamucyate | rasatanmātramityasmātsalilaṃ khalu jāyate || 22 || madhyāvasthāyutaṃ vāripṛthivyordravyamucyate | gandhatanmātramityasmātpṛthivī samudetyasau || 23 || ādyaṃ śabdavadanyacca śabdasparśa ducyate | rūpaśabdasparśavatsyāttṛtīyaṃ ca turīyakam || 24 || rūpaśabdasparśa 'rasayuktaṃ gandhādhikaṃ param | tanmātrapañcakaṃ bhūtapañcakaṃ caivamīritam || 25 ||
